### [1] [Arithmetic growth in partially commutative semigroups of endomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02348)
*Wade Hindes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了品种自同态的部分交换半群的次数与高度增长率函数的渐近性质，证明了连接乘性与非负函数的增长率一般结果，并构造了若干新的自同态族，为此将标准的单个元素ping-pong引理推广到子半群的ping-pong引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究部分交换半群（生成元之间仅存在交换关系的半群）的增长率渐近性质具有一定的理论意义，但现有的增长率分析工具尚不充分，且缺少处理这类半群中自同态族的统一方法，因此需要建立更一般的增长率联系定理并扩展相关引理。

Method: 1）证明一个连接两个抽象函数增长率的一般性定理：其中一个是乘性函数、另一个仅非负，并附加Tate telescoping假设与底层半群上足够良好的正规形式；2）将标准的单个元素ping-pong引理推广为子半群的ping-pong引理；3）利用上述工具构造若干新的自同态族。

Result: 建立了品种自同态的部分交换半群的次数与高度增长率函数的渐近性质；证明了连接乘性与非负抽象函数增长率的一般定理；构造了多类新的自同态族，且所建立的渐近性结果适用于这些族。

Conclusion: 通过一般性增长率定理与子半群ping-pong引理的推广，成功刻画了部分交换自同态半群的增长率渐近行为，并扩展了适用的自同态族范围，为后续相关研究提供了新的工具与构造方法。

Abstract: We establish asymptotics for the degree and height growth rate functions of partially commutative semigroups of endomorphisms of varieties, i.e., semigroups where the only relations between generators, if any, are commuting ones. To do this, we prove a general result connecting the growth rates of two abstract functions, one multiplicative and one simply nonnegative, subject to a Tate telescoping hypothesis and a sufficiently nice normal form on the underlying semigroup. Finally, we construct several new families of endomorphisms on which our asymptotics apply. To do this, we generalize the standard ping-pong lemma for elements to a ping-pong lemma for subsemigroups.

</details>


### [2] [The proportion of polynomials over F_2 with positive orthogonal multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02352)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究 F_2[T] 中单项多项式正交重数的非零比例 p(d)，证明两个关于微分留数 psi_P(f) 的无条件事实（互反关系与局部精确分布），在局部条件独立汇合的假设下得到 p(d) ~ L d^{-1/2}（L=1.2116120743...），并验证 d<=33 的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackburn (1998) 提出问题：F_2[T] 中次数为 d 的单项多项式 f 满足正交重数 m(f)>0 的比例 p(d) 在 d→∞ 时的渐近行为如何，该问题此前未获解决。

Method: 对 f 的每个素因子 P 关联 F_2 中的留数 psi_P(f)=dT/(T(T+1)f)；证明两条无条件事实：(1) 当 f 与 T(T+1) 互素时各 psi_P(f) 之和为零（互反关系）；(2) 局部精确分布——P 恰好整除 f 时 psi_P 消失的比例为 (|P|-2)/(2(|P|-1))，P^e (e>=2) 恰好整除时为 1/2。在此基础上假设这些局部条件可独立汇合。

Result: 在局部独立汇合假设下得到 p(d) ~ L d^{-1/2}，其中 L=(9/(4 sqrt(pi)))∏_{deg P>=2}(1+1/(2|P|))(1-1/|P|)^{1/2}=1.2116120743...；同一欧拉乘积在第二个参数处取值给出 m(f) 按次数求和恰为 2^d 的恒等式，从而固定归一化；给出 1<=d<=33 的 2^d p(d) 精确值，预测值与精确值在 27<=d<=33 范围内误差在 5*10^{-5} 以内。

Conclusion: 该工作用留数互反关系与精确局部分布刻画了正交重数非零的条件，并在独立汇合假设下给出 p(d) 的 d^{-1/2} 渐近律及显式常数；数值验证支持该渐近预测，但 p(d)~L d^{-1/2} 仍依赖于局部独立性假设（非完全无条件）。

Abstract: Let A = F_2[T]. The orthogonal multiplicity m(f) of a monic f in A of degree d >= 1 is the number of g in A with deg g < d, gcd(g,f) = 1, and all partial quotients of g/f of degree one. Blackburn (1998) asked how the proportion p(d) of monic polynomials of degree d with m(f) > 0 behaves as d tends to infinity.
  We attach to each prime divisor P of f a residue psi_P(f) in F_2 of the differential dT/(T(T+1)f), and prove two unconditional facts about this family: a reciprocity relation, namely that the psi_P(f) sum to zero whenever f is prime to T(T+1), and an exact local distribution, namely that psi_P vanishes for a proportion exactly (|P|-2)/(2(|P|-1)) of the admissible completions when P exactly divides f, and for exactly half of them when P^e exactly divides f with e >= 2.
  Granting that these local conditions pool independently, we obtain p(d) ~ L d^(-1/2) with L = (9/(4 sqrt(pi))) times the product over the monic irreducibles P of A of degree at least two of (1 + 1/(2|P|)) (1 - 1/|P|)^(1/2), equal to 1.2116120743... The same Euler product evaluated at a second parameter returns the exact identity that the m(f) of a given degree sum to 2^d, which fixes its normalisation. We give the exact value of 2^d p(d) for 1 <= d <= 33; the predicted values agree with these to within 5 10^(-5) for 27 <= d <= 33.

</details>


### [3] [Shimura Reciprocity for Real Quadratic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02419)
*Joseph DiCapua*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在假设实二次域上阶１阿贝尔Stark猜想成立的条件下，给出了Shintani–Faddeev模余圈实乘法值上Galois作用的条件性显式公式，并提出了一个关于该Galois作用的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Shintani–Faddeev模余圈在实乘法点处的取值所受的Galois作用，目前缺乏显式描述；作者希望借助Stark猜想给出条件性的显式公式，并为进一步验证提供可检验的猜想。

Method: 在假定实二次域上阶１阿贝尔Stark猜想的前提下，对Shintani–Faddeev模余圈的实乘法值推导Galois作用的条件性显式公式，并据此提出一个新的猜想。

Result: 得到了Galois作用在实乘法值上的条件性显式公式，并给出了一个新的关于Galois作用的猜想；该猜想的数值证据将在后续工作中给出。

Conclusion: 该工作将Shintani–Faddeev模余圈的实乘法值与Stark猜想联系起来，给出条件性显式结果并提出可数值检验的新猜想，后续工作将提供数值证据。

Abstract: The purpose of this note is to give conditional explicit formulas for the Galois action on real multiplication values of the Shintani--Faddeev modular cocycle. We assume the order 1 abelian Stark conjecture for real quadratic fields. We conclude this note by presenting our own conjecture about the Galois action. Numerical evidence for this conjecture will be provided in forthcoming work.

</details>


### [4] [Counterexamples for Rational Points Near Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02443)
*Mingfeng Chen,Rajula Srivastava,Niclas Technau*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究有理点在矩曲线附近分布计数 $N_{\mathcal{C}}(\delta,Q)$ 的渐近范型 $N_{\mathcal{C}}(\delta,Q)\asymp\delta^{n-1}Q^2$ 成立所需的 $\delta$ 范围，证明已知上界在 $n\to\infty$ 时几乎是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 $N_{\mathcal{C}}(\delta,Q)\asymp\delta^{n-1}Q^2$ 这一 heuristic 在 $\delta$ 关于 $Q$ 的何种范围内成立，并澄清此前普遍认为可改进 $O(n^{-1})$ 的预期是否正确，评估 Hickman 与第二作者已有上界结果的尖锐程度。

Method: 针对矩曲线情形，分析已有上界所适用的 $\delta\in[Q^{\alpha(n)+\nu},1/2)$ 范围（其中 $\alpha(n)=-4n^{-2}+O(n^{-3})$ 为显式计算值），通过构造性/下界论证说明该范围在 $n\to\infty$ 渐近意义下几乎尖锐，至多可延拓至 $\delta\in[Q^{\alpha(n)},1/2)$；并结合 Gan–Guo–Oh 最近建立的互补上界完成该剩余范围的刻画。

Result: 证明该范围在 $n\to\infty$ 渐近意义下出人意料地接近尖锐，至多可延拓到 $\delta\in[Q^{\alpha(n)},1/2)$；尖锐指数至多只能比 $\alpha(n)$ 改善 $O(n^{-3})$，而非此前广泛认为可能的 $O(n^{-1})$ 改进。剩余范围内的互补上界已由 Gan–Guo–Oh 建立。

Conclusion: 矩曲线情形下范型 $N_{\mathcal{C}}(\delta,Q)\asymp\delta^{n-1}Q^2$ 的有效 $\delta$ 范围几乎就是 $[Q^{\alpha(n)},1/2)$，其精度已达渐近最优，纠正了此前关于可获 $O(n^{-1})$ 改进的预期。

Abstract: Let $δ\in [0,1/2]$ and $Q\geq 1$. Given a compact $C^{\infty}$-curve $\mathcal{C}$ in $\mathbb{R}^n$, denote by $N_{\mathcal{C}}(δ, Q)$ the number of pairs $(\mathbf{a}, q)\in \mathbb{Z}^n\times [Q/2,Q]$ such that the rational point $\mathbf{a}/q$ is $δ/q$-close to $\mathcal{C}$. We investigate the range of $δ$ in terms of $Q$ which is necessary for the heuristic $N_{\mathcal{C}}(δ, Q)\asympδ^{n-1}Q^2$ to be correct for the moment curve. For a nondegenerate curve $\mathcal{C}\subset \mathbb{R}^n$ and for sufficiently large $Q$, Hickman and the second author previously established that $N_{\mathcal{C}}(δ, Q)\lesssim_ν δ^{n-1}Q^2$ for all $δ\in[Q^{α(n+1)+ν},1/2)$ and any $ν>0$. Here $α(n)=-4n^{-2}+O(n^{-3})$ was explicitly computed. We show that this range is surprisingly close to being sharp in the asymptotic sense as $n\to\infty$, and can at most be extended to $δ\in[Q^{α(n)},1/2)$. In particular, the sharp exponent can only be about $O(n^{-3})$ better, even though it was widely believed before that an improvement of $O(n^{-1})$ should be possible. The complementary upper bound in this remaining range has been recently established by Gan--Guo--Oh.

</details>


### [5] [The Prime Wavelet Tree: Compressing the Carmichael Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02550)
*Ankur Gupta,Jonathan Webster,Sylvia Webster*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出两种数论压缩技术（局部与全局），针对满足Korselt类准则的数集，利用素数小波树与启发式除数选择，将10^24以下全部308,279,939个Carmichael数的文本文件从18.4 GB压缩至588.7 MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carmichael数的大规模表格（10^24以下共308,279,939个，文本形式占18.4 GB）存储与传输成本高，而通用的标准压缩方法未能充分利用其数论结构，故需要针对该数集设计专门的压缩技术。

Method: 提出两种压缩技术：一是局部的，逐个压缩单个数字；二是全局的，借助"素数小波树"（一种具有独立价值的数据结构）压缩整个数集。两技术适用于任何满足Korselt类准则的数集，并与标准压缩技术及启发式除数选择相结合。

Result: 对10^24以下全部308,279,939个Carmichael数（18.4 GB文本），组合使用标准压缩、启发式除数选择与本文技术后，文件压缩至588.7 MB，较文本文件小31.2倍；原本每个80比特的数现平均仅约15.3比特。

Conclusion: 数论结构（Korselt类准则、素数小波树、启发式除数）可显著提升特定数集的压缩效果，说明专用数论压缩技术在大规模数论数据存储中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We present two number-theoretic compression techniques. The first is \textit{local}, and compresses individual numbers. The second is \textit{global}, and compresses the entire set of numbers using a \textit{prime wavelet tree}, which is a data structure of independent interest. Our techniques apply to any set of numbers satisfying a Korselt-like criterion. We demonstrate our compression techniques using the recently completed tabulation of all $308{,}279{,}939$ Carmichael numbers less than $10^{24}$ that occupies $18.4$ gigabytes as text. When combined with standard compression techniques and a heuristic choice of divisors, the resulting file is $588.7$ megabytes ($31.2$ times smaller than the text file). The initial $80$-bit numbers now use about $15.3$ bits.

</details>


### [6] [Extreme values of Dirichlet $L$-functions on the critical line with a growing prime modulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02709)
*Zikang Dong,Qiyu Yang,Shengbo Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究增长素数模下Dirichlet L-函数在临界线上的极值，得到比经典共振方法更大的下界，并验证了Bondarenko等人（2023）二分法中的一支可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirichlet L-函数在临界线上的极值是解析数论的核心问题。Soundararajan的经典共振方法给出了极值下界，但在此族（增长素数模）中可能并非最优；同时Bondarenko、Darbar、Hagen、Heap和Seip（2023）建立了关于极值行为的二分法，需要确定该族究竟属于哪一支。

Method: 采用共振方法（resonance method），并结合对GCD和（GCD sums）的精确估计。

Result: 在所考虑的Dirichlet L-函数族中，获得了比Soundararajan经典共振方法更大的极值下界；由此证明Bondarenko等人（2023）二分法中的一支可在该族上实现。

Conclusion: 增长素数模下的Dirichlet L-函数在临界线上具有比经典共振方法所预示更极端的取值，从而在该族上确定了二分法的一支，深化了对L-函数极值行为的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we obtain extreme values of Dirichlet $L$-functions on the critical line with a growing prime modulus. Our result yields a larger lower bound for extreme values than that from Soundararajan's classical resonance method. Consequently, we show that one branch of the dichotomy established by Bondarenko, Darbar, Hagen, Heap, and Seip (2023) can be realized for this family of Dirichlet $L$-functions. The proof relies on the resonance method together with precise estimates for GCD sums.

</details>


### [7] [A uniform degree bound for modular rank-two Nahm sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02743)
*Cetin Hakimoglu-Brown*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对于秩二对称可对称化Nahm和，若 $q^cF$ 模形式，则其Nahm点生成域的次数 $[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 24$；行和相等时为2，互补Nahm坐标时为2或4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文研究秩二对称可对称化Nahm和的模性与其Nahm点所在数域次数之间的关系，旨在给出模性约束下Nahm点生成域次数的上界，并分类相关退化情形。

Method: 设定秩二可对称化Nahm和，对称化子为 $\operatorname{diag}(d_1,d_2)$，矩阵正定有理、偏移有理，$m=d_2/d_1$。利用模性强制对数径向渐近展开的高阶系数消失，通过Newton多边形与沿行列式二次曲线的极点阶分析研究所得多项式方程；对 $m=1$ 给出共同分量所在显式族。行和相等时用Bloch群挠；互补Nahm坐标时用前两项渐近修正；部分证明依赖精确计算。

Result: 若 $q^cF$ 模形式，则 $[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 24$。对于 $m=1$，多项式方程的共同分量仅出现在一个显式族上，其Nahm点为有理。行和相等时 $[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 2$。互补Nahm坐标时，$m\ne 1$ 时为2，$m=1$ 时为4。

Conclusion: 秩二对称可对称化Nahm和的模性对其Nahm点生成域次数构成强约束，上界为24；在行和相等与互补Nahm坐标等特殊情形下可进一步降至2或4，并刻画了 $m=1$ 时可能出现的退化族。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a rank-two symmetrizable Nahm sum, with any symmetrizer $\operatorname{diag}(d_1,d_2)$, a positive-definite rational matrix and rational shifts, and let $K$ be the field generated by its Nahm point; write $m=d_2/d_1$. We prove that if $q^cF$ is modular, then $[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 24$. Modularity forces the higher coefficients of the logarithmic radial asymptotic expansion to vanish; we study the resulting polynomial equations through their Newton polygons and a pole-order analysis along the determinant conic, and for $m=1$ show that their common components occur only on one explicit family, whose Nahm point is rational. For equal row sums, Bloch-group torsion gives $[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 2$. For complementary Nahm coordinates, the bound is $2$ when $m\ne 1$ and $4$ when $m=1$, using the first two asymptotic corrections in the latter case. Several steps of the proofs rely on exact computation.

</details>


### [8] [The Wang--Sun Sum over Affine Derangements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02806)
*Octavio A. Agustín Aquino*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文计算了仿射群上错排的Wang–Sun型符号和，通过算术分组化为偶数因子上的显式和，用Liu–Xin公式和Cvijović–Klinowski方法得到Bernoulli多项式闭式，并证明若干整性与符号规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅能用定义计算该仿射Wang–Sun型和的初始值，缺乏一般闭式与结构性结论，需要给出可计算且能揭示算术规律的公式。

Method: 对仿射映射π(x)=vx+u按σ=gcd(1-v,n)及u模σ的剩余类分组，乘积仅依赖这些量；对平移符号求和后，全和化为偶数因子上的带局部重数的和；剩余分析块为交错偶次余切幂和，用Liu–Xin公式以Bernoulli多项式与通用系数闭式表达，并用Cvijović–Klinowski结果实现纯有理算术求值。

Result: 得到全仿射和的一般显式公式，数值上可纯有理计算；据此证明该和对2的幂取整数值、对2p（p>3素数）分母为素数，且符号交替。

Conclusion: 该公式将几何/符号和归约为经典余切幂和，提供高效有理算法并揭示整性与符号等一般模式。

Abstract: Let $n$ be even, let $ζ$ be a primitive $n$-th root of unity, and let $Δ_n$ be the set of derangements in the affine group $\overrightarrow{GL}(\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z})$. We evaluate the Wang--Sun type sum \[
  S_n=\sum_{π\inΔ_n}\mathrm{sign}(π)
  \prod_{x\in\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z}}
  \frac{1+ζ^{x-π(x)}}{1-ζ^{x-π(x)}}. \]
  The main step is an arithmetic grouping: for an affine map $π(x)=vx+u$, the product depends only on $σ=\gcd(1-v,n)$ and on the residue class of $u$ modulo $σ$. After summing the translation signs, the full sum becomes a sum over even divisors with explicit local multiplicities. The only remaining analytic blocks are alternating even cotangent power sums; a recent formula of Liu and Xin then gives a closed expression in Bernoulli polynomials and universal coefficients. Moreover, using results by Cvijović and Klinowski we can evaluate the cotangent power sums using exclusively rational arithmetic. This supplies a general formula for the full affine sum whose initial values were computed using the definition only in an earlier work. The formula also lets us prove some general patterns of the sum: it has integral value for powers of $2$, prime denominator for $2p$ for primes $p>3$ and it has alternating signs.

</details>


### [9] [A new upper bound for the irrationality exponent of $ζ(2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02912)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明 ζ(2) 的无理性指数 μ(ζ(2)) < 5.0193784，改进了 Zudilin (2014) 的 5.095412 及 Kleid (2026) 的 5.0495243。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无理性指数是无理数有理逼近研究的核心定量指标，对 ζ(2) 给出更优上界是该领域的持续目标。已有最好结果包括 Zudilin 2014 年的 5.095412 以及 2026 年 9 月 J. Kleid 公开的带机器验证证明的 5.0495243，本文旨在进一步降低该上界。

Method: 采用 Zudilin 的两个超几何构造，但选取新的参数取值。由此得到的两族关于 1 与 ζ(2) 的线性型在充分大指标下重合；这一重合是 Zudilin 所猜想恒等式的一个实例。作者通过比较差值分母的算术上界与其大小的解析上界来证明该重合，未使用 creative telescoping。所有数值常数均在球算术下认证。

Conclusion: 本文通过Cauchy核赋值、严格归一化shtuka积、特征留数与有限挠迹配对等技术，完整刻画了秩一Drinfeld A-模特殊函数模的自由性，解决了Gazda–Maurischat猜想，并给出了基本周期、Drinfeld对数及Pellarin L(1)-级数的精确公式。结论的显著特点是对无穷远闭点次数N≥1的完全一般性，以及不依赖源周期格的自由性假设，从而大幅推广了已有理论框架。

Abstract: Let \(A\) be the coefficient ring of a smooth projective curve over \(\mathbb{F}_q\) with a closed point at infinity of arbitrary degree \(\mathrm{N}=°(\infty)\ge 1\), and let \(\varphi\) be a rank-one Drinfeld \(A\)-module. As an application, Pellarin's \(\mathcal L(1)\)-series is expressed exactly as the first Frobenius twist of the generating differential at a fundamental period. Method: 对互素三元组a+b=c定义质量X与对数质量Y，引入比值λ=Y/X度量平衡性并确定其渐近范围(2,3)；在(X,Y)平面中将abc猜想与Szpiro猜想表示为阈值X=1、Y=3的渐近界，并由此推出二者蕴含关系。随后把Müller、Taktikos、de Weger的逼近增益与幂增益置于同平面，对给定三元组表示定义指数因子f以联系增益与坐标，并证明该族Szpiro猜想等价于f≤3对所有表示一致成立。最后在固定表示的两个独立渐近增益界假设下推导X、Y的上极限界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基自等价群$\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)$是流形分类中的自然不变量。Hambleton与Kreck的交换辫子框架为4维流形的该群计算提供了系统方法，但尚未推广至更高维；本文意在将其扩展到$BO\langle n+1\rangle$-流形。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a closed, oriented manifold. The group $\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)$ of based self-equivalences of $M$ is a natural invariant in manifold classification, and Hambleton and Kreck's commutative braid gives a systematic way to compute it for $4$-manifolds. We extend their framework to $BO\langle n+1\rangle$-manifolds of dimension $2n$ or $2n-1$, under two hypotheses: (F), that the structure map factors through the Postnikov $n$-type, and (T), triviality of the relevant monodromy. Both hold whenever $M$ is stably parallelizable, which is the case in every example we treat. We then discuss products and connected sums of spheres. For $S^n\times S^n$, the isometry group of the relevant intersection form is the symplectic group $Sp_2(\mathbb{Z})=SL_2(\mathbb{Z})$ when $n$ is odd, and the finite group $(\mathbb{Z}/2)^2$ when $n$ is even, which we verify directly with a Whitehead-product argument. Feeding this into the braid gives, in each case, a short exact sequence $0\to K\to\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)\to Q\to0$, with $Q$ this same isometry group and $K$ a bordism-theoretic kernel built from unstable homotopy groups of spheres. We work this out for $S^1\times S^{m-1}$; for $S^3\times S^3$, where the sequence splits as a direct product; for connected sums of $r$ copies of $S^3\times S^3$, where permuting the summands acts non-trivially on $K$ instead; for $S^3\times S^4$; and for $S^4\times S^4$, where $Q$ is fully determined but $K$ is only conjectured. We also discuss the connected sum $(S^3\times S^5)$ \connsum $(S^3\times S^5)$, where the same method gets most of the way to an answer without quite closing it. Throughout, comparing with the smooth structure set shows how differently homotopy and diffeomorphism classification can behave.

</details>

Abstract: A regular complex bundle on an orbispace has fibres that are positive multiples of the stabilizers' regular representations. Abstract: In this paper we study a discrete homotopy theory for undirected graphs. More precisely, we consider $A$-homotopy groups, first defined in the work of Barcelo, Kramer, Laubenbacher and Weaver. For every degree $n$ and every group $G$ (Abelian if $n\geq 2$), we construct a graph whose $n$-th $A$-homotopy group is isomorphic to $G$ and whose other $A$-homotopy groups all vanish. Using these, we show that for every infinite sequence of groups $G_1,G_2,\ldots$, with $G_i$ Abelian for $i\geq 2$, there is a graph $X$ such that the $i$-th $A$-homotopy group of $X$ is isomorphic to $G_i$ for all $i$. Along the way, we identify relative $A$-homotopy groups with relative classical homotopy groups, and prove a discrete version of the Blakers-Massey theorem. We then construct partial Postnikov systems and (full) Whitehead towers.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dipolar-octupolar（DO）烧绿石材料中的分数化热力学此前多用玻色量子自旋冰图像解释（如Ce基化合物），需要发展费米子部分子视角，并超越已有仅限U(1)部门的DO烧绿石QSL分类。

Result: PSG给出与一般费米子分类相同的48个对称Z₂类；DO对称性（由子格无关算符编码）选出物理上不同的对称允许平均场通道，其ansatz、自旋子谱和可观测量均不同于有效自旋1/2情形及玻色DO分类。相图中仅少数Z₂态稳定，若干态支持无能隙或平带自旋子谱；比热幂律指数可作为自旋子节点结构的诊断，近线性温度比热与宽连续谱可解释Nd₂ScNbO₇等DO材料的无能隙分数化热力学。

Abstract: We construct and classify $Z_2$ quantum spin liquids (QSLs) of fermionic partons on the dipolar-octupolar (DO) pyrochlore lattice. The DO pseudospin shares the space-group and time-reversal algebra of the effective spin-1/2 Kramers doublet, its projective symmetry group (PSG) yields the same 48 symmetric $Z_2$ classes as the generic fermionic classification. We show, the distinct DO symmetry action, encoded in a set of sublattice-independent operators, selects a physically different set of symmetry-allowed mean-field channels, so that the resulting ansatze, spinon spectra, and observables differ from both the effective spin-1/2 case and the bosonic DO classification. Decomposing the anisotropic XYZ exchange model into all symmetric singlet and triplet hopping and pairing channels and minimizing the mean-field energy self-consistently, we obtain phase diagrams in the ($\tilde{J_x}, \tilde{J_z}$) plane in which only a few $Z_2$ states, several supporting gapless or flat-band spinon spectra, are stabilized. For these states we compute the spinon band structures, the low-temperature specific heat, and the dynamical spin structure factor, and we argue that the power-law specific-heat exponent is a diagnostic of the spinon nodal structure. A near-linear-in-temperature specific heat together with broad continua in the structure factor gives a fermionic-parton account of the gapless, fractionalized thermodynamics reported in DO materials such as $Nd_2ScNbO_7$, complementing the bosonic quantum-spin-ice picture of the Ce-based compounds. The work extends the fermionic-parton classification of DO pyrochlore QSLs beyond the $U(1)$ sector, where $Z_2$ topological order is realized equally naturally by fermionic and bosonic spinons.

</details>


### [31] [Fractionalization-Induced Time-Reversal Symmetry Breaking at Continuous Superconducting Transitions in Low-Symmetry Kondo Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02635)

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在常规Landau理论中，超导与额外对称性的同时连续出现通常需要多维序参量或精细调节。作者旨在寻找一条不依赖精细调节的新路径，利用对称分数化来规避这一限制。

Method: 基于Kondo格点中的Z2自旋液体，分析Kondo场继承的投影对称作用。当投影时间反演作用满足平方为-1时，利用投影对称代数论证其禁止保持时间反演对称的凝聚，并导致临界模二重简并；由于Z2自旋液体中的Kondo凝聚同时破坏电磁U(1)对称性，超导与时间反演破缺可在单一连续相变中出现。作者在各项异性Kondo-Kitaev模型中具体验证该机制。

Result: 即使晶体对称性只允许一维不可约表示，也可实现超导与时间反演破缺的同时连续出现。在各项异性Kondo-Kitaev模型中，超导、时间反演破缺与镜面反演对称性的破缺同时发生。

Conclusion: 对称分数化为单一连续相变同时触发超导和额外对称性破缺提供了新机制，无需多维序参量或精细调节，并可在具体模型中实现。

Abstract: In conventional Landau theory, simultaneous continuous onset of superconductivity and the breaking of additional symmetries generally requires a multidimensional order parameter or fine tuning. We show that symmetry fractionalization provides a different route. In a Kondo lattice with a $\mathbb Z_2$ spin liquid, the Kondo fields inherit the projective symmetry action of the spinons. When the projective time-reversal action on these fields squares to $-1$, the projective symmetry algebra forbids a condensate that preserves time-reversal symmetry and enforces twofold degeneracy of the critical modes. Because Kondo condensation in a $\mathbb Z_2$ spin liquid also breaks electromagnetic $U(1)$ symmetry, superconductivity and time-reversal symmetry breaking can emerge at a single continuous transition, even if crystal symmetry admits only one-dimensional irreducible representations. We demonstrate this mechanism in an anisotropic Kondo-Kitaev model, where superconductivity and the breaking of time-reversal and mirror symmetries emerge simultaneously.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we investigate a continuum model of two-dimensional electrons interacting via long-range Coulomb forces and subject to a triangular periodic potential at filling factor $ν=2$. Using neural quantum states based on message-passing neural networks optimized with variational Monte Carlo, we determine the ground-state phase diagram as a function of periodic potential strength and Zeeman field. Although the noninteracting system is expected to form a conventional band insulator, we show that strong correlations can qualitatively reshape the insulating state and, in part of the phase diagram, suppress the band insulator in favor of a correlated metallic phase, with a first-order transition separating the two regimes. Moreover, the metal--insulator transition exhibits a strong dependence on spin polarization: the unpolarized system becomes insulating at substantially weaker periodic potentials through the formation of an $s$-wave molecular insulator, where opposite-spin electrons bind near each potential minimum, whereas the fully polarized system requires a significantly deeper potential and instead forms a symmetry-broken orbital nematic molecular crystal. This spin-dependent molecular transition gives rise to discontinuous magnetization curves, metamagnetic transitions, and several multicritical points in the phase diagram, together with clear signatures in the density profiles, real-space correlation functions, and structure factors. To further elucidate the role of the long-range character of the Coulomb interaction, we also study short-range repulsive interactions, which can be realized with ultracold-atom platforms. We find that the qualitative structure of the many-body phase diagram remains unchanged, demonstrating the robustness of our results across different interaction models.

Abstract: We determine the thermal melting phase diagram of the electron solid by measuring the onset density of the double-threshold voltage-current curves in the insulating state in the ultra-high mobility two-dimensional electron system in SiGe/Si/SiGe quantum wells at different temperatures in perpendicular magnetic fields. The introduction of a magnetic field leads to a considerable increase in the onset density, which is strongest at the lowest temperatures and decreases with increasing temperature, and the melting temperature increases with magnetic field. These data are in agreement with theoretical predictions. Unexpectedly, the melting temperature as a function of electron density in a fixed magnetic field reveals a plateau, which can be interpreted in terms of resonance modes in the crystallites.

Abstract: Many-body perturbation theory is a powerful framework to calculate spectroscopic quantities, as well as the total energy. However, the field suffers from the fact that different flavors of approximations lead to a wide range of results. We present several in principle exact expansions and discuss how to use them consistently. We concentrate on the first two orders in the respective screened Coulomb interaction. Of special interest is a consistent expansion around the Kohn-Sham system. We illustrate the performance of the various choices using the symmetric Hubbard dimer over a wide range of correlation strength. In particular, the expansion based on exact Kohn-Sham ingredients stands out as a promising route towards good energies and good spectra.

</details>


### [35] [Decohering Kitaev's Sixteenfold Way: A Holographic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03610)
*Shuyu Zhang,Linhao Li,Zhen Bi,Tzu-Chieh Wei,Zijian Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究二维拓扑序中的任意子退相干，以Kitaev十六重态中的费米子退相干为显式例子，并猜想退相干诱导的相变密度矩阵可由三步过程（提升至Walker-Wang模型、过渡至另一Walker-Wang模型、迹掉体自由度）获得，将二维拓扑序退相干与其三维对应体的纯态相变联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十六重态包含手性拓扑相，一般认为其不允许有交换投影算子哈密顿量（commuting projector Hamiltonian），因此难以直接研究其退相干，需要一种在交换投影算子层面研究手性拓扑序任意子退相干的系统方法。

Method: 以Kitaev十六重态的费米子退相干为显式例子研究二维拓扑序中的任意子退相干；提出一个三步猜想过程：先将拓扑序提升为其Walker-Wang模型，随后过渡至另一个Walker-Wang模型，最后迹掉体自由度，从而得到退相干诱导相变的密度矩阵，并给出多个例子加以说明。

Result: 发现对十六种态中的费米子进行退相干，总会得到内禀混合态拓扑序（intrinsically mixed-state topological order），这是因为对应的三维体总是转变为三维费米子toric code（fTC）。

Conclusion: 结果建立二维拓扑序退相干与其三维对应体纯态相变之间的联系，并提示了一种在交换投影算子哈密顿量层面系统研究手性拓扑序中任意子退相干的方法。

Abstract: We study anyon decoherence in 2D topological order, with the fermion decoherence in Kitaev's sixteenfold way as explicit examples. The sixteenfold way includes chiral topological phases, which are believed to admit no commuting projector Hamiltonian, making it challenging to study their decoherence directly. We further conjecture the density matrix produced by this decoherence induced phase transition can be obtained by a three-step procedure: first lift the topological order to its Walker-Wang model, followed by a transition to another Walker-Wang model, and finally trace over the bulk degrees of freedom. This establishes a connection between the decoherence of a topological order and a pure state phase transition in its 3D counterpart. Many examples are given. In particular, we show that decohering the fermion in each of the sixteen states always yields an intrinsically mixed-state topological order, a consequence of the fact the corresponding 3D bulk always transits into the 3D fermionic toric code (fTC). Our result suggests a systematic method for studying anyon decoherence in chiral topological orders at the level of commuting projector Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [36] [Finite-momentum pairing and magnetic halos in a spin-imbalanced Holstein model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03678)
*Chunhan Feng,George Batrouni,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用Holstein模型中延迟吸引对符号涨落的抑制，实现自旋不平衡下的无偏DQMC模拟，揭示零动量配对向有限动量FFLO配对的演化及其与CDW、自旋关联的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFLO超导（自旋不平衡驱动的有限动量库珀配对）在二维中难以数值研究；自旋不平衡吸引Hubbard模型的费米子符号问题严重限制了量子蒙特卡洛在低温配对关联显著区域的访问能力。

Method: 采用Holstein模型（声子动力学导致的延迟有效吸引）以强抑制自旋不平衡下的符号涨落，从而在深进入该区域时进行无偏的行列式量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）模拟。

Result: 随磁化增强，零动量配对演化为有限动量FFLO配对，表现为实空间配对关联振荡以及配对磁化率峰移至有限Q；有限动量磁化率显著高于零动量值。在高密度n~0.875、磁化m≈1-n（多数自旋密度接近半满）时，短程上FFLO配对与棋盘电荷密度波序共存，较大距离处出现交错纵向自旋关联作为磁性晕。

Conclusion: 上述空间纹理为有限动量配对提供了可实验观测的特征，并推动冷原子体系实现FFLO物理。

Abstract: Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov (FFLO) superconductivity, Cooper pairing at finite-momentum driven by spin imbalance, remains difficult to study numerically in two dimensions. The fermion sign problem in the spin-imbalanced attractive Hubbard model severely limits quantum Monte Carlo access to the low temperatures where pairing correlations are most pronounced. We show that the Holstein model, in which the effective attraction is retarded by phonon dynamics, strongly suppresses sign fluctuations under spin imbalance, enabling unbiased determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations deep in this regime. With increasing magnetization, zero-momentum pairing evolves into finite-momentum FFLO pairing, evidenced by oscillating real-space pair correlations and a shift of the pairing susceptibility peak to finite $\vec{Q}$. The finite-momentum susceptibility substantially exceeds its zero-momentum value. At high density $n \sim 0.875$, near magnetization $m \approx 1-n$ where the majority-spin density approaches half filling, FFLO pairing coexists with checkerboard charge-density-wave order at short range, while staggered longitudinal spin correlations emerge as a magnetic halo at larger distances. These spatial textures provide experimentally accessible signatures of finite-momentum pairing and motivate cold-atom realizations of FFLO physics.

</details>

### [37] [Awomo-SimDataEngine: Agentic Simulation-ReadyWorld Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02274)
*Awomo-PhysicalRSI Team,Danjiao Ma,Enhui Ma,Haohan Liu,Heng Jia,Hui Shan,Jianhua Xu,Jiahuan Zhang,Jiangdi Xu,Kaiwen Guo,Kaicheng Yu,Linwei Zhang,Liyang Jin,Maochun Luo,Pengyao Niu,Shiwen Li,Shuangyu Feng,Tong Zhang,Tianheng Wang,Xin Wang,Xiangru Huang,Yongqiang Huang,Zhaozhi Wang,Zijian Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Awomo-SimDataEngine 是一个智能体系统，将资产/场景生成与机器人演示合成打通，在 LIBERO-Plus 上通过联合训练将 WAM 成功率从 77.17% 提升到 89.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成有用的机器人训练数据需要物体可交互、布局物理有效且任务可重复执行，仅视觉上合理的场景是不够的。现有流程中资产、场景与演示合成彼此割裂，缺乏统一的构建、验证与修复机制。

Method: 系统由共享资产服务（含 ISArt 的结构接地部件与关节生成）提供刚体和铰接物体；场景生成有两条路线：Unravel 从图像重建可编辑场景，SimForge 从文本构建单/多房间环境；图原生 harness 协调构建、验证与有界修复，将失败路由至责任模块并保留未受影响的场景状态；PolicyForge 将验证过的世界与任务、机器人本体绑定，产出可回放演示。

Result: 评测涵盖资产几何、场景质量与下游策略学习。在基于 MuJoCo 的 LIBERO-Plus 上，加入 Isaac Sim 演示联合训练后，World-Action Model 总体成功率从 77.17% 提升至 89.43%；目标类与空间类成功率分别提升 31.66 和 6.25 个百分点。长时程任务上收益较有限。

Conclusion: 生成数据对跨仿真器策略训练具有实用价值，尤其在目标与空间任务上提升显著；但长时程任务仍受限于现有能力，是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Generating useful robot-training data requires more than visually plausiblescenes: objects must support interaction, placements must remain physicallyvalid, and tasks must admit repeatable execution. We present\textbf{Awomo-SimDataEngine}, an agentic system that connects asset and scenegeneration to robot demonstration synthesis. Shared asset services providerigid and articulated objects, including structure-grounded part and jointgeneration with ISArt. Scene generation supports two complementary routes:Unravel reconstructs editable scenes from images, while SimForge buildssingle-room and multi-room environments from text. A graph-native harnesscoordinates construction, validation, andbounded repair, routing failures to the responsible module while retainingunaffected scene state. PolicyForge binds validated worlds to tasks and robotembodiments to produce replayable demonstrations. Evaluations cover assetgeometry, scene quality, and downstream policy learning. On MuJoCo-basedLIBERO-Plus, co-training with Isaac Sim demonstrations improves the overallsuccess rate of a World-Action Model (WAM) from $77.17\%$ to $89.43\%$. Goal and spatialsuccess improve by $31.66$ and $6.25$ percentage points, respectively.These results support the utility of the generated data for cross-simulatorpolicy training, with more limited gains on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [38] [World-Calibrated Proposal-to-Action Flow for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02323)
*Jie He,Wei Li,Junwen Tong,Rui Shao,Wei-Shi Zheng,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProAct是一个"世界校准的proposal-to-action"框架，使生成式源本身可预测：用Proposal Expert保留运动连续性，用World Expert预测未来并校准以proposal为中心的各向异性源，从而提升性能、减少去噪步数与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的VLA策略从任务无关的各向同性高斯源生成动作块，该源既未以近期执行状态为条件，也未以预测的未来演化为条件，导致：(i) 丢弃了近期已执行运动建立的局部连续性；(ii) 即使引入预测性世界表征，也往往只条件化传输动力学，而不决定生成起点、允许的偏离范围以及可扩展的动作方向。

Method: 提出ProAct框架：(i) Proposal Expert将近期动作通过一步以运动为锚的端点流匹配转换为场景感知假设，使生成初始化在演示动作流形附近；(ii) 前瞻性World Expert将该假设作为软运动先验，预测任务一致的潜在未来，以联合捕获预期场景演化与proposal-future兼容性；(iii) 基于该兼容性校准以proposal为中心的各向异性源：有界的每步范围控制允许偏离，迹归一化的低秩几何在条件数预算下将细化分配到耦合的平移、旋转与夹爪方向。

Result: 与π0.5相比，ProAct在仿真与真实世界任务上均提升性能，同时减少50%去噪步数、推理延迟最多降低25.8%、吞吐量最多提升34.8%。

Conclusion: 使生成式源本身可预测（基于世界校准的proposal）能在保持运动连续性并联合建模未来演化与proposal兼容性的同时，显著提升VLA策略的效率与性能。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies generate action chunks by transporting samples from a task-agnostic isotropic Gaussian source. As this source is conditioned on neither recent execution nor predicted future evolution, (i) it discards the local continuity established by recently executed motion. (ii) Even when predictive world representations are introduced, they often only condition the transport dynamics rather than determine where generation starts, how far it may deviate, or along which action directions it may expand. Building on this observation, we introduce ProAct, a world-calibrated proposal-to-action framework that makes the generative source itself predictable. (i) To preserve motion continuity, a lightweight Proposal Expert converts recent actions into a scene-aware hypothesis via one motion-anchored endpoint flow-matching step, initializing generation near the demonstrated action manifold. (ii) To jointly capture intended scene evolution and proposal-future compatibility, a prospective World Expert treats the hypothesis as a soft motion prior while predicting the task-consistent latent future. (iii) From this compatibility, the model calibrates a proposal-centered anisotropic source, where a bounded per-step extent controls the allowed deviation and a trace-normalized low-rank geometry under a condition-number budget allocates refinement over coupled translation, rotation, and gripper directions. Compared with $π_{0.5}$, ProAct improves performance across simulation and real-world tasks while reducing denoising steps by 50%, inference latency by up to 25.8%, and increasing throughput by up to 34.8%.

</details>


### [39] [SoTa: Soft Tactile Skins for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02338)
*Jingyun Yang,Baiyu Shi,Timothy Yu,Haitian Liu,Alberta Longhini,Weichen Wang,Rika Antonova,Zhenan Bao,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SoTa，一种低成本电容式触觉皮肤，可为人类与机器人手部提供全覆盖且布局一致的202个触觉单元，支持人-机器人协同训练，显著提升接触丰富操作任务的策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视触觉机器人数据稀缺：灵巧演示依赖遥操作，限制了数据集规模。人类演示采集成本更低、可扩展，但前提是人类手与机器人手需具备信号对应的触觉传感器，这要求传感器能适应不同手部几何、覆盖全手且在跨具身间共享统一布局。

Method: 设计SoTa多层电容触觉皮肤，采用织物电极，实现薄、柔软、几何可定制的皮肤，材料成本低于10美元；在人与机器人对应手指与手掌区域保持202个触觉单元的一致布局，从而无需学习跨传感器映射即可用统一触觉编码器进行人-机器人协同训练。

Result: 传感器在10,000次加载-卸载循环后仍保留超过97%的初始响应范围，1,280次紧握拳折叠循环中走线保持连续。在三个接触丰富操作任务中，触觉观测较仅视觉策略提升分布内成功率；在固定机器人演示预算下，加入人类演示使八种评估条件的平均成功率从22.8%提升至45.9%，并在全部五种分布外条件下均有提升。

Conclusion: SoTa以低成本、可自制、跨具身布局一致的触觉皮肤，解决了视触觉数据规模化难题，验证了人-机器人协同训练的有效性；作者计划开源制造与使用所需资源。

Abstract: A growing body of work suggests that tactile sensing gives robot policies contact information that complements vision in dexterous manipulation. However, visuo-tactile robot data remains scarce: dexterous demonstrations require teleoperating robots, which limits dataset scale. Human demonstrations are far cheaper to collect and offer a path to scale this data, but only if human and robot hands carry tactile sensors with corresponding signals. This requires sensors that conform to different hand geometries, cover the full hand, and share a common layout across embodiments. We present SoTa, a low-cost capacitive tactile skin that provides full-hand coverage on humans and robots while preserving a shared layout of 202 taxels across corresponding finger and palm regions. Our multilayer design with fabric electrodes enables in-house fabrication of thin, soft skins with customizable geometry for under $10 in materials per skin. The sensor retains over 97% of its initial response span after 10,000 loading-unloading cycles with traces retaining continuity through 1,280 tight-fist folding cycles. The shared taxel layout supports human-robot co-training with a common tactile encoder and no learned cross-sensor mapping. Across three contact-rich manipulation tasks, tactile observations improve in-distribution success over vision-only policies. With a fixed robot demonstration budget, adding human demonstrations more than doubles mean success across eight evaluation conditions, from 22.8% to 45.9%, improving success in all five out-of-distribution conditions. We plan to open-source the resources needed to fabricate and operate these skins.

</details>


### [40] [NEEDLEWORK: Offline Rewriting of Robot Data with Verified Local Stitches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02339)
*Juntao Ren,Yifan Hou,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEEDLE 是一种离线数据集增强算法，通过在高维机器人演示的已记录观测之间添加经过验证的短动作桥，实现轨迹缝合，无需新环境交互或特权状态，并在真机任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示即便单条轨迹低效或失败，仍可能包含有用行为。轨迹缝合可将这些行为组合为更好的训练数据，但在高维机器人数据中，识别有用连接并验证其可行性十分困难，已有方法多依赖低维状态表示。

Method: NEEDLE 仅使用 RGB 图像、本体感知和回合级结果，在高维演示的已记录观测之间识别并创建连接，绕开次优绕行、扩大动作覆盖，并利用失败轨迹扩充原始数据集，且无需新环境交互或特权物体状态。随后提出一种采样技术，将接受的桥接纳入策略训练，不合成中间图像、不丢弃原始演示。

Result: 在真实机器人任务上，NEEDLE 相对每个任务中最强基线，平均提升成功率达 21 个百分点。

Conclusion: NEEDLE 表明，仅凭 RGB 图像、本体感知与回合级结果即可在高维机器人演示中进行有效的轨迹缝合式数据增强，使策略在学习替代动作的同时保留原始数据覆盖，从而显著提升真机任务成功率。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations may contain useful behavior even when individual episodes are inefficient or unsuccessful. Trajectory stitching offers a way to compose these behaviors into improved training data, but identifying useful connections and verifying their feasibility is difficult in high-dimensional robot data, where many prior methods rely on low-dimensional state representations. We introduce NEEDLE, an offline dataset-augmentation algorithm that addresses these challenges by adding short, verified action bridges between recorded observations in high-dimensional robot demonstrations. First, NEEDLE identifies and creates connections that bypass suboptimal detours, broaden action coverage, and augment the original dataset with failed trajectories, using only RGB images, proprioception, and episode-level outcomes, without new environment interaction or privileged object state. Next, we present a sampling technique that incorporates accepted bridges into policy training without synthesizing intermediate images or discarding the original demonstrations, allowing policies to learn alternative actions while retaining the original dataset's coverage. On real-robot tasks, NEEDLE improves success rate over the strongest baseline on each task by an average of 21 percentage points. Videos and supplementary materials are on https://needle-work.github.io/.

</details>


### [41] [Filter-Aware Fine-Tuning for Safe Humanoid Whole-Body Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02341)
*Pranit Mohnot,Christian Helten,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文揭示人形机器人跟踪策略与运行时安全滤波器（CBF）独立设计的根本性失配，并提出滤波器感知微调方法 CoFiT，显著降低约束违反时间并减少滤波器干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中，人形机器人安全全身运动至关重要。当前主流方案将参考生成（规划器/遥操作/运动生成器）与执行（强化学习跟踪策略）分离，再用 CBF 等运行时安全滤波器对跟踪器输出进行干预以施加新约束。然而作者指出，将跟踪策略与安全滤波器独立处理会导致根本性失配：滤波不仅改变执行动作，还改变诱导的状态分布。

Method: 通过案例研究剖析策略—滤波器接口，隔离出动力学失配、目标失配与信息失配三类问题并追溯其根因；基于这些洞见提出 CoFiT（Constrained Filter-aware Tuning），一种面向预训练跟踪器的滤波器感知微调方法。

Result: 在多种约束场景下，CoFiT 相比仅滤波器训练将违反时间在 TWIST2 上降低 91%、在 SONIC 上降低 21%，且所需安全滤波器修正更小。在 Unitree G1 硬件上，CoFiT 将 TWIST2 违反时间降低 83%，所有试验均无需操作员干预完成，而基线有 50% 试验需要操作员急停。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于策略—滤波器交互的可操作洞见，并确立了将学习型跟踪器与运行时安全滤波器集成的设计原则。

Abstract: Safe whole-body motion is essential for deploying humanoid robots in unstructured environments. Modern humanoid control commonly separates reference specification from execution, with a planner, teleoperator, or motion generator providing a reference that a reinforcement-learning policy tracks through dynamically feasible whole-body control. Runtime safety filters, such as control barrier functions (CBFs), offer a promising approach for enforcing newly introduced constraints via interventions on the tracker's outputs. We show, however, that treating the tracking policy and safety filter independently induces fundamental mismatches, as filtering alters both the executed actions and the induced state distribution. We study this policy-filter interface through case studies that isolate dynamics, objective, and information mismatches, highlight their root causes, and use these insights to develop CoFiT (Constrained Filter-aware Tuning), a filter-aware fine-tuning method for pretrained trackers. Across diverse constraint scenes, CoFiT reduces violation time relative to filter-only training by 91% on TWIST2 and 21% on SONIC, while requiring smaller safety filter corrections. On Unitree G1 hardware, CoFiT reduces violation time by 83% for TWIST2 and completes every trial without operator intervention, whereas 50% of baseline trials require an operator stop. Together, these results provide actionable insights into policy-filter interactions and establish design principles for integrating learned trackers with runtime safety filters.

</details>


### [42] [SocialVLA: A Social Perception Gateway for Human-Reaction-Based Failure Detection and Recovery in VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02360)
*Sofya Konstantinova,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SocialVLA 是一种本地、与策略无关的社交感知网关，将人类自发的语音、面部与语言反应转化为 VLA 机器人操作的运行时中断信号，并在物理机器人上验证了从社交反应到物理中断与恢复的完整本地闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA（视觉-语言-动作）策略可支持多样化的机器人操作，但在执行过程中无法识别自身错误而失败。人类观察者的自发反应（声音、面部、语言）往往在失败发生前就已出现，可提供互补的中断信号。现有方法缺乏将这类自发的社会性反应转化为运行时干预的机制，因此作者希望构建一个与策略无关的感知网关来利用这一信号。

Method: 提出 SocialVLA，一个本地、与策略无关的社交感知网关。系统融合：①因果副语言音频检测，②视觉反应识别，③显式停止短语，④机器人相关性估计。采用异步“首事件”融合机制，由最早达到足够置信度的信号触发 VLA 暂停；另设独立语音通道捕获口头纠正，用于参与者主导的继续、重启或指令修订。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 物理操作平台上，15 名参与者、238 个标注的应干预片段和 1.038 小时非干预行为。冻结离线回放：召回率 54.6%，精确率 69.5%；未过滤的音视频融合召回率达 64.3%。相关性估计将误停片段从 100 降至 57，精确率从 60.5% 提升至 69.8%。在第 16 名未见参与者的前瞻部署中，冻结系统召回率 59.5%，精确率 91.7%。延迟方面：检测器到融合中位数 47.9 ms，VLA 门控到物理暂停 336 ms，反应发生到暂停 1.021 s。

Conclusion: SocialVLA 实现了从自发的社会性反应到物理 VLA 中断、再到参与者主导恢复的完整本地通路，验证了将人类即时社会信号用于机器人运行时干预的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies enable diverse robotic manipulation but can fail during execution without recognizing their own errors. Human observers provide complementary signals, as unexpected robot behavior can trigger rapid vocal, facial, or verbal reactions before failure is completed. We introduce SocialVLA, a local, policy-agnostic social perception gateway that converts spontaneous human reactions into runtime intervention signals for VLA manipulation. SocialVLA combines causal paralinguistic audio detection, visual reaction recognition, explicit stop phrases, and robot-relevance estimation. An asynchronous first-event fusion mechanism triggers a VLA hold from the earliest sufficiently confident signal, while a separate speech channel captures verbal corrections for participant-directed continuation, restart, or instruction revision. We evaluate SocialVLA on physical Unitree G1 manipulation using 15 participants, with 238 annotated intervention-worthy episodes and 1.038 h of non-intervention behavior. Frozen offline replay achieves 54.6% recall and 69.5% precision, while unfiltered audio-video fusion reaches 64.3% recall. Relevance estimation reduces false-stop episodes from 100 to 57 and increases precision from 60.5% to 69.8%. In prospective deployment on an unseen 16th participant, the frozen system achieves 59.5% recall and 91.7% precision. Median detector-to-fusion latency is 47.9 ms, VLA-gate-to-physical-hold latency is 336 ms, and reaction-onset-to-hold latency is 1.021 s. These results demonstrate a complete local pathway from spontaneous social reaction to physical VLA interruption and participant-directed recovery.

</details>


### [43] [Rethinking World-Action Model for Compositional and In-Context Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02368)
*Shukai Gong,Xuanran Zhai,Yintianrun Zhang,Ruopeng Cui,Ye Huang,Yiyang Fu,Dexuan Lyu,Chaojie Li,Xinyi Song,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Mingyuan Jia,Yufan Deng,Jiaxin Fang,Bo Liang,Jiaxin Li,Yuxiang Gao,Hao Liu,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViGAR：一种将操作任务分解为视觉子目标规划器与子目标执行器的分层框架，二者共享预训练世界模型表示，实现子任务级推理与上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程组合操作任务需多子任务协调，现有 world-action models 虽联合预测短时程视觉未来与动作，但缺乏显式的子任务级推理能力。

Method: 提出 Visual Goal-conditioned Action Reasoning (ViGAR) 分层框架：子目标规划器根据当前观测与全局指令预测下一子任务的视觉子目标；子目标执行器以该子目标为条件，联合生成未来视觉轨迹与动作；两组件共享预训练世界模型表示，从而共享物理知识；并以全局目标图像作为上下文实现无需参数更新的上下文学习。

Result: 在 RoboTwin Clean2Random 基准上，Clean 与 Random 设置下成功率分别为 82.00% 与 67.02%，平均成功率超过最强基线 12.86 个百分点；在五个组合任务与两个上下文学习任务的真实机器人实验中也验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 显式子任务级分解与共享世界模型表示可显著提升长时程组合操作性能，并原生支持上下文学习，具备真实机器人部署价值。

Abstract: Long-horizon compositional manipulation has become increasingly important for real-world robot deployment, where a single task involves multiple coordinated subtasks. Existing world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict short-horizon visual futures and actions, but typically lack explicit subtask-level reasoning. We propose Visual Goal-conditioned Action Reasoning (ViGAR), a hierarchical framework that factorizes manipulation into a visual subgoal planner and a subgoal executor. Given the current observation and global instruction, the subgoal planner predicts a visual subgoal for the next subtask. The subgoal executor then jointly generates future visual trajectories and actions conditioned on the predicted subgoal. Both components share a pretrained world-model representation, enabling task-level planning and action generation to benefit from common physical knowledge. Moreover, our framework naturally supports in-context learning: using a global goal image as context can induce different subtask decompositions and behaviors without parameter updates. On the RoboTwin Clean2Random benchmark, ViGAR achieves 82.00% and 67.02% success rates under the Clean and Random settings, respectively, surpassing the strongest baseline by 12.86 percentage points in average success rate. Real-world robot experiments on five compositional and two in-context learning tasks further confirm the effectiveness of ViGAR.

</details>


### [44] [Keep the Effect, Drop the Actor: Programmable Effect-to-Execution World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02398)
*Junyi Hu,Zhewen He,Zhenhua Li,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"效果程序"表示及PEWAM世界-动作模型：以物体3D关键点轨迹等为条件进行四流独立流匹配生成，实现可编辑、跨本体、闭环重解的机器人操作编程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示同时记录了"物体发生了什么"与"某条机械臂如何做到"。作者认为前者才是任务本质、具身无关的，因而以效果而非动作作为条件，可获得可编辑、可跨本体迁移、可闭环重解的策略表示，摆脱对语言或目标图像的依赖。

Method: 将演示编译为"效果程序"：被移动物体的3D关键点轨迹、标记抓取位置的两点、去除演示者后的终止场景构型。PEWAM（71.5M参数的世界-动作模型）把效果、机器人执行、动作、终止状态作为四条流，采用相互独立的流匹配时间采样；固定（clamp）程序并采样执行流，即把推理转化为编程，并基于实时场景闭环重解。

Result: LIBERO-Goal留出任务上，单条演示的程序完成90个episode中的40个，而同一骨干网络以目标图像或语言为条件、以及已发表的演示条件方法最多完成19个；在Meta-World三个留出类别上超过最佳已发表结果（但五类均值未超过）。程序可人工编辑：位移改变放置、旋转接触点改变抓取姿态。同一程序无需重训练即可在四种机械臂上运行；受扰动后闭环重解完成60个episode中的23个，而回放演示仅完成6个。在Franka真机上，仅用其他任务的真实演示微调后，由单段人类视频编译的程序完成40次试验中的36次，而以视频末帧为目标图像的同一骨干仅完成22次。

Conclusion: 以物体效果为中心的坐标化程序表示，配合多流独立流匹配的生成式世界-动作模型，可在单演示条件下取得显著优于语言/目标图像条件基线与已发表方法的性能，并天然支持程序编辑、跨本体零重训练部署与场景扰动下的闭环重解；真机结果表明其可从普通人类视频中提取可执行程序，具备较强的实用性与可解释性。

Abstract: A robot demonstration records two things in the same frames: what happened to the objects, and how one particular arm made it happen. We condition on the first. A demonstration is compiled into an effect program: the 3D keypoint trajectories of the objects that moved, two points marking where each was held, and the configuration the scene ends in, with the demonstrator removed. PEWAM, a 71.5M-parameter world-action model, generates effect, robot execution, action and terminal state as four streams with independent flow-matching times, so clamping a program and sampling the execution turns inference into programming, re-solved closed loop from the live scene. On held-out LIBERO-Goal tasks, one demonstration's program completes 40 of 90 episodes, where the same backbone given a goal image or language, and published demonstration-conditioned methods, complete at most 19; on three of Meta-World's held-out classes it exceeds the best published results, though not on the five-class mean. Because a program is a set of coordinates, a person can edit it: the placement follows a shifted terminal state and the grasp turns with rotated contact points. The same program runs on four robot arms without retraining, and after a push, re-solving completes 23 of 60 episodes where replaying the demonstration completes 6. On a Franka arm, fine-tuned on real demonstrations of other tasks, programs compiled from single human videos complete 36 of 40 trials, against 22 for the same backbone conditioned on the video's last frame as a goal image.

</details>


### [45] [Autonomous mobile robot operations logistics: a dataset of jobs, dispatch events and robot states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02428)
*Jan-Felix Klein,Yongkuk Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了MoRoOp数据集，记录了自主移动机器人（AMR）在实验室物料配送场景中连续九个八小时班次的操作数据，包含1382个作业、4815个操作、19352个调度事件和140386条机器人状态观测，并提供原始与清洗后的状态表，支持AI代理评估、扰动检测、操作预测、数据驱动仿真和事件日志分析等重用方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMR在生产物流中的物料运输日益普及，其运行受作业生成、调度和机器人控制共同支配，但缺乏能够同时链接作业规格、操作过程、调度事件与机器人状态观测的真实运行数据集，制约了相关AI方法与仿真分析的研究。

Method: 在实验室套件准备与供给场景中，通过九个八小时班次采集AMR运行数据；每班次机器人执行随机生成的套件供给、空箱补充和充电作业；数据集将作业规格、构成各作业的操作、记录操作状态转移与结果的调度事件，以及包含位置、朝向、速度、各轮荷电状态和诊断信息的机器人状态观测相互关联，并保留技术上有效的延误、导航受阻和操作失败观测，同时提供原始与清洗后的机器人状态表。

Result: 数据集共包含1382个作业、4815个操作、19352个调度事件和140386条机器人状态观测，并提供作业生成所用的套件规格；数据在真实运行的实验室环境中采集，保留了延误、导航受阻及不成功操作等有效观测。

Conclusion: MoRoOp为AMR运行研究提供了一个多层级、真实环境下的关联数据集，其记录的重用方向包括操作决策记录上的AI代理评估、扰动检测、操作预测、数据驱动仿真以及事件日志分析。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots (AMRs) increasingly perform material transport in production logistics, where their operation is governed by job generation, dispatching and robot control. We present MoRoOp, a dataset of AMR operations recorded in a laboratory kit preparation and supply scenario over nine eight-hour shifts. During each shift, an AMR executed stochastically generated kit supply, empty-box refill and charging jobs. The dataset links job specifications, the operations constituting each job, dispatch events documenting operation state transitions and outcomes, and robot-state observations comprising position, orientation, velocity, per-wheel state of charge and diagnostics. It contains 1,382 jobs, 4,815 operations, 19,352 dispatch events and 140,386 robot-state observations together with the kit specifications used during job generation. The dataset was recorded in an operating laboratory environment, and technically valid observations of delays, obstructed navigation and unsuccessful operations were retained. Both raw and cleaned robot-state tables are provided. Documented reuse directions include the evaluation of AI agents on operational decision records, disturbance detection, operation prediction, data-driven simulation and event-log analysis.

</details>


### [46] [OpenRUA: Robot-Use Agents Are Zero-Shot Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02459)
*Zhaoyang Chu,Earl T. Barr,Claire Le Goues,Peter O'Hearn,Mark Harman,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpenRUA——一个零抽象层的"工作区即框架"设计，仅向通用编码代理开放机器人的原生软件接口 ROS 2 的终端访问，使其无需专用工作流或定制接口即可充当零样本视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理开始通过编写并执行机器人控制程序介入物理世界。按常理，代理本应直接复用工程师数十年积累的成熟软件栈来读取传感器与控制运动；但既有工作普遍为机器人场景手工构建复杂定制框架，预设专用工作流并提供定制化接口。这引出核心疑问：这类额外的"框架工程"是否真的必要？

Method: OpenRUA 采用极简的 workspace-as-harness 设计：不为代理编排任何代理式工作流，仅提供 ROS 2 文档与基础终端工具，由编码代理自行组织工作。在该工作区内，将感知重塑为文件 I/O、将操作重塑为编码。基于 Claude Code（Claude Opus 5）在 CaP-Bench 与 LIBERO-PRO 上进行零样本评测。

Result: 在 CaP-Bench 上成功率达 99.0%，在 LIBERO-PRO 上达 87.0%，表明通用编码代理可经由机器人原生接口充当零样本视觉运动策略，无需定制原语或任务专训练。分析还揭示显著涌现行为：感知方面，96.80% 的回合中代理自发编写程序处理原始传感输入并推导度量测量；操作方面，95.87% 的回合中自发构建运动控制客户端（如夹爪控制），50.13% 的回合中构建闭环控制程序（如依据传感器反馈调整运动）。

Conclusion: 额外的框架工程并非必要：仅提供原生接口与最小工具面，通用编码代理即可零样本完成机器人感知与控制任务，并在无任何流程编排的情况下自发涌现出度量感知、运动控制客户端与闭环控制等行为，为机器人代理系统设计提供了"少即是多"的新范式。

Abstract: Coding agents are extending their reach into the physical world by writing and executing robot control programs. One might expect the agents to use the existing mature software stack that engineers have developed over decades to access sensors and control motion. Yet prior work primarily engineers complex custom harnesses to orchestrate agents for robot use, particularly by prescribing specialized workflows and providing bespoke interfaces. This raises the question: "Is such additional harness engineering necessary?" We introduce OpenRUA, a zero-abstraction harness that bypasses bespoke abstraction layers by providing off-the-shelf coding agents with only terminal access to the robot's native software interface ROS 2. OpenRUA employs a minimalist workspace-as-harness design, only offering ROS 2 documentation and basic tools while leaving the coding agent to organize its own work without orchestrating any agentic workflow. Within this workspace, OpenRUA recasts perception as file I/O and manipulation as coding. With Claude Code powered by Claude Opus 5, OpenRUA achieves success rates of 99.0% on CaP-Bench and 87.0% on LIBERO-PRO, demonstrating that an off-the-shelf coding agent can serve as a zero-shot visuomotor policy through the robot's native interface, without bespoke primitives or task-specific training. Under this minimalist design, further analysis reveals striking emergent behaviors of coding agents: (1) For perception, the agent spontaneously writes programs that process raw sensory inputs and derive metric measurements in 96.80% of episodes. (2) For manipulation, the agent spontaneously builds motion-control clients (e.g., gripper control) in 95.87% of episodes and closed-loop control programs (e.g., adjusting motion based on sensor feedback) in 50.13% of episodes. Our code is available at https://github.com/terminalworld/OpenRUA.

</details>


### [47] [RUL-Aware RRT*: Degradation-Balanced Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02469)
*Haibo Li,Zhiguo Zeng,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RUL-aware RRT*运动规划方法，将关节剩余使用寿命信息融入规划，缓解关节退化不均问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械臂长时间运行会出现关节退化不均，最弱执行器提前失效，导致非计划停机与重大运营损失；传统运动规划不考虑关节健康状态，反而加剧这种不平衡。

Method: 提出RUL-aware RRT*，在规划过程中引入关节剩余使用寿命（RUL）信息，并根据健康状态变化自适应调整各关节的使用程度；在三种代表性场景（完全健康场景FHS、异质退化场景HDS、局部退化场景LDS）下进行评估。

Result: RUL-aware RRT*能有效抑制退化不平衡，推迟瓶颈失效的发生，并提升机械臂系统在长时间运行中的长期可靠性。

Conclusion: 将健康反馈融入运动规划，是提升持续磨损条件下机械臂耐久性与运行韧性的实用且稳健的途径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulators operating over long durations often experience uneven joint degradation, which causes the weakest actuator to fail prematurely, leads to unplanned downtime, and results in significant operational losses. Traditional motion-planning algorithms do not account for joint health conditions and therefore tend to exacerbate this imbalance during extended operation. To address this challenge, this study introduces the RUL-aware RRT*, a motion-planning method that incorporates joint remaining useful life information into the planning process and adaptively adjusts joint usage in response to evolving health conditions. The method is evaluated across three representative scenarios, namely the Full Health Scenario (FHS), the Heterogeneous Degradation Scenario (HDS), and the Local Degradation Scenario (LDS). The results show that the RUL-aware RRT* effectively suppresses degradation imbalance, delays the emergence of bottleneck failures, and improves the long-term reliability of the robotic system during extended operation. These findings demonstrate that integrating health feedback into motion planning provides a practical and robust pathway for enhancing the durability and operational resilience of manipulators subject to continuous wear.

</details>


### [48] [CriticHack: Evaluating Visual Rewards Under Robot Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02527)
*Jiaxuan Luo,Xingguo Xu,Shanshan Wang,Yuhan Zhou,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 优化学习型视觉奖励模型（如Robometer）会同时提升任务成功率与"错误对象"失败率，且常规监控指标看似健康；通过冻结结果验证器可将优化导向正确任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉奖励模型可能对"作用于错误对象"的执行给出与完成任务同样高的分数，而优化此类奖励会放大错误对象的失败，同时奖励与任务成功率却双双上升，导致从业者惯常监控的信号显示"健康"，掩盖了真实问题。

Method: 在抽屉任务上，对扩散策略的全部去噪器参数针对Robometer进行微调，起始策略为未接触过奖励的监督策略；对比在模拟器任务完成信号上训练的对照组。通过多种实验设置（已有奖励优化历史的策略、策略原生扩散采样器、与初始策略等距、两个critic与两个optimizer的受限策略实验）验证放大效应。提出"tilt model"（倾斜模型）解释该现象，并在26个受限设置中检验预测。同时考察Robometer的成功-终止配方。

Result: 5次训练使512个评估种子上任务成功率提升10.2个百分点，错误对象失败率提升10.9个百分点；而基于模拟器任务完成信号的5次训练成功率提升但不放大错误对象失败（差异9.2个百分点，95% CI 5.6–13.0）。放大效应在多种条件下复现。Robometer整体区分成功与失败良好（AUROC .81），但对错误对象失败的评分略高于成功（AUROC .37），故优化同时抬高两者。倾斜模型在26个受限设置中预测结果偏移（Spearman .89），包括任务成功率下降的情形；Robometer公开的成功-终止配方也继承了该错误。冻结的结果验证器可将同一优化重定向至请求的任务。

Conclusion: 学习型视觉奖励模型在优化中会系统性放大错误对象失败，且不反映在常规监控信号中；研究者提出的倾斜模型能解释并预测该现象，而采用冻结结果验证器可纠正优化方向。

Abstract: Learned visual reward models are increasingly used to optimize robot policies, yet a reward model can score an execution that acts on the wrong object as highly as one that completes the task. We show that optimizing such a reward can amplify these wrong-object failures while reward and task success both rise, so the signals a practitioner would normally monitor look healthy. We fine-tune every denoiser parameter of a diffusion policy against Robometer on a drawer task. Starting from a supervised policy with no prior reward exposure, five training runs raise task success by 10.2 percentage points and wrong-object failures by 10.9 points on 512 evaluation seeds, whereas five runs trained on the simulator's task-completion signal raise success without amplifying wrong-object failures (difference 9.2 points, 95% CI 5.6 to 13.0). The amplification recurs from a policy previously optimized against learned rewards, under the policy's native diffusion sampler, at matched distance from the initial policy, and across constrained-policy experiments with two critics and two optimizers. A tilt model explains when it occurs: under KL-regularized optimization, an outcome becomes more frequent whenever its expected reward under the initial policy exceeds the population average. Robometer separates successes from failures well overall (AUROC .81) but scores wrong-object failures slightly above successes (AUROC .37), so optimization raises both. The same model predicts the outcome shifts across 26 constrained settings (Spearman .89), including those in which task success falls, and Robometer's own published success-termination recipe inherits the error. A frozen outcome verifier redirects the same optimization toward the requested task.

</details>


### [49] [Real-time Event-camera Stereo Visual Odometry via Keytime Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02601)
*Nikan Nobari,Jonathan D. Gammell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种连续时间事件相机双目视觉里程计（VO）流水线，通过关键时间（keytime）状态与WNOA先验插值，在保持事件原生时间分辨率的同时实现实时运行，精度与实时性均优于现有离散方法ES-PTAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率与高动态范围，在运动模糊和弱光环境下优于传统帧相机。要充分发挥这些优势，VO流水线需以原生时间分辨率处理异步事件流。然而，连续时间高斯过程回归配合白噪声加速度（WNOA）先验虽可处理异步测量，但直接应用会导致估计状态量过大，难以实时运行。

Method: 提出连续时间事件相机双目VO流水线：将估计状态缩减至关键时间（keytime），通过基于物理的WNOA先验把测量插值到其精确时间戳，从而在保持全部时间分辨率与事件异步特性的同时，使状态规模与密集测量数量解耦。

Result: 在MVSEC和DSEC数据集上评估。除一个序列外，所有测试序列上精度均优于最先进的离散估计器ES-PTAM；在MVSEC与DSEC上分别以22 Hz和6 Hz实时输出估计；所有有效序列的RMS相对误差为0.46 cm和0.038度，分别比ES-PTAM好11倍和15倍。

Conclusion: 通过关键时间状态与WNOA先验插值的连续时间双目VO流水线，能够在保持事件原生异步时间分辨率的前提下实现实时估计，并在精度与实时性上全面超越现有离散方法ES-PTAM。

Abstract: Event cameras have microsecond-level temporal resolution and high dynamic range which make them more resilient to motion blur and poor illumination than standard frame-based cameras. Event-camera visual odometry (VO) pipelines maximize these benefits when they process the asynchronous event stream at the native temporal resolution. Continuous-time Gaussian process (GP) regression and a white-noise-on-acceleration (WNOA) prior can handle asynchronous measurements but result in a prohibitively large estimation state when applied naively.
  This paper presents a continuous-time event-camera stereo VO pipeline that maintains the native measurement times of asynchronous events while also running in real time. It reduces the estimation states to keytimes while maintaining full temporal resolution by interpolating measurements to their exact timestamps with a physically founded WNOA prior. This decouples the state size from the dense number of measurements without discarding their asynchronous nature.
  The real-time continuous-time VO pipeline is evaluated on the MVSEC and DSEC datasets. It provides estimates in real time that are more accurate than ES-PTAM, a state-of-the-art discrete estimator, in all but one of the tested sequences. The pipeline respectively provides estimates at 22 Hz and 6 Hz on MVSEC and DSEC and RMS relative errors of 0.46 cm and 0.038 degrees across all valid sequences, which were 11 and 15 times better than ES-PTAM, respectively.

</details>


### [50] [A Passive AI System for Verifying Physical State on Automated Liquid Handlers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02668)
*Junqiong Joanne Qiu,Zeckria Kamrany,Ananya Anand,Emma Vidal,Yunjia Johanna Bai,Nathan S Chen,Sean Son,Varada Abhyankar,Taryn Jakub,Eleazar Eskin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Labware Setup Checker被动视觉系统，用外部摄像头在Opentrons Flex上校验实验台面配置，无需改动机器人硬件或固件，数秒内完成校验并报告缺失、错位或多余耗材。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化液体处理机器人执行数字协议时，操作员仍需人工组装台面并确认物理配置与实验意图一致，缺乏独立、非侵入式的自动核查手段。

Method: 消费级网络摄像头置于机器人外部，浏览器应用接收图像；将台面分割为槽位，利用已知台面几何校正槽位分配，对耗材分类，并与参考协议库比对，输出缺失、错位或异常耗材报告；系统独立于机器人控制器，不中断或门控运行。

Result: SwabSeq RVP pre-PCR台面准备中，追踪某槽位从空到底座再到完整板-底座组件，机器人运动前29帧全部识别出完整组件，348次精确匹配分类中95.1%与协议一致；480帧部分遮挡储液槽场景下6,720次槽位分配全部正确；三种相机位置与两种光照条件下槽位分配100%正确，耗材分类与协议一致率97.73%；笔记本电脑纯CPU中位校验时间2.10 s，AWS实验室部署下从采帧到收到结果4.24 s。

Conclusion: 外部摄像头配合独立软件可在数秒内对照协议要求完成台面配置校验，使操作员能在启动运行前复核物理设置。

Abstract: Automated liquid handlers execute digital protocols, but operators must assemble the deck and confirm that the physical setup matches the intended experiment. We present the Labware Setup Checker, a passive vision system that verifies deck preparation on an Opentrons Flex without modifying its hardware or firmware. A consumer webcam mounted outside the robot supplies images to a browser application. The checker segments the deck into slots, corrects slot assignments using the known deck geometry, classifies the labware, and compares the observed setup with a reference protocol library to report missing, misplaced, or unexpected labware. It operates independently of the robot controller and does not interrupt or gate a run. During SwabSeq Respiratory Viral Panel (RVP) pre-PCR deck preparation, the checker tracked a slot from empty to a base and then to the completed plate-and-base assembly, identifying the completed assembly in all 29 frames before robot motion. Across these frames, 95.1% of the 348 labware classifications for slots requiring exact matches agreed with the protocol reference. In 480 frames with partially occluded reservoirs, all 6,720 slot assignments were correct. Across three camera positions and two lighting conditions, slot assignment was 100% correct, and 97.73% of labware classifications matched the protocol reference. Median verification time was 2.10 s on a laptop using only its CPU and 4.24 s from frame capture to receipt of results during AWS deployment in the laboratory. These results show that an external camera and independent software can provide verification of deck setup against protocol requirements within seconds, allowing operators to review the physical setup before starting a run.

</details>


### [51] [DeltaWorld: Physically Consistent Interactive World Simulators via Action-Conditioned Latent Increment Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02691)
*Boyuan Hou,Xiaoge Cao,Chaofan Zhang,Shuo Wang,Shaowei Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeltaWorld，一种物理一致的交互式世界模拟器，通过预测动作引起的潜在特征变化（Delta-LTM）并对齐交互区域（Interaction-aware Latent Alignment），提升机器人操作中长时域动作条件视频预测的物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模拟器可用于机器人规划、策略训练与评估，减少对反复物理实验的依赖，但需生成忠实响应动作且能长时域保持机器人-物体交互动态的图像序列。现有世界模型通常直接预测整个下一潜在状态，难以捕捉机器人动作引起的细微变化，导致物体相互穿透与过度变形等物理不合理结果。

Method: 提出DeltaWorld物理一致交互式世界模拟器。核心是Delta Latent Transition Model（Delta-LTM），预测动作引起的潜在特征变化量并将其加到当前潜在状态上，而非直接预测下一潜在状态；同时引入Interaction-aware Latent Alignment，通过构建反事实交互区域来监督与交互相关的潜在变化，以缓解预测帧中的物体相互穿透与过度变形。

Result: 在IWS操作基准及自采集的跨机器人数据集（涵盖多种机器人本体与操作任务）上评估。在跨机器人数据集上，DeltaWorld相对IWS基线将FVD降低46.6%，LPIPS降低31.1%。

Conclusion: 结果表明DeltaWorld在机器人操作的长时域动作条件视频预测中具有潜力。

Abstract: Interactive world simulators can provide scalable environments for robot planning, policy training, and evaluation by predicting action consequences while reducing reliance on repeated physical rollouts. To serve these applications, they must generate future image sequences that respond faithfully to robot actions and preserve the dynamics of robot-object interactions over long horizons. However, existing world models typically predict the entire next latent state and often fail to capture subtle changes induced by robot actions. Such omissions can produce physically implausible outcomes, including object interpenetration and excessive deformation. To address this limitation, we propose DeltaWorld, a physically consistent interactive world simulator for robotic manipulation. Our method introduces the Delta Latent Transition Model (Delta-LTM), which predicts action-induced latent feature changes and adds them to the current latent state to obtain the next state, rather than predicting the next latent state directly. To mitigate object interpenetration and excessive deformation in predicted future frames, Interaction-aware Latent Alignment is introduced to construct counterfactual interaction regions and supervise interaction-related latent changes. DeltaWorld is evaluated on the IWS manipulation benchmark and a self-collected cross-robot dataset covering multiple robot embodiments and manipulation tasks. On the cross-robot dataset, DeltaWorld reduces FVD by 46.6% and LPIPS by 31.1% relative to the IWS baseline. These results highlight the potential of DeltaWorld for long-horizon action-conditioned video prediction in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [52] [Who Went Where When on the Lunar Surface: Forensic Trajectory Analysis to Identify Byzantine Rovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02694)
*Lachlan Holden,Feras Dayoub,Melissa de Zwart,David Harvey,Tat-Jun Chin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对非合作行星漫游车的事后轨迹重建，论文提出"取证式轨迹分析"问题：在存在拜占庭漫游车（提供错误或伪造测量）时，标准鲁棒位姿图优化会失效。作者提出基于"漫游车可信度"而非"单次测量有效性"的归因感知估计方法，通过评估可信子集的一致性与边界检测，识别拜占庭节点并显著提升轨迹估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来行星表面任务可能由多台独立运行的漫游车共享同一部署区域，需核查其是否遵守诸如"月球安全区"之类的运行约束。由于难以持续进行原位观测，只能依赖稀疏遥测（里程计、位姿先验、漫游车间相对检测）做事后轨迹重建。而当存在拜占庭漫游车——提供错误标定或蓄意伪造的测量以支持错误轨迹——时，标准离群点鲁棒位姿图优化会被攻破：拜占庭测量可内部自洽且数量充足，反使真实的"指控性"测量被误判为离群点。因此需要一个针对该对抗情形的取证分析框架。

Method: 提出归因感知（attribution-aware）的轨迹估计方法。核心是不判断"单条测量是否有效"，而是推理"哪台漫游车可信"。方法枚举候选的可信漫游车子集，将其内部相对检测与跨边界相对检测的统计一致性同给定先验进行比较，据此给候选子集打分，选出可信集合；随后仅使用归属于可信智能体的测量来估计各漫游车轨迹。

Result: 在合成仿真数据和真实行星类比轨迹数据上，所提方法能够识别出拜占庭漫游车，并且轨迹估计精度显著优于现有的鲁棒位姿图优化基线方法。

Conclusion: 将事后轨迹验证建模为存在拜占庭智能体的取证式估计问题更贴合实际对抗场景；以智能体可信度为归因单元的方法在理论上能避免被自洽且数量占优的伪造测量误导，在实验上验证了其识别与估计优势。

Abstract: Future planetary surface missions are likely to involve multiple independently operated rovers sharing the same deployment region, raising the need to verify compliance with operational constraints such as Lunar Safety Zones. Because continuous in-situ observability is rarely available, such verification requires post-hoc reconstruction of rover trajectories from sparse telemetry, including odometry, pose priors, and relative inter-rover detections. We introduce the problem of forensic trajectory analysis for non-cooperative planetary rovers in the presence of Byzantine agents: rovers that provide miscalibrated or deliberately falsified measurements to support an incorrect trajectory. We show that standard outlier-robust pose graph optimisation methods are vulnerable in this setting, because Byzantine rovers can generate measurements that are internally consistent and numerous enough to make truthful incriminating measurements appear as outliers. To address this, we propose an attribution-aware trajectory estimation method that reasons over rover credibility rather than individual measurement validity. The method evaluates candidate credible rover subsets by comparing the statistical consistency of their internal and boundary relative detections against provided priors, and then estimates trajectories using only measurements attributed to credible agents. Across synthetic simulations and real planetary-analogue trajectory data, the proposed method identifies Byzantine rovers and produces significantly more accurate trajectory estimates than existing robust pose graph optimisation baselines.

</details>


### [53] [GeoScaffold: Learning Compact Geometric Latents via Reconstruction for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02697)
*Yixuan Jiang,Wentong Li,An Liu,Zihao Xin,Fulin Tang,Cong Leng,Yang Gao,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GeoScaffold，一种仅在训练阶段使用几何监督的框架，通过将深度等几何先验内化到策略中，使流式 Video-LLM 导航策略在推理时无需深度传感器或3D编码器即可具备空间感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流式 Video-LLM 的视觉-语言导航（VLN）策略直接从第一视角 RGB 观测与指令映射到低层动作，继承了2D预训练中薄弱的3D几何先验。已有的几何感知扩展方案需要持续的推理代价，如深度传感器、3D编码器或每步感知工具调用，不利于轻量部署。

Method: GeoScaffold 在训练时一次性"付清"几何代价：首先在训练轨迹的深度图上学习一个紧凑的深度分词器（depth tokenizer）并将其冻结；随后用少量可学习的几何查询 token（geometry query tokens）微调策略，训练其隐状态重建导航关键几何（深度、连通性、可通行性）。该监督使查询状态成为用于动作解码的紧凑几何潜变量，并通过共享权重将几何内化到主干表示中。形如脚手架：分词器、目标生成器与重建头在训练后全部丢弃，主干与动作接口保持不变。

Result: 大量实验表明，GeoScaffold 在连续 VLN 基准上持续优于领先的纯视觉导航器，为空间感知具身导航模型的轻量化边缘部署提供了实用范式。

Conclusion: GeoScaffold 通过训练期几何监督将3D几何先验内化到策略与主干表示中，推理阶段保持与纯视觉策略相同的接口与开销，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升连续 VLN 性能，实现轻量边缘部署。

Abstract: Recent vision-and-language navigation (VLN) systems increasingly adopt streaming Video-LLM policies that map egocentric RGB observations and instructions directly to low-level actions. Yet these policies inherit weak 3D geometric priors from 2D pretraining. Existing geometry-aware extensions charge a persistent inference-time price: depth sensors, 3D encoders, or per-step perception tool calls. We propose GeoScaffold, a geometric supervision framework that pays this price once, at training time, by internalizing geometry into the policy itself. It first learns a compact depth tokenizer on depth maps from the training trajectories and freezes it. It then fine-tunes the policy with a handful of learnable geometry query tokens, training their hidden states to reconstruct navigation-critical geometry such as depth, connectivity, and traversability. This supervision turns the query states into compact geometric latents for action decoding, and through the shared weights also internalizes geometry into the backbone's own representations. Like a scaffold, the tokenizer, target generators, and reconstruction heads are discarded after training, leaving the backbone and action interface unchanged. Extensive experiments show that GeoScaffold consistently outperforms leading vision-only navigators on continuous VLN benchmarks, offering a practical paradigm for lightweight edge deployment of spatially aware embodied navigation models.

</details>


### [54] [AdaTempo: Learning Shared Relative Tempo from Demonstrations for Faster Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02706)
*Jiale Cao,Yike Niu,Zhengrong Xue,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaTempo：一种自监督方法，利用示教中的共享相对节奏结构加速视觉运动策略，可将ACT或Diffusion Policy等策略最高加速3.57倍，并获得更优的成功率–速度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习训练出的视觉运动策略常继承遥操作示教中不必要的缓慢节奏；然而统一加速不可靠，因为操作任务的不同阶段对加速的容忍度不同。

Method: AdaTempo建立阶段对应关系，将对齐后的相对节奏聚合为共识，并映射为连续加速曲线，据此对示教重采样生成加速训练轨迹；随后在重采样轨迹上训练ACT、Diffusion Policy等标准策略，将目标节奏直接嵌入所学行为，无需运行时节奏选择或在线重定时。

Result: 大量评估表明，AdaTempo实现最高3.57倍加速，并在成功率与速度的权衡上优于原始策略和代表性加速基线。

Conclusion: AdaTempo通过自监督的相对节奏共识与连续加速曲线重采样，实现阶段自适应加速，并可将节奏直接内化到策略中，优于统一加速方案。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies trained via imitation learning often inherit the unnecessarily slow timing of teleoperated demonstrations. Yet uniform speedup is unreliable because different phases of a manipulation task tolerate acceleration differently. In this work, we introduce AdaTempo, a self-supervised method that accelerates visuomotor policies by exploiting shared relative-tempo structure in demonstrations. AdaTempo establishes phase correspondence, aggregates the aligned relative tempo into a consensus, and maps it to a continuous speedup profile used to resample demonstrations into accelerated training trajectories. Training standard policies such as ACT or Diffusion Policy on these resampled trajectories directly embeds the desired tempo in the learned behavior, without runtime tempo selection or online retiming. Extensive evaluations show that AdaTempo achieves up to a $3.57\times$ speedup and yields a stronger success--speed trade-off than the original policies and representative acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [55] [RoboChemGym: A Protocol-Driven Generative Simulation Framework for Long-Horizon Chemical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02708)
*Chenxi Li,Haiyuan Wan,Rui Li,Jingyuan Li,Sha Zhang,Bohan Feng,Jianbao Cao,Zhangrui Zhao,Di Hu,Wangmeng Zuo,Shixiang Tang,Minting Pan,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboChemGym框架，通过自改进任务合成机制自动生成符合真实实验规范的高保真化学操作演示，并构建分层基准评估具身智能体从原子操作到全流程实验的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿实验是科学发现中假设验证的金标准，但劳动密集、成本高且具有安全风险；具身智能体有望自动化这些流程，却受限于真实世界训练数据稀缺。现有仿真方法多针对短时程、交互结构松散的任务，无法满足化学实验严格的程序约束与精细操作需求。

Method: 提出RoboChemGym框架，自主生成与真实实验协议对齐的高保真操作演示；核心为自改进任务合成（self-improving task synthesis）机制，迭代优化任务执行与场景配置，从而为超过10个交互步骤的复杂多物体协议可靠生成专家轨迹；并设计分层基准，覆盖从原子操作到完整实验流程的不同粒度评估。

Result: 能够可靠生成复杂、多物体、超过10个交互步骤的实验协议专家轨迹，并在分层基准上系统评估具身智能体从原子操作到全周期实验工作流的性能。

Conclusion: RoboChemGym为复杂化学任务中具身智能体的自动化数据合成与能力评估建立了可扩展范式，是迈向全智能实验室的关键一步。

Abstract: Wet-lab experimentation serves as the gold standard for hypothesis verification in scientific discovery; yet it is inherently labor-intensive, costly, and safety-critical. Embodied agents hold the promise of automating these tedious workflows, but their development is hindered by the scarcity of real-world training data. While simulation offers a scalable alternative for producing demonstrations, current methods primarily target relatively short-horizon tasks with loosely structured interactions, failing to meet the strict procedural constraints and fine-grained manipulation demands of chemical experiments. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{RoboChemGym}, a framework that autonomously generates high-fidelity manipulation demonstrations aligned with real-world experiment protocols, featuring a \textit{self-improving task synthesis} mechanism to iteratively refine task execution and scene configurations, enabling the reliable generation of expert trajectories for complex, multi-object protocols exceeding 10 interaction steps. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical benchmark that systematically assesses performance across varying granularities, spanning from atomic operations to full-cycle experimental workflows. RoboChemGym sets a scalable paradigm for the automated data synthesis and capability evaluation of embodied agents in intricate chemical tasks, serving as a critical stepping stone toward fully intelligent laboratories.

</details>


### [56] [RoboBridge: A Self-Evolving Embodied Agent Framework for Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02717)
*Chenxi Li,Zhangrui Zhao,Rui Li,Yuan Gao,Kehui Liu,Jiarui Li,Dong Wang,Tong Si,Minting Pan,Wanli Ouyang,Dongzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboBridge 框架，将仿真到现实的迁移视为可执行任务技能的持续自适应，通过在任务语义与交互接口层面统一表示可迁移技能，实现一次性策略部署到跨环境持续程序化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能落地的核心挑战在于仿真到现实的能力迁移与部署后的持续适应。端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽具备强操作能力，但其迁移通常依赖仿真视觉与动力学条件校准、额外目标域示范采集及再训练优化；而现有工具使用型具身智能体虽强调任务编排与经验复用，却局限于给定环境内的执行，缺乏跨仿真与现实迁移程序化知识并持续适应的支持。

Method: 将任务知识表示为连接任务意图、观测、工具操作与结果验证的程序（procedures）。利用交互反馈生成候选技能修订，经评估后决定持久化或拒绝。将预训练 VLA 策略封装为可复用动作工具，并在推理阶段加入引导，从而在不重训底层策略的前提下实现细粒度执行。可迁移技能建立在仿真与现实共享的任务语义与交互接口之上，保留可复用任务结构，同时允许依赖环境的操作通过真实执行反馈被选择性修订。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 及对应物理任务上评估了该框架，分别研究了技能演化与迁移后适应两方面表现。

Conclusion: RoboBridge 提供了一条从一次性策略部署通向跨环境持续程序化学习的路径。

Abstract: A key challenge in bringing embodied intelligence into the real world is transferring capabilities from simulation to reality and enabling agents to continually adapt after deployment. End-to-end vision-language-action policies provide strong manipulation capabilities, but their transfer to physical environments typically relies on calibrating simulated visual and dynamical conditions, collecting additional target-domain demonstrations, and optimizing the policy through further training. Tool-using embodied agents offer flexible task orchestration, yet existing systems primarily emphasize task execution and experience reuse within a given environment, with limited support for transferring procedural knowledge and continuously adapting it across simulation and reality. We propose RoboBridge, a framework that treats sim-to-real transfer as the continued adaptation of executable task skills. The agent represents task knowledge as procedures connecting task intent, observations, tool operations, and outcome verification. Interaction feedback is used to generate candidate skill revisions, which are evaluated before being persisted or rejected. A pretrained vision-language-action policy is exposed as a reusable action tool and enhanced with inference-time guidance, enabling fine-grained execution without retraining the underlying policy. RoboBridge grounds transferable skills in task semantics and interaction interfaces shared across simulation and reality. This representation preserves reusable task structure while allowing environment-dependent operations to be selectively revised through real-world execution feedback. We evaluate the framework on LIBERO-PRO and corresponding physical tasks, studying both skill evolution and post-transfer adaptation. Our framework provides a route from one-shot policy deployment to continual procedural learning across environments.

</details>


### [57] [Around the World: Unified Learned Locomotion on a 270 g Continuous-Rotation Quadruped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02728)
*Arturo Flores Alvarez,Nathan Lintu,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MiNI-Q（270 g四足机器人），利用腿部连续旋转特性（T⁸环面参考+重力条件变换），以单一姿态条件化强化学习策略实现正立/倒立行走与翻身恢复，无需状态机；仅<10万参数，正立/倒立速度跟踪RMSE为0.037/0.050 m/s，30次释放试验中24次恢复行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环学习式运动控制在商用四足机器人上已成熟，但在亚公斤级尺度仍不常见；同时希望借助连续旋转腿部带来的双侧支撑构型能力，用统一策略覆盖正立、倒立与恢复等多种姿态，避免依赖状态机或相位切换。

Method: 以270 g四足机器人MiNI-Q为平台，采用单体姿态条件化强化学习策略并完全机载运行。在环面T⁸上建立连续关节空间参考，并经重力条件变换统一连接正立行走、倒立行走与落地恢复；训练上使用姿态与释放（release）协同课程；并配合执行器辨识、跨引擎验证与嵌入式部署以支撑硬件迁移。

Result: 同一<100k参数网络实现前向速度跟踪：正立RMSE 0.037 m/s、倒立0.050 m/s；30次释放试验中24次恢复行走；在四种可互换足部几何与四种室内地面上均能运行。硬件实验与仿真消融将上述能力归因于对平台运动范围加以利用的表示、条件化与训练设计。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过在环面关节参考与重力条件化下训练单一策略，可在亚公斤级连续旋转腿四足平台上以极低参数量实现多姿态运动与恢复，验证了表示、条件化和课程训练设计对利用平台运动范围的有效性。
