Abstract: Closed-loop learned locomotion is established on commercial quadrupeds but remains uncommon at the sub-kilogram scale. Continuous-rotation legs give MiNI-Q, a 270 g quadruped, access to supporting configurations on either side of the body. We exploit this range with a single posture-conditioned reinforcement-learning policy that runs entirely onboard. A continuous joint-space reference on the torus $T^8$ and its gravity-conditioned transformation connect upright walking, inverted walking, and landing recovery without state machines or phase switching. Coordinated posture and release curricula train this behavior family; identified actuation, cross-engine validation, and embedded execution support hardware transfer. The same sub-100k-parameter network tracks forward velocity with RMSE of 0.037 m/s upright and 0.050 m/s inverted, resumes walking in 24 of 30 release trials, and operates with four interchangeable foot geometries across four indoor surfaces. Hardware experiments and simulation ablations connect these capabilities to the representation, conditioning, and training choices that exploit the platform's motion range. Demonstration videos and supplementary material are available on the project website: https://submissionreview.github.io/around-the-world/.

</details>


### [58] [CSIR: Contextually and Socially Informed Robots for Efficient Person Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02750)
*Tyler Chung,Nahl Farhan,Hao Zhang,Mingfeng Yuan,Jinjun Shan,Amy Wu,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonNav（人物目标导航）问题，并给出一个融合距离与语义信息、按用户信息信任度加权的规划框架，配合合成基准与硬件验证，性能优于经典距离基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有找人系统依赖对目标个体的精确知识，且受隐私限制难以获取公开数据；需要在信息可及性受限、语言不确定的现实条件下实现机器人搜索人。

Method: 提出规划框架，结合关于被寻找者的距离信息与语义信息（习惯与意图），并以用户所提供信息的信任度进行加权；构建包含角色、物品与请求的合成基准以模拟自然语言人机交互；并设置LLM变体与显式信念表示（便于未来贝叶斯滤波）。

Result: 知情搜索优于经典基于距离的图基线；仅使用语义会导致无根据、零散的搜索；本方法相对基线取得显著提升，LLM变体表现相当；硬件测试表明方法可支持真实环境具身，能利用语义与距离信息。

Conclusion: 该工作朝更智能的移动服务智能体迈进，使其能在现实环境中进行类人的知情搜索，以用于日常使用。

Abstract: We address the Person Goal Navigation (PersonNav) problem, enabling robots to search for people under realistic constraints on information accessibility and language uncertainty. This tackles the current solutions revolving around rigid person finding systems requiring exact knowledge of the individual for item-delivery in indoor settings. While the focus in literature is on learned methods using unavailable public or sparse data due to human privacy. We propose a planning framework that combines distance and semantic information about the recipient (habits and intent) weighted by the trust of the user-provided information. A synthetic benchmark of scenarios is also developed including actors, items, and requests to evaluate performance before real-world deployment aiming to simulate natural language human-robot interactions. Results show our informed search outperforms classical distance-based graph baselines, while semantics alone lead to ungrounded, sporadic search. Our method achieves strong gains over baselines, with an LLM-based variant performing comparably, and its explicit belief representation naturally supports future Bayesian filtering. Hardware tests demonstrate our method supports real-world embodiment able to leverage between semantic and distance information in a real setting. This work moves toward more intelligent mobile service agents capable of human-like, informed search in realistic environments to be leveraged in day-to-day use. https://anonymous.4open.science/r/personnavsite-4ED2/index.html.

</details>


### [59] [Proprioceptive Sketches as Long-Horizon Intent for Generative Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02759)
*Fangyuan Wang,Songhao Huang,Haoxiang Sun,Shipeng Lyu,Chengyang He,Anqing Duan,Peng Zhou,David Navarro-Alarcon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出本体感受动作模型（PAM），在单一Transformer去噪器中联合生成无时间索引的关节空间路径草图与可执行动作块，以低成本提供长时程几何意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式机器人策略仅预测短动作块，缺乏显式长时程意图；语言计划、子目标图像或视频预测等方案生成成本高且仍需转换为机器人运动；而直接预测未来运动虽有优势，但稠密且按时间索引的轨迹参数多，短时程内近似重复动作块，指导价值有限。

Method: PAM在单个Transformer去噪器中同时生成剩余关节空间路径的紧凑无时间草图与稠密可执行动作块。草图按弧长而非时间参数化，捕捉与执行速度无关的几何意图。采用块因果注意力与交错去噪调度，维持有向的“草图到动作”依赖，使动作token在整个采样过程中条件依赖于逐步更清晰的草图。

Result: 在仿真环境中，PAM在Push-T与LIBERO-Long上优于仅动作的对应基线；在四个真实世界双臂任务中，成功率从47.5%提升至75.0%。

Conclusion: 通过以弧长参数化的无时间路径草图作为长时程意图表示，并与动作块在单模型内联合去噪，可在不引入高昂模态转换成本的前提下显著提升长时程机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Generative robot policies predict short action chunks but lack explicit long-horizon intent. Recent methods expose longer-horizon structure through language plans, subgoal images, or video forecasts, which are costly to generate and still need to be translated into robot motion. Predicting future robot motions avoids this translation, but a dense, time-indexed trajectory requires numerous parameters to cover the full remaining task, and over a short horizon it largely repeats the action chunk and adds little guidance for action generation. We propose Proprioceptive Action Models (PAM), which jointly generate a compact, timing-free sketch of the robot's remaining joint-space path and a dense executable action chunk within a single transformer denoiser. The sketch parameterizes the path by arc length rather than time, capturing geometric intent invariant to execution timing. Block-causal attention and a staggered denoising schedule maintain directed sketch-to-action dependence, ensuring the action tokens condition on a progressively cleaner sketch throughout sampling. In simulation, PAM improves over its action-only counterparts on Push-T and LIBERO-Long; on four real-world bimanual tasks, it raises success from 47.5% to 75.0%. Project page: https://nicehiro.github.io/pam_dp/

</details>


### [60] [Localized Conformal Safety Monitoring with Vision-Language Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02765)
*Luís Marques,Rong Fang,Disha Kamale,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SLLCP，一种在冻结VLM之上的事后校准层，将不可靠预测转为概率校准的安全预测集，显著提升碰撞轨迹识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测规划驾驶轨迹需准确估计与受自车运动影响的其他交通参与者的碰撞可能性。经典方法受限于预测模型质量；VLM虽擅长高层动作后果推理，但其近似预测不适合自动驾驶等安全关键场景。因此需要一种能对黑盒模型预测进行不确定性量化的数据驱动方法。

Method: 提出Split Label-Localized Conformal Prediction (SLLCP)，作为冻结VLM之上的事后校准层。考虑安全估计能力依赖于所观测驾驶场景，引入局部化(localized)过程，在计算不确定性阈值时对相关历史经验加权。并在可交换性假设下提供标签条件(label-conditional)的有限样本、无分布覆盖保证。

Result: 在CARLA的15k条来自未见场景的轨迹上评估：SLLCP在Qwen骨干下正确标记89.6%的碰撞致因轨迹，Cosmos骨干下为88.4%；而基础VLM仅分别标记了4.6%和39.1%。

Conclusion: 局部、标签条件的校准能够减少对不安全轨迹的漏报，表明SLLCP可有效将VLM的不可靠预测转化为概率校准的安全预测集。

Abstract: Monitoring planned driving trajectories requires accurately estimating the collision likelihood with actors whose motion is itself impacted by the ego motion. Existing classical approaches are often limited by the quality of their forecasting model. Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promise in reasoning about the consequences of high-level actions, yet their approximate predictions are unsuitable for safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. Conformal prediction (CP) has emerged as a data-driven framework for quantifying the uncertainty of black-box model predictions. We propose Split Label-Localized Conformal Prediction (SLLCP), a post-hoc calibration layer over frozen VLMs that transforms their unreliable predictions into probabilistically calibrated safety prediction sets. We consider how the ability to estimate safety can depend on the observed driving scene and introduce a localized procedure that upweights relevant past experience when calculating uncertainty thresholds. We provide label-conditional finite-sample distribution-free coverage under exchangeability. Evaluated over 15k CARLA trajectories from unseen scenarios, SLLCP correctly flags 89.6% of collision-causing trajectories with a Qwen backbone and 88.4% with a Cosmos backbone, while the base VLMs only flagged 4.6% and 39.1% of the collision-causing trajectories, respectively. These results indicate that local, label-conditional calibration can reduce missed unsafe trajectories.

</details>


### [61] [SimpleTouch: Can Vision-Language-Action Models Master Contact-Rich Manipulation Without Tactile Policy Pretraining?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02784)
*Chen Yang,Linzhe Shi,Changjie Wu,Hang Zhang,Ronghan Chen,Lingjun Zhang,Xu Hu,Mu Xu,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SimpleTouch：在冻结的预训练触觉编码器与 π_{0.5} VLA 基础上，仅用任务演示进行单阶段训练，无需大规模触觉策略预训练或独立视触觉对齐，即在 UniVTAC 六个任务和四个真实任务上取得最优成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉为机器人操作提供关键接触信息，但将其融入预训练 VLA 模型存在困难。常见担忧是仅在任务微调阶段引入触觉无法弥合跨模态鸿沟，收益有限甚至降低成功率。因此现有方法多依赖大规模触觉策略预训练或单独的视触觉对齐，带来额外数据和训练阶段开销。本文意在检验这些额外阶段是否必要。

Method: 将 π_{0.5} 扩展出一个触觉专家（tactile expert）。使用冻结的预训练触觉编码器的全部 token，专家通过动作监督与多步未来触觉隐变量预测进行学习。整个训练为单阶段，仅使用任务演示数据，不进行额外触觉策略预训练，也不做单独对齐。

Result: 每任务 50 条演示下，在全部六个 UniVTAC 任务上成功率最优，平均 77.5%，高于 FTP-π_{0.5} 的 45.2% 与 FTP-1 的 66.7%，分别提升 32.3 与 10.8 个百分点；在四个真实任务上平均 71.3%，比 FTP-1 高 8.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 在已有预训练 VLA 与触觉表示的前提下，额外的触觉策略预训练并非取得强性能的前提条件，为接触丰富型操作提供了更简洁的路径。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides essential contact information for robotic manipulation, yet incorporating it into pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging. A common concern is that simply introducing touch during task-specific fine-tuning may fail to bridge the cross-modal gap, yielding limited gains or even reduced success. Consequently, existing methods often rely on large-scale tactile policy pretraining or separate visuotactile alignment, adding data requirements and training stages. We introduce SimpleTouch, a simple VLA extension that augments $π_{0.5}$ with a tactile expert, to test whether these additional stages are necessary. Leveraging all tokens from a frozen pretrained tactile encoder, the expert learns from action supervision and multi-horizon prediction of future tactile latents. This single-stage training uses only task demonstrations, without additional tactile policy pretraining or separate alignment. With 50 demonstrations per task, SimpleTouch achieves the highest success rate among evaluated methods on all six UniVTAC tasks. Its average success rate reaches 77.5%, compared with 45.2% for FTP-$π_{0.5}$ and 66.7% for FTP-1, corresponding to gains of 32.3 and 10.8 percentage points, respectively. Across four real-world tasks, it averages 71.3%, exceeding FTP-1 by 8.8 percentage points. These results demonstrate that, given pretrained VLA and tactile representations, additional tactile policy pretraining is not a prerequisite for strong performance on these tasks, offering a simpler route to contact-rich manipulation. Project page: https://simpletouch-robot.github.io/

</details>


### [62] [Skill2Real: Agentic Skill Learning for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02788)
*Xincheng He,Siyu Ma,Chang Yu,Yunuo Chen,Yanjia Huang,Ying Nian Wu,Yin Yang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Skill2Real：通过共享API与Proposer-Verifier-Governor循环学习可执行技能层级，实现零微调的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人技能从仿真迁移到现实，需要能在感知、动力学与本体差异下依然可用的任务知识。核心难点在于所学技能往往与特定仿真条件耦合，难以直接迁移。

Method: 提出Skill2Real智能体策略框架：(1) 以共享API为技能接口，使技能建立在公开观测与API语义之上；(2) Proposer-Verifier-Governor (PVG) 循环，利用特权仿真证据诊断结果并验证更新；(3) 分层学习：Cerebellum先习得局部操作技能，随后冻结Cerebellum由Brain学习任务级组合；两段记忆均可直接迁移至真机。

Result: LIBERO-90上训练、冻结checkpoint由Astra评估，LIBERO-Pro Long成功率由2.0%升至56.3%（未在Pro Long上训练）；Robosuite七任务上Sol与Opus 5平均成功率分别为85.1%与89.4%；冻结的Sol-LIBERO-90技能在四个真实操作任务上平均完成率78.75%；去掉Verifier或Governor分别使最终Pro Long成功率下降17.3与13.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，借助统一机器人接口可以学习并迁移可执行技能层级；PVG中的验证与调控组件对技能质量与迁移性能均有显著贡献，且迁移无需任务策略微调或技能记忆更新。

Abstract: Transferring robotic skills from simulation to reality requires task knowledge that remains usable across differences in perception, dynamics, and embodiment. We introduce Skill2Real, an agentic policy framework that learns executable skills through a shared application programming interface (API). A Proposer-Verifier-Governor (PVG) loop uses privileged simulation evidence to diagnose outcomes and validate updates, while keeping learned skills grounded in public observations and API semantics. The Cerebellum first acquires local manipulation skills; the Brain then learns task-level composition with the Cerebellum frozen. Both memories transfer to the real robot without task-policy fine-tuning or skill-memory updates. As GPT-5.6 Sol learns skills on LIBERO-90, evaluating each frozen checkpoint with GPT-6 Astra raises LIBERO-Pro Long success from 2.0% to 56.3%, without training on Pro Long. Independent Robosuite training reaches 85.1% and 89.4% mean success with Sol and Opus 5 across seven tasks, respectively. Frozen Sol-trained LIBERO-90 skills achieve 78.75% mean completion across four real-world manipulation tasks with Astra. Removing the Verifier or Governor during LIBERO-90 training lowers final Pro Long success by 17.3 and 13.3 percentage points, respectively. These results support learning and transferring a hierarchy of executable skills through a common robot interface.

</details>


### [63] [ManiPhysicsBench: Physics-Based Assessment of Object Preservation in VLA Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02802)
*Sangwu Park,Yeonjun In,Wonjoong Kim,Sungwon Kim,Sein Kim,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出ManiPhysicsZoo与ManiPhysicsBench，用求解器评估VLA模型在操作任务中是否保持物体不被损坏，发现现有模型任务成功率与“安全成功率”存在显著差距，并尝试用物体特定的连续夹爪标签改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有刚体操作基准仅衡量任务是否完成，无法反映VLA模型是否在操作过程中保持物体完整（不产生形变或断裂）。因此需要一个融合材料属性与物理求解器的评估框架来量化“物体保持”能力。

Method: 1) 构建ManiPhysicsZoo：整合文献支持的材料属性、3D网格与参考文献，形成可复用物体资产。2) 基于几何、材料属性与记录的抓取条件，用求解器计算抓取特定的损伤阈值，并与记录的接触力比较以评估潜在形变与断裂。3) 在此基础上构建ManiPhysicsBench，在LIBERO与SimplerEnv上沿三个物理维度和三个难度等级评估物体保持。4) 用物体特定的连续夹爪标签重新训练一个VLA模型，观察行为变化。

Result: 公开VLA检查点显示出任务成功率与安全成功率（在保持物体的前提下完成任务）之间的显著差距。其夹爪命令集中在接近全开与全闭处，且不同物体间总体分布大致相似，与二值夹爪监督一致。重训练后的模型表现出更强的物体相关抓取与更高的安全成功率，但任务成功率下降，且物体保持行为的泛化能力有限。

Conclusion: 任务成功并不等同于安全操作，物体保持是现有VLA模型被忽视但重要的能力维度。物体特定的连续夹爪监督能部分改善安全成功率，但会牺牲任务成功率与泛化性，说明该方向仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models aim to perform diverse manipulation tasks, but task success in existing rigid-body benchmarks does not indicate whether they preserve objects. We introduce ManiPhysicsZoo, which consolidates literature-supported material properties, 3D meshes, and supporting references into reusable object assets. Using these assets, a solver-based assessment computes grasp-specific damage thresholds from object geometry, material properties, and recorded grasp conditions and compares them with recorded contact forces to assess potential deformation and fracture. Building on these components, ManiPhysicsBench evaluates object preservation in LIBERO and SimplerEnv across three physics axes and three difficulty levels. Public VLA checkpoints show a substantial gap between task success and safe success, defined as task completion while preserving the object. Their gripper commands concentrate near full opening and closure, with largely similar aggregate distributions across objects, consistent with binary gripper supervision. We examine how object-specific continuous gripper labels change model behavior by retraining a VLA model. The retrained model shows more object-dependent gripping and higher safe success, but lower task success and limited generalization of object-preserving behavior.

</details>


### [64] [LOCUS: Landmark-Oriented Container Discrimination Using Spatial Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02803)
*Taylor Bergeron,Shibani Senthilbabu,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LOCUS方法，通过场景图上的GNN更新CLIP嵌入并结合家居本体语义，实现空间与语义联合推理，用于物体搜索与容器判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化真实环境中操作与导航时，需推理物体间复杂的空间与语义关系。例如，判断不在视野内的物体应去哪里搜索，取决于其物理合理性与语义可能性。而余弦相似度结合CLIP等流行方法难以区分空间和语义上相似、都可能容纳目标物体的容器。

Method: LOCUS（Landmark-Oriented Container Discrimination Using Spatial Graphs）：在完整环境场景图上训练GNN，依据邻近性在节点间传递嵌入信息，从而更新CLIP嵌入；同时将CLIP嵌入与家居本体融合以提供稳健的语义信号，实现空间信息与语义的联合推理。

Result: 在仿真中使用来自模拟器元数据的无噪声场景图进行评估（使方法检测无关）。在多数房间类别与配置下，该方法优于随机策略、CLIP、Tidybot及LLM规划器。为验证对场景图节点位置与标签噪声的鲁棒性，展示了物理移动操作机器人在完整探索流程中的运行，包括使用Detic生成的场景图标签。

Conclusion: LOCUS通过空间图上的GNN嵌入更新与本体语义融合，有效实现容器判别与物体搜索，在仿真中超越多种基线，并在真实机器人探索流程中展现出对节点放置和标签噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in unstructured, real-world environments, the ability to reason about complex spatial and semantic relationships among objects remains a fundamental challenge in enabling robust and generalizable manipulation and navigation. For example, deciding where to search for an object that is not in plain sight depends on where it is physically plausible as well as semantically likely. Popular methods such as cosine similarity with CLIP struggle to disambiguate between spatially and semantically similar objects that could contain a target object. We propose Landmark-Oriented Container Discrimination Using Spatial Graphs (LOCUS). To jointly reason about spatial information and semantics, we train a GNN to update CLIP embeddings on a full environment scene graph by passing embedding information between nodes based on proximity. CLIP embeddings, augmented by semantic knowledge from a fusion of household ontologies provide a robust semantic signal. We evaluate in simulation on a noiseless scene graph from simulator metadata, making the approach detection-agnostic. Our approach outperforms random, CLIP, Tidybot, and an LLM planner in the majority of room classes and configurations in simulation. To show our approach is robust to scene graph node placement and label noise, we demonstrate a physical mobile manipulator running in an exploration pipeline start-to-finish including scene graph labels from Detic.

</details>


### [65] [SARI: Phase-Split Sim-Real Co-Training for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02804)
*Xingxin He,Yuxuan Jiang,Haonan Zhang,Chuhan Cui,Kaile Li,Zhongxing Zheng,Caihao Xu,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SARI：分阶段仿真-真机协同训练框架，用仿真生成多样化接近轨迹、用真机少量演示采集接触交互，单策略无缝拼接，减少真实数据采集时间34.3%，未见位姿成功率27.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型适配接触密集操作任务需大量真机演示，尤其跨物体摆放位置泛化时成本高昂。核心洞察：空间覆盖与接触物理应由各自最擅长的域提供——自由空间接近需要空间多样性但对仿真误差宽容，适合合成；接触交互需精确物理但跨摆放变化小，少量真机演示即可泛化。

Method: SARI（Simulated Approach, Real Interaction）：在逼真数字孪生中生成多样的仿真接近阶段；仅在少量摆放位置采集真机接触交互。基于分阶段演示做后训练，通过视觉外观对齐与共享的相机相对动作表示，使单一策略无缝拼接仿真接近与真机接触，无需显式阶段标签或手工切换逻辑。

Result: 在5个真机接触密集操作任务上，真实数据采集时间减少34.3%；在未见摆放位置达到27.5%成功率，而所有全任务仿真-真机基线均为0%。

Conclusion: 按阶段拆分数据来源的仿真-真机协同训练可显著降低真机数据成本，并实现跨摆放位置的泛化，说明将空间覆盖与接触物理分别交由最适配的域获取是有效策略。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often require costly real-world demonstrations to adapt to contact-rich manipulation tasks, particularly when generalization across object placements is needed. We propose SARI (Simulated Approach, Real Interaction), a phase-split sim-and-real co-training framework built on a simple insight: spatial coverage and contact physics should be acquired from the domains best suited to them. Specifically, free-space approaches require spatial diversity but tolerate modest simulation gaps, making them ideal for synthetic generation; conversely, contact interactions demand accurate physics but vary little across object placements, allowing a few real demonstrations to generalize across the workspace. SARI generates diverse simulated approaches in a photorealistic digital twin while collecting real contact interactions at only a few placements. Post-trained on these phase-segmented demonstrations, a single policy seamlessly stitches simulated approaches with real contact interactions using visual appearance alignment and a shared camera-relative action representation--without explicit phase labels or hand-coded switches. Across five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, SARI reduces real-data collection time by 34.3% and achieves 27.5% success at unseen placements, where all full-task sim-real baselines fail completely (0%).

</details>


### [66] [Learning Reflexive Behavior for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02811)
*Quan Nguyen,Yunho Kim,Joonho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于本体感觉的反射策略（proprioceptive reflex policy），在仿真中于弹簧、平面、导轨三个交互原语上训练，将任务空间指令映射为关节位置目标，作为高层控制器的冻结执行层，在双臂举箱、轴孔装配和曲面跟随任务中显著提升接触安全性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作中，机器人与环境的交互蕴含局部几何信息（如表面防穿透、孔引导轴）。传统的位置控制、力位混合控制或笛卡尔阻抗控制使用预设的跟踪目标、刚度或力控方向，可能与环境局部约束不匹配，从而降低性能。因此需要一种能利用交互信息、顺应环境约束同时保持任务意图的低层控制方式。

Method: 在仿真中针对三种简单交互原语（弹簧、平面、导轨）学习一个本体感觉反射策略。该策略以状态历史为输入，将任务空间命令映射为关节位置目标，不直接使用力或几何测量。训练完成后，作为固定（冻结）的执行层置于高层控制器之下，供运动规划器、学习策略和遥操作使用。

Result: 在双臂举箱中，反射策略将力保持在阈值以下，而基线失败；在粗糙曲面跟随中，接触力保持在10 N参考值以下，与调优后的力位混合控制相当；在0.02 mm轴孔装配中，估计接触力均值降至基线的一半以下，硬件成功率从最高22%提升至36–58%。

Conclusion: 将接触响应与指令生成分离，反射策略为运动规划器、学习策略和遥操作提供了一种鲁棒的接触丰富执行方式，能有效顺应环境约束并保持任务意图。

Abstract: During contact-rich manipulation, interactions between a robot and its environment carry information about local geometry: a surface prevents penetration, a bore guides a peg. A controller that exploits these interactions can comply with environmental constraints while preserving task intent. Robot-earning systems commonly use position, hybrid force-position, or Cartesian impedance control. Their prescribed tracking objectives, stiffness, or force-control directions may not match local constraints and may degrade performance. We learn a proprioceptive reflex policy in simulation on three simple interaction primitives: a spring, a plane, and a rail. The policy maps task-space commands to joint-position targets using state history, without direct force or geometrical measurements. Once trained, it serves as a frozen execution layer beneath higher-level controllers. We evaluate it in dual-arm box lifting, peg insertion, and surface following. In box lifting, the reflex kept the force below the threshold while the baseline failed. In rough-surface following it stayed below the 10 N reference, on par with tuned hybrid force-position control. In 0.02 mm peg insertion It reduced mean estimated contact force to less than half that of the baseline while increasing hardware success rates from at most 22 % to 36-58 %. By separating contact response from command generation, the reflex policy provides motion planners, learned policies, and teleoperators with robust contact-rich execution.

</details>


### [67] [Register-Routed Delayed Fusion: Rewiring Shortcut-Prone Observation Fusion in Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02813)
*Jieting Long,Weidong Cai,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Register-Routed Delayed Fusion (RRDF)，通过屏蔽紧凑模态对视觉的早期直接注意力、改由学习到的 register 工作区分阶段路由跨模态交互，在保持紧凑动作条件的同时提升视觉模仿策略的响应性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动模仿策略需融合高维视觉观测与本体感觉等紧凑信号，其融合拓扑决定了两者的交互时机与 token 路径。在密集 token 融合中，视觉 token 从编码器第一层起就能直接注意紧凑 token，使动作预测性紧凑线索过早影响空间视觉表征的形成。作者希望探究控制这一路径是否能改善视觉响应性与策略行为。

Method: 提出 RRDF，掩蔽紧凑-视觉的直接注意力，并通过学习到的 register 工作区分阶段进行跨模态交互；采用 isolate-collect-route 调度：先保护早期流分离前缀，随后仅允许 register 介导的交换，同时紧凑条件仍可作用于原生动作生成器。评估涵盖五个仿真任务与三个真机任务，并做外观偏移、留出位置测试、相位匹配输入探针与消融实验。

Result: 在五个仿真和三个真机任务中，RRDF 在标称条件下达到或优于密集 ACT；四个仿真任务的外观偏移评估与三个真机任务的留出位置评估同样倾向 RRDF。相位匹配输入探针显示其状态到图像的敏感度更低；消融表明仅加入 register 无法复现完整性能增益。

Conclusion: 结果支持在保留紧凑动作条件的同时对跨模态传播加以控制，说明融合拓扑的设计（而非单纯的容量增加）是提升视觉模仿策略响应性与泛化性的关键。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation policies combine high-dimensional visual observations with compact signals such as proprioception, and their fusion topology determines when and through which tokens these streams interact. In dense token fusion, visual tokens may attend directly to compact tokens from the first encoder layer, allowing action-predictive compact cues to influence spatial visual representations early in their formation. We ask whether controlling this route improves visual responsiveness and policy behavior. We introduce Register-Routed Delayed Fusion (RRDF), which masks direct compact-visual attention and stages cross-modal interaction through a learned register workspace. Its isolate-collect-route schedule protects an early stream-separated prefix and later permits only register-mediated exchange, while compact conditioning remains available to the native action generator. Across five simulation tasks and three real-robot tasks, RRDF matches or improves dense ACT under nominal conditions. Appearance-shift evaluations on four simulation tasks and held-out-position evaluations on three real-robot tasks also favor RRDF. Phase-matched input probes show lower measured state-to-image sensitivity, while ablations indicate that adding registers alone does not reproduce the full performance gain. These results support controlling cross-modal propagation while retaining compact action conditioning.

</details>


### [68] [FastOPD: On-Policy Distillation for Lightweight VLA Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02832)
*Yoojin Oh,Jeongsol Kim,Yeonwoo Seo,Jangho Park,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Youngmin Kim,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastOPD：一种"基础到轻量"的VLA框架，通过高效在线策略蒸馏实现大规模VLA的实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA基础模型规模快速扩张以提升操作性能与泛化性，但计算成本高昂，导致真实部署困难。现有方法多通过缩小架构或减少基于流的策略迭代去噪步数来缓解，但效果有限。

Method: 提出FastOPD框架：(1) 为单状态教师监督适配flow map；(2) 结合自一致性（self-consistency）目标，构建学习教师动态的紧凑学生模型；(3) 理论证明最小化该目标可使蒸馏学生恢复与理想少步教师模型相当的分布。

Result: 在LIBERO上，仅用两步推理即保留π_{0.5}的84%性能，推理延迟降低78.1%，平均成功率优于现有少步蒸馏基线；以LingBot-VLA为教师，在RoboTwin 2.0上单步成功率较基础学生提升15.9个百分点；此外验证了其对世界动作模型（WAM）的适用性，并在真机上部署了从MolmoAct2蒸馏的紧凑学生模型。

Conclusion: FastOPD通过在线策略蒸馏有效平衡了VLA基础模型的性能与部署效率，在仿真与真机实验中均验证了其通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have scaled rapidly to enhance manipulation performance and generalizability, but this scaling incurs high computational costs that render real-world deployment increasingly challenging. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by designing smaller architectures or reducing the iterative denoising steps in flow-based policies. In this work, we propose FastOPD, a foundation-to-lightweight VLA framework that enables the practical deployment of large-scale VLAs through efficient on-policy distillation. Specifically, FastOPD adapts a flow map for single-state teacher supervision and combines it with a self-consistency objective to construct a compact student that learns the teacher dynamics. Furthermore, we theoretically demonstrate that minimizing this objective allows the distilled student to recover a distribution on par with that induced by an ideal few-step teacher model. We evaluate FastOPD across diverse foundation policies in simulation and real-world experiments. On LIBERO, FastOPD retains 84% of the performance of $π_{0.5}$ with only two inference steps, reducing inference latency by 78.1% while outperforming existing few-step distillation baselines in average success rate. With LingBot-VLA as the teacher, FastOPD improves the single-step success rate over the base student by 15.9 percentage points on RoboTwin 2.0. We further demonstrate its applicability to a World Action Model (WAM) and deploy a compact student distilled from MolmoAct2 on a real robot.

</details>


### [69] [PointWAM: 3D World Action Modeling for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02840)
*Chunghyun Park,Beomjun Kim,Seungcheol Park,Heeseung Kwon,Yashu Shukla,Seunghoon Sim,Jinwoo Shin,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Point World Action Model（PointWAM），一种将世界分解为场景与手部并在共享时空坐标系中以 3D 点轨迹联合预测的 3D 世界动作模型，在 DexJoCo 与真机任务上显著超越先前方法与强 VLA 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型多以 RGB 帧或隐空间表示世界、以末端位姿或关节角预测动作，难以刻画灵巧操作所依赖的 3D 空间结构与接触几何；同时缺乏无需任务特定物体/关键点标注的大规模人类视频预训练范式。

Method: PointWAM 将世界显式解耦为场景（环境）与手部（执行者），在统一时空坐标系内以 3D 点轨迹联合预测二者的协同演化。输入为彩色点云与语言指令，模型预测场景与手部在 3D 空间中的时序演化，并将预测的手部运动重定向为机器人动作。该解耦表示为大规模人类演示视频的预训练提供了可能，且无需任何任务特定的物体或关键点选取。

Result: 在人类视频上预训练使 DexJoCo 平均成功率提升 56.9 个百分点；场景轨迹监督相较仅预测手部再提升 10.9 个百分点。两者结合后，PointWAM 在十个 DexJoCo 任务上超越先前最优方法 11.7 个百分点，并在真机上优于强 VLA 基线。

Conclusion: 显式解耦的场景—手部 3D 点轨迹表示与人类视频预训练可有效提升灵巧操作策略的空间与接触建模能力，验证了 3D 世界动作模型作为机器人操作学习范式的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: World action models jointly learn to forecast world dynamics and predict robot actions, such that the learned internal world dynamics guide accurate actions. Existing approaches typically represent the world as RGB frames or latent counterparts while predicting actions as end-effector poses or joint angles, but they often struggle to capture the 3D spatial structure and contact geometry central to dexterous manipulation. We introduce Point World Action Model (PointWAM), a 3D world action model that decomposes the world into a scene (i.e., environment) and hands (i.e., actor), and jointly forecasts both as 3D point trajectories within a shared space-time coordinate frame. This explicit, disentangled representation enables effective pre-training on large-scale human demonstration videos without requiring any task-specific object or keypoint selection. Given a colored point cloud and a language instruction, PointWAM predicts how the scene and hands co-evolve in 3D space over time, then retargets the forecast hand motion to robot actions. Pre-training on human videos improves average DexJoCo success by 56.9 percentage points, and scene-trajectory supervision adds 10.9 points over forecasting the hands alone. With both, PointWAM surpasses the prior state of the art on ten DexJoCo tasks by 11.7 points and outperforms strong VLAs on a real robot.

</details>


### [70] [Permutation Robustness Is Not Enough: Action Collapse in Multi-Agent Transformer Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02848)
*Amit Thakur,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究考察多智能体Transformer策略在智能体顺序置换下的合作导航表现，指出仅用置换误差评估可能产生误导，需结合动作塌缩诊断指标，并发现最优等变正则强度随团队规模变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体团队本质上无序，而Transformer按有序token序列处理智能体，这种不匹配会影响合作导航策略。现有评估可能因"所有智能体选择相同动作"而虚高置换鲁棒性。

Method: 在智能体顺序置换条件下评估合作导航策略，同时使用两类指标：置换一致性指标与动作塌缩诊断（动作多样性、同动作比例、最大动作频率）；对比PPO-ID基线、强等变正则与弱等变惩罚，并测试N=3与N=4团队。

Result: PPO-ID基线行为未塌缩但对顺序敏感；强等变正则会诱发同质化行为；弱等变惩罚在N=3时能提升鲁棒性并保持动作多样性，而N=4需要显著更小的正则权重。

Conclusion: 多智能体Transformer策略的评估不应只看回报与置换鲁棒性，还应考察其是否维持非塌缩、差异化的多智能体行为；等变正则强度需随团队规模调整。

Abstract: Transformer policies are attractive for multi-agent robot learning because self-attention can model interactions among agents. However, multi-agent teams are unordered, while transformers typically process agents as ordered token sequences. We study how this mismatch affects cooperative navigation policies under agent-order permutations. Our results show that low permutation error alone can be misleading: policies may appear robust simply because all agents choose the same action. We therefore evaluate policies using both permutation-consistency metrics and action-collapse diagnostics, including action diversity, same-action fraction, and maximum action frequency. A PPO-ID baseline yields non-collapsed behavior but remains order-sensitive, while strong equivariance regularization can still induce homogeneous behavior. A weak equivariance penalty improves the robustness while preserving more diverse actions for teams with \(N=3\) agents, whereas teams with \(N=4\) agents require substantially smaller regularization weights. These findings suggest that multi-agent transformer policies should be evaluated not only by return and permutation robustness, but also by whether they maintain non-collapsed, differentiated multi-agent behavior.

</details>


### [71] [SceneFactory-3D: Lifting 2D Traffic Scenes into 3D Physical Counterfactuals for Scalable Physically Grounded Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02874)
*Yicheng Zhu,Linfeng Tian,Tianmu Zhao,Yang Chen,Fan Zuo,Tao Li,Zilin Bian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SceneFactory-3D，一个 GPU 批量化、物理驱动的多智能体驾驶仿真器，通过逐轮接触点的悬挂与摩擦受限力执行车辆指令，并支持并行匹配反事实评估，用于研究控制器对路面条件的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展驾驶仿真器多以预设行为或运动学规则执行车辆指令，忽略轮胎-路面接触物理，难以刻画恶劣路面与环境条件如何改变车辆执行并传播至交通流。

Method: 车辆通过悬挂与摩擦受限力在每个轮胎接触点执行加速度与转向指令；空间变化摩擦、逐世界 3D 高度场、重力与刚性接触共同约束车轮运动与车身碰撞；借助逐世界地形隔离与 GPU 批处理，实现交通场景与控制器固定、仅道路条件变化的并行匹配物理反事实评估。

Result: 在 21 种摩擦与坡度条件下，对三类学习策略（每条件 1024 个匹配的 12 车世界）和两种经典规划器（共享 32 世界子集）进行评估；摩擦从 1.0 降至 0.18 时，学习策略安全通过作业区的车辆比例下降 6 至 90 个百分点（经典规划器为 18-19），且每种学习策略的近碰撞情形均更频繁。

Conclusion: SceneFactory-3D 提供物理落地的并行反事实评估能力，揭示学习型控制器对路面条件的高度敏感性，表明现有规划与控制方法在低摩擦场景下存在显著安全退化。

Abstract: Scalable driving simulators typically execute vehicle commands using prescribed behavioral or kinematic rules, overlooking the physics of tire-road interfaces, thereby limiting their ability to capture how adverse road and environmental conditions alter vehicle execution and propagate through traffic. To address this limitation, we present SceneFactory-3D, a GPU-batched, physics-grounded multi-agent driving simulator. Vehicles execute acceleration and steering commands via suspension- and friction-limited forces evaluated at each wheel-contact point. Spatially varying friction, per-world 3D heightfields, gravity, and rigid contact consistently govern wheel motion and chassis collisions. Per-world terrain isolation and GPU batching enable SceneFactory-3D to run matched physical counterfactuals in parallel: traffic scenario setup and vehicle controllers remain fixed while only the road condition changes, enabling the resulting closed-loop effects to be evaluated across parallel worlds. To demonstrate the advantage of the SceneFactory-3D-enabled counterfactual evaluation, we conduct an empirical study on vehicle controllers' sensitivity to road conditions. We study three learned-policy families on 1,024 matched 12-vehicle worlds per condition, and two classical planners on a shared 32-world subset, across 21 friction and grade conditions. When friction drops from 1.0 to 0.18, the share of vehicles that clear the work zone safely falls by 6 to 90 percentage points across learned policies (18-19 for classical planners), and near-collision situations become more frequent for every learned policy. Code: https://github.com/SmallWorldLab/SceneFactory_3D

</details>


### [72] [MixVLA: Adaptive Mixing of Non-Invariant Information for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02898)
*Pingrui Zhang,Yu Zhang,Pengyuan Wu,Bin Wang,Haoming Song,Xianqiang Gao,ZhaxiZhuoma,Zhigang Wang,Dong Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MixVLA，一种模型无关的训练框架，通过自适应混合非不变信息（AMI）正则化分布特异性变异，在不增加OOD数据或改动架构的前提下提升VLA模型的零样本泛化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中进展显著，但在分布外（OOD）条件下的零样本泛化仍受限。核心问题在于模型将任务相关的不变结构与环境特定的非不变因素纠缠在一起，导致动作预测依赖虚假的外观线索。

Method: MixVLA为模型无关训练框架，核心组件是自适应混合非不变信息（AMI）：随机混合非不变表征，以正则化分布特异性变异，同时保留互补的预测线索；随后将混合后的非不变特征与不变表征融合，用于最终动作预测。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin扰动套件及真实世界任务等挑战性操作设置上的大量实验表明，MixVLA在保持较强域内性能的同时，提升了整体零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦并混合非不变表征，可在不增加OOD数据、不修改架构的条件下增强VLA策略的泛化能力，兼顾鲁棒性与策略表达力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved remarkable advances in robotic manipulation, yet their zero-shot generalization under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions remains limited. These models often entangle task-relevant invariant structure with environment-specific non-invariant factors, causing policies to rely on spurious appearance cues during action prediction. In this work, we propose \textbf{MixVLA}, a model-agnostic training framework that improves the generalization of VLA models without requiring additional OOD data or architectural modifications. The key component of MixVLA is \textbf{Adaptive Mixing of Non-Invariant Information (AMI)}. AMI stochastically mixes non-invariant representations to regularize distribution-specific variability while preserving complementary predictive cues. The mixed non-invariant features are then fused with invariant representations for final action prediction, resulting in improved robustness without sacrificing policy expressiveness. Extensive experiments across challenging manipulation settings, including LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, the RoboTwin perturbation suite, and real-world tasks, demonstrate that MixVLA improves overall zero-shot robustness while retaining strong in-domain performance.

</details>


### [73] [From Language Priors to Field Adaptation: Preference Learning for Traversability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02974)
*Simon Schwaiger,David Seyser,Alessandro Scherl,Zlatan Ajanović,Wilfried Wöber,Gerald Steinbauer-Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于von Mises-Fisher混合原型与自然语言先验的可通过性估计方法，通过稀疏相对图像标注微调，实现样本高效的跨域自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像可通过性估计依赖机器人平台、部署域和任务偏好，导致专用训练模型泛化性差，需要在目标域进行域适应，而目标域标注成本高。

Method: 在冻结的视觉-语言特征空间中，用von Mises-Fisher混合原型表示可通过性；以相对自然语言规则提供常识先验，用稀疏的相对图像标注通过计算与样本高效的微调来调整原型方向和效用值。

Result: 在WayFAST上取得与端到端训练估计器相当的精度，同时支持样本高效的图像域适应；定性实验显示语言先验具有零样本适用性，微调后的估计器在语义图上密集预测改善，并通过剖析语义相近的自然语言提示解释所学原型。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了以原型+语言先验+稀疏偏好标注进行样本高效域适应的有效性，代码与训练好的估计器已开源。

Abstract: Image-based traversability estimation is inherently dependent on the robot platform, deployment domain, and mission preferences, which limits the applicability of purpose-trained models. To facilitate domain adaptation, this work aims to reduce the number of required annotations in the target domain using sample-efficient preference learning. Our method represents traversability through von Mises-Fisher mixture prototypes in a frozen vision-language feature space. Relative natural-language rules provide a commonsense prior, while sparse relative image annotations adapt the prototype directions and utilities to a target domain through computationally and sample-efficient fine-tuning. Experiments on WayFAST demonstrate accuracy competitive with end-to-end trained estimators while enabling sample-efficient image-based adaptation. Qualitative experiments further demonstrate the language prior's zero shot applicability and the fine-tuned estimator's improved dense prediction on semantic maps. Evaluation is complemented via semantic interpretation of learned prototypes by dissecting semantically close natural language prompts. Code and trained estimators available at https://resireg.github.io

</details>


### [74] [On Representational Alignment among Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02985)
*Fulvio Mastrogiovanni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"关系充分性"框架，用于刻画具身智能体在异构、异步、观察者相关表征下，为达成连贯交互究竟需要解决哪些表征歧义，并给出所需额外标量锚点的局部一阶下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体在同一物理过程中可能持有异构、异步且相对于观察者的表征。通常假设这些表征应全局对齐，但作者质疑这一假设，转而研究：为了实现连贯交互，究竟哪些表征差异必须被消解。核心动机是寻找一种任务相关的对齐停止条件，而非一味消除所有残余分歧。

Method: 1) 形式化"关系充分性"：定义交互特定的关系证据映射，它刻画比较后仍未解决的表示歧义；当每个残余歧义纤维（fiber）都落在某个交互等价类内时，充分性成立。2) 若条件不满足，则需引入额外的感觉运动锚点，精确分离那些可能改变交互相关结果的歧义。3) 针对光滑系统，推导所需独立标量锚点数量的局部一阶下界，其值等于"对关系证据不可见、但对交互相关映射可见"的方向维数。4) 在异步交接场景的两个变体中实例化该理论，二者共享相同的观察者智能体、表征、传输和关系证据。

Result: 在异步交接场景的两个变体中：相对同步变体不需要任何额外锚点；而在绝对时间上的交接变体恰好需要1个额外锚点。二者在观察者、表征、传输和关系证据上完全相同，仅因交互相关映射不同而导致所需锚点数不同。

Conclusion: 该分析为表征对齐提供了一个任务相关的停止条件：当残余分歧相对于交互是不变量时，无需将其消除。换言之，表征对齐的目标不应是全局一致，而是相对于特定交互的关系充分性。

Abstract: Embodied agents interacting with the same physical process may maintain heterogeneous, asynchronous, and observer-relative representations. Rather than assuming that such representations should always be globally aligned, we investigate which distinctions among them must actually be resolved for coherent interaction. We formalize this question through relational sufficiency: an interaction-specific relational-evidence map defines the representational ambiguity left unresolved by comparison, and sufficiency holds when each residual ambiguity fiber lies within an interaction-equivalence class. When this condition fails, additional sensorimotor anchors must separate precisely the ambiguities that can change the interaction-relevant outcome. For smooth systems, we derive a local first-order lower bound on the number of inde- pendent scalar anchors required, given by the dimension of directions that are invisible to relational evidence but visible to the interaction-relevant map. We instantiate the theory in two variants of an asynchronous hand-off scenario that share the same observer agents, representations, transports, and relational evidence. Relative synchronization requires no additional anchor, whereas hand-off at an absolute time requires exactly one. The analysis there- fore provides a task-relative stopping condition for representational alignment: residual disagreement need not be eliminated when it is invariant with respect to the interaction.

</details>


### [75] [Subject-Specific Predictive Musculoskeletal Simulations of Lower-Limb Exoskeleton Assistance: Metabolic and Biomechanical Effects of Joint Assistance Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02991)
*Neethan Ratnakumar,Mariya Tohfafarosha,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究利用肌骨模型预测仿真，评估六名受试者在25与50 Nm峰值力矩下不同下肢关节助力组合的能量最优策略，发现H+K+A组合节能最多，H+A是最佳双执行器方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下肢外骨骼在减少行走能耗方面已有进展，但受个体体型与生物力学差异影响，最优助力策略设计仍具挑战性，需探索能量最优的关节助力配置。

Method: 采用基于BMI肌肉力量缩放的六名健全受试者个体化肌骨模型，进行预测仿真以生成自选步速步态；引入理想执行器，在25 Nm和50 Nm峰值力矩下模拟多种关节助力组合，并通过运输成本、关节运动学、肌肉激活、助力力矩及关节功率指标评估效果。

Result: 50 Nm下H+K+A组合平均COST降低最多（48.50±4.55%，个体42.22%–54.65%）；单关节中髋助力最有效（31.77±5.21%），膝与踝较小且相近（19.17%、17.02%）；H+A为最佳双关节方案（44.60±7.08%）。力矩上限提高主要增加髋踝正助力功率，膝助力敏感性低且在预摆动期提供正功率。

Conclusion: 结果支持H+A作为高效的双执行器助力目标，并指出膝关节在预摆动期异常功率策略值得针对性实验验证。

Abstract: Lower-limb exoskeletons have made considerable progress in reducing energy expenditure during walking. However, designing optimal assistance strategies remains challenging, particularly given inter-individual variability in anthropometry and biomechanics. This study explores energy-optimal lower-limb joint-assistance strategies using predictive simulations with musculoskeletal models. Subject-specific models of six able-bodied subjects, with BMI-based muscle strength scaling, were used in predictive simulations to generate gait at self-selected walking speeds. Ideal actuators were incorporated to simulate various combinations of joint assistance at peak levels of 25 Nm and 50 Nm to examine the effects of assistance on gait and metabolic savings. The effects of each assistance configuration were assessed through cost of transport (COT), joint kinematics, muscle activations, assistive torques, and joint-level power metrics to characterize the biomechanical and energetic impacts of different assistance strategies. At 50 Nm, combined H+K+A (hip-knee-ankle) assistance resulted in the greatest mean COT reduction of 48.50 +/- 4.55%, with individual reductions ranging from 42.22% to 54.65% across the subjects. Among single-joint conditions, assisting the hip was most effective, reducing COT by 31.77 +/- 5.21%; the knee and ankle produced smaller, comparable reductions (19.17% and 17.02%). H+A (hip-ankle) assistance (44.60 +/- 7.08%) emerged as the most effective two-joint assistive configuration. Increasing the torque bound increased positive assistive power primarily at the hip and ankle, while knee assistance showed little sensitivity and delivered positive power near pre-swing. These results support H+A assistance as an efficient two-actuator target, while identifying the knee's atypical pre-swing power strategy as a candidate for targeted experimental validation.

</details>


### [76] [Safe Streaming Flow Planning by Aligning Sampling Dynamics with Execution Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03132)
*Seunghwan Jang,Jeongyong Yang,Siddharth Ancha,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SafeStreamingFlow，一种目标条件生成式规划器，通过将流采样动力学与执行动力学对齐，并仅在执行步上施加高阶控制屏障函数约束，实现快速在线重规划与安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配生成式规划器虽能从示范中学习长时域轨迹合成，但实际部署需要：(i) 执行过程中满足安全约束；(ii) 以快速执行频率进行紧密在线重规划。现有安全扩散/流规划器一次性生成完整轨迹并反复扰动中间状态以满足安全约束，导致计算开销大，且因采样动力学与系统执行动力学不一致而引入分布偏移。

Method: 提出SafeStreamingFlow：1）顺序积分学习到的状态向量场，并采用分层状态预测，使流采样动力学与执行动力学对齐；2）仅在已执行的那一步上通过高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）施加安全约束，避免对整条轨迹反复扰动。

Result: 在导航、竞速和运动控制（locomotion）基准上，与现有方法相比，SafeStreamingFlow降低了规划延迟、提升了安全性，同时保持具有竞争力的目标达成成功率。

Conclusion: 通过将生成式流的采样过程与真实执行过程对齐，并把安全约束局部化到实际执行的单步之上，可同时实现低延迟在线重规划与安全保障，且不牺牲任务成功率。

Abstract: Generative planners based on diffusion/flow matching can learn to synthesize long-horizon trajectories from demonstrations. However, real-world deployment requires (i) enforcing safety constraints during execution and (ii) tight online replanning at fast execution rates. Prior safe diffusion/flow planners generate the agent's full trajectory at once, while repeatedly perturbing intermediate states to satisfy safety constraints. This approach is not only computationally intensive, but also introduces distribution shift since the learned sampling dynamics is distinct from the system's execution dynamics. We propose SafeStreamingFlow, a goal-conditioned planner that aligns flow sampling dynamics with execution dynamics by sequentially integrating a learned state vector field with hierarchical state prediction. Importantly, we need to enforce safety constraints only for the executed step via high order control barrier functions. Across navigation, racing, and locomotion benchmarks, SafeStreamingFlow reduces planning latency and improves safety compared to existing methods, while maintaining competitive goal-reaching success.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Reward Hacking: Proxy Divergence Across Four Layers of a Staged Humanoid Learning Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03196)
*Arunabh Bora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将足式机器人强化学习流程中的奖励、课程门控、评估统计与参考动作统一定义为代理量,分别推导其偏离目标的条件并给出修正方案,并用一个在单张笔记本GPU上训练13500次迭代的四阶段PPO人形机器人实验加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点只把奖励视为被优化对象,因而把规格失效(奖励欺骗)仅归因于奖励本身。作者认为课程门控、评估统计与参考动作同样是形式意义上的代理量,各自存在特征性的偏离机制,需要系统性地识别并闭合这些偏离。

Method: 将RL流程分解为四层代理,每层给出传统形式、偏离目标的推导条件与替代方案:在二次核平坦处采用一阶(L1)代价;用峰值与结果统计替代课程门控的均值;引入门控可达性与信息检查;采用确定性且相位去同步的评估;将课程状态纳入模型;采用可行性优先的参考设计并叠加残差前馈;以及函数保持的输入拓宽,使单一策略可生长而非重训练。实验采用一条连续演进的PPO策略,面向仿真中1.91米人形机器人,四个阶段共13500次迭代,单张笔记本GPU完成。

Result: 实验显示两类典型失效:基于平均误差构建的课程门控在每次检查时都以其速率上限推进,而其所门控的技能实际并不存在;批量推力测试因同步重置与步态相位发生混叠,将0.5 m/s的推力误判为比2.0 m/s更危险。

Conclusion: RL流程的规格失效并非仅源于奖励,而是分布在奖励、课程门控、评估统计与参考动作四层代理中;每层均可通过特定重构闭合偏离,从而实现策略的可生长式训练与更可靠的技能获取。

Abstract: A reinforcement-learning (RL) pipeline for a legged robot is assembled from proxies. A reward stands in for intended behaviour, a curriculum gate stands in for competence, an evaluation statistic stands in for robustness, and a reference motion stands in for an achievable skill. The traditional view treats only the first of these as optimised against, and so locates specification failure (reward hacking) in the reward alone. I argue that all four are proxies in the same formal sense, that each has a characteristic divergence mechanism, and that each admits a reformulation that closes it. For every layer I state the traditional formulation, derive the condition under which it diverges from its target, and give the alternative: first-order (L1) costs where quadratic kernels are flat, peak and outcome statistics where curriculum gates average, gate reachability and information checks, deterministic and phase-desynchronised evaluation, curriculum state treated as part of the model, feasibility-first reference design with residual feed-forward, and function-preserving input widening that lets one policy grow instead of being retrained. The arguments are illustrated by measurements from one continuous lineage of a PPO policy for a simulated 1.91 m humanoid, grown over four stages and 13,500 iterations on a single laptop GPU. Among them, a curriculum gate built on averaged error advanced at its rate limit on every check while the skill it gated was absent, and a batched push test whose synchronised resets aliased the gait phase ranked a 0.5 m/s push as more dangerous than a 2.0 m/s one.

</details>


### [78] [DexJoCo-X: Benchmarking Action Representations for Multi-Hand Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03278)
*Xiangwei Jiang,Yao Mu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DexJoCo-X 基准与工具包，通过七种灵巧手、六个任务、2100 条平衡演示对比三种策略，发现跨具身学习依赖动作坐标、预训练与架构的协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手种类日益增多，为每种形态单独采集数据、训练策略难以为继；跨具身学习需要统一表示，以同时捕捉共享操作结构与形态特有的控制；而现有研究因手、任务、数据集、控制接口不一致，无法分离表示、预训练与架构各自的贡献。

Method: 构建 DexJoCo-X：统一七种代表性灵巧手的多手多任务评测协议，共享场景、成功判据与执行接口，重新设计 glove-to-hand 映射，并提供自动化流水线在随机化场景中扩充经审核演示（2100 条平衡演示）。在统一协议下评估 π0.5、Ego-Pi、Being-H0.5，检验共享动作接口是否足以支持多手学习，并比较不同动作表示（function-aligned action slots、原生坐标、DexLatent）。

Result: 将 π0.5 扩展为 80 维双臂输出后成功率接近零；Ego-Pi 通过交错预测保留预训练动作头，可支持单手多任务学习，但对七手联合训练无效；Being-H0.5 结合跨具身预训练、统一动作空间与具身感知专家，实现单一策略控制全部七只手，其中 function-aligned action slots 平均成功率 47.7%，原生坐标 47.0%，DexLatent 33.1%。

Conclusion: 跨具身表示的效果取决于整个学习系统，动作坐标、预训练与架构必须协同作用，才能将共享操作结构与具身特定控制有效解耦。

Abstract: As dexterous hands proliferate, collecting data and training policies separately for every morphology becomes increasingly impractical. Scalable cross-embodiment learning therefore requires a unified representation that captures shared manipulation structure while preserving morphology-specific control. Differences in hands, tasks, datasets, and control interfaces prevent existing studies from isolating the effects of representation, pretraining, and architecture. We introduce DexJoCo-X, a benchmark and toolkit for controlled comparison across seven representative dexterous hands, six single-arm and bimanual tasks, and 2,100 balanced demonstrations. DexJoCo-X provides a matched multi-hand, multi-task protocol with common scenes, success criteria, and execution interfaces, redesigned glove-to-hand mappings, and an automated pipeline that expands reviewed demonstrations across randomized scenes. Using $π_{0.5}$, Ego-Pi, and Being-H0.5, we examine whether a shared action interface is sufficient for multi-hand learning. Expanding $π_{0.5}$ to an 80-dimensional bimanual output yields near-zero success. Ego-Pi preserves the pretrained action head through interleaved prediction and supports per-hand multi-task learning, but remains ineffective for seven-hand joint training. By contrast, Being-H0.5 combines cross-embodiment pretraining, a unified action space, and embodiment-aware experts, enabling one policy to control all seven hands. Within this architecture, function-aligned action slots achieve 47.7% mean success, compared with 47.0% for native coordinates and 33.1% for DexLatent. These results show that cross-embodiment representation depends on the entire learning system: action coordinates, pretraining, and architecture must jointly separate shared manipulation structure from embodiment-specific control.

</details>


### [79] [HexVIO: Towards All-Day Stereo-Inertial Tracking Through Commodity DSPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03283)
*Patrick Wolf,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HexVIO，将双目惯性里程计的视觉前端卸载至Hexagon DSP，在主CPU运行后端，实现67%功耗降低或86%吞吐提升，可在手机上以0.83 W持续30 fps实时跟踪约18小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备自定位是空间计算的基本前提，VIO是成本低且精度高的方案；机器人、可穿戴设备、XR设备与无人机需要高效VIO实现，以获得更轻、更凉、更便宜、续航更长且体验更好的设备。因此需要探索利用现代智能手机和XR设备中普遍存在的商用协处理器（Hexagon DSP）来提升VIO效率。

Method: 将双目惯性里程计的视觉前端卸载到Hexagon DSP，后端保留在主CPU；针对DSP架构进行实现优化，并与仅CPU执行进行对比。

Result: 在商用智能手机上，HexVIO相比仅CPU执行实现67%功耗降低或86%吞吐提升；可长期实时维持30 fps跟踪，功耗0.83 W，对应测试设备上约18小时跟踪时长。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了商用DSP在机器人和移动设备中实现全天候视觉惯性跟踪的潜力。

Abstract: The ability of a device to localize itself within its surroundings is a fundamental prerequisite for spatial computing. Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) has proven to be a cost-effective and accurate solution for this task. Robots, wearables, XR devices, and drones can benefit significantly from efficient implementations of VIO since they allow for cooler, lighter, and cheaper devices with longer battery life and a better user experience. In this work, we propose to enhance the efficiency of a VIO system by leveraging the Hexagon DSP, a commodity co-processor present in many modern smartphones and XR devices. Our approach offloads the visual frontend of a stereo-inertial odometry system to the DSP while keeping the backend on the main CPU. By optimizing the implementation for the DSP architecture, we achieve significant reductions in power consumption and latency compared to CPU-only execution. Our system, HexVIO, demonstrates a 67% reduction in power consumption or an 86% increase in throughput on a commodity smartphone, with the ability to sustain long-term real-time 30 fps tracking for 0.83 W, corresponding to ~18 hours of tracking on the testing device. These results highlight the potential of commodity DSPs for enabling all-day visual-inertial tracking in robotics and mobile devices.

</details>


### [80] [Equivariant Visual-Tactile Diffusion Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03333)
*Lik Hang Kenny Wong,Yiyao Ma,Xiu-Shen Wei,Zelong Tan,Zhuheng Song,Dongsheng Xie,Kai Chen,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VISTA：一种基于工作空间等变性与球面融合的视觉-触觉扩散策略，用于数据高效的高接触富操作模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高接触富操作模仿学习依赖高质量专家数据，采集成本高，因此需要样本高效策略。现有方法未充分利用视觉与触觉的空间等变结构。

Method: 将视觉与触觉观测投影为球面token；通过置换等变球面融合把触觉接触线索注入视觉球面方向；利用末端执行器姿态旋转融合后的调和表示；该表示条件化等变扩散策略以预测空间一致的動作。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人设置中，VISTA相比强视觉-触觉模仿学习基线显著提升数据效率。

Conclusion: 工作空间级等变视觉-触觉扩散策略可有效提升高接触富操作的数据效率。

Abstract: Imitation learning for contact-rich manipulation requires high-quality expert data that is expensive to obtain. This makes learning a sample-efficient policy a key issue. To address this, we propose VISTA, a workspace-level equivariant visuotactile diffusion policy for data-efficient contact-rich imitation learning. VISTA projects visual and tactile observations into spherical tokens, injects tactile contact cues into visual spherical directions through permutation-equivariant spherical fusion, and rotates the fused harmonic representation using the end-effector orientation. The resulting representation conditions an equivariant diffusion policy to predict spatially consistent actions. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world robotic settings show that VISTA substantially improves data efficiency over strong visuotactile imitation learning baselines. Project website: https://vista-paper.github.io/

</details>


### [81] [KungfuAthleteBot: learning high-dynamic humanoid motion from video with unified robust recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03388)
*Zhongxiang Lei,Lulu Cao,Xuyang Wang,Tianyi Qian,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KungfuAthleteBot (KAB) 框架，将"从视频学习高动态人形机器人运动"作为核心问题，逐一解决视频数据的物理不一致、缺乏驱动力信息、缺少失败/恢复信息三大缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频是丰富、廉价且富含极限运动行为的人体运动数据来源，但难以直接用于人形机器人：视频重建轨迹物理不一致、不含驱动力信息、也不涉及失败与恢复情形。作者认为，修复并补偿视频数据（而非单纯收集更多数据）才是释放高动态人形技能的关键。

Method: (C1) 构建KungfuAthlete数据集（国家级武术运动员视频），引入物理引导的抛物线轨迹修正，消除腾空与落地阶段的高度漂浮、穿地和抖动；(C2) 提出物理驱动的伪低动能(LKE)采样，将初始化偏向动力学可行状态，使策略发现可行驱动模式而非模仿不可行轨迹；(C3) 提出统一策略的直接训练范式，在同一策略内同时学习扰动抑制与摔倒恢复，无需恢复参考数据与手动模式切换。

Result: 在人形机器人上，KAB可从视频学习动态技能，并在约0.7秒内从任意摔倒状态恢复，为统一策略下已报道的最快恢复速度；统一策略的消融实验证实各组件均为必要。

Conclusion: 修复与补偿视频数据（而非仅扩大数据采集）是解锁高动态人形机器人技能的关键，验证了KAB三部分机制的有效性与必要性。

Abstract: Video is an abundant, inexpensive source of human motion data that is rich in extreme athletic behaviors. Making it usable for humanoid robots, however, is not a matter of simply retargeting a reconstructed trajectory: video-derived motion is physically inconsistent, devoid of actuation information, and says nothing about failure or recovery. We present KungfuAthleteBot (KAB), a framework that treats learning high-dynamic motion from video as the central problem and resolves each of these three failure modes in turn. (C1) We build the KungfuAthlete dataset from videos of national-level martial artists and introduce a physics-guided parabolic trajectory correction that removes height floating, ground penetration, and high-frequency jitter from reconstructed aerial and landing phases. (C2) Because video carries no force information, strict tracking of a reconstructed trajectory is dynamically infeasible, and error-driven initialization keeps re-launching the policy from infeasible aerial poses. We introduce physics-driven pseudo-low-kinetic-energy (LKE) sampling, our central mechanism for making such references learnable: it biases initialization towards dynamically feasible states, letting the policy discover feasible actuation patterns instead of imitating infeasible ones. (C3) Finally, we introduce a direct training paradigm in which disturbance rejection and fall recovery are learned inside the same policy that tracks the video motion, requiring no recovery reference data and no manual mode switching. On a humanoid robot, KAB learns dynamic skills from video and recovers from arbitrary falls in about 0.7 s, the fastest reported recovery for a unified policy. Ablations on the unified policy confirm the necessity of its components, supporting the view that repairing and compensating video data, rather than only collecting more of it, is what unlocks high-dynamic humanoid skills.

</details>


### [82] [I2CD: Direct Image-to-Convex Decomposition for Simulation-Ready Collision Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03453)
*Qian Wang,Liam Merz Hoffmeister,Brian Scassellati,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 I2CD，直接从单张 RGB 图像预测凸分解，冻结预训练的 Hunyuan3D-2 并仅训练轻量交叉注意力头，输出可直接用于物理引擎的紧凑凸多面体几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真器与运动规划需要凸碰撞几何，而图像到3D生成模型输出的是稠密且常为非流形的视觉网格。弥合二者差距目前依赖"先重建再分解"的流水线（修复、抽稀、近似凸分解），速度慢且脆弱。

Method: 不训练新的图像到3D模型，而是冻结预训练的 Hunyuan3D-2 图像条件扩散Transformer与形状解码器，仅训练一个轻量交叉注意力头（38M参数，不到十GPU小时）。该头学习"凸槽"（convex-slot）token，输出 K 个凸多面体的半平面参数，从而按构造保证凸性且无需后处理。

Result: 在227个留出的 OmniObject3D 与 Google Scanned Objects 实例上，I2CD 取得八条"先重建再分解"流水线中最高的体积 IoU，端到端速度快 6–37 倍（约0.5秒/图）。在 MuJoCo、PyBullet、Genesis、Isaac Sim 的跨仿真器研究中，各引擎可直接使用 I2CD 几何；而原始生成网格虽能"加载"，多数情况下会被静默替换为不同碰撞形状，或需要数秒至数分钟的单物体预处理。在实体 xArm7 上，I2CD 为20物体杂乱场景生成可规划几何仅需11秒（最强基线328秒），抓取放置成功率相当（85/100 对 90/100）。

Conclusion: I2CD 通过冻结生成模型、仅训练凸槽交叉注意力头，实现从单图直接、快速、无需后处理地生成物理可用的凸分解几何，在精度、速度与跨仿真器/实体机器人可用性上均优于先重建再分解的方案。

Abstract: Physics simulators and motion planners require convex collision geometry, yet image-to-3D generative models output dense, frequently non-manifold visual meshes. Bridging the two today takes a slow, brittle reconstruct-then-decompose pipeline of repair, decimation, and approximate convex decomposition. We present I2CD, which predicts a convex decomposition directly from a single RGB image. Rather than train a new image-to-3D model, I2CD freezes the pretrained Hunyuan3D-2 image-conditioned diffusion transformer and shape decoder and trains only a lightweight cross-attention head (38M parameters, under ten GPU-hours) whose learned "convex-slot" tokens emit the halfplane parameters of $K$ convex polytopes. The output is compact, convex by construction, and loads into physics engines without any post-processing, in ${\sim}0.5$s per image. On $227$ held-out OmniObject3D and Google Scanned Objects instances, I2CD attains the highest volumetric IoU among eight reconstruct-then-decompose pipelines while running $6$-$37\times$ faster end-to-end. In a cross-simulator study in MuJoCo, PyBullet, Genesis, and Isaac Sim, every engine uses I2CD geometry as delivered, whereas raw generated meshes "load" everywhere but are silently replaced by a different collision shape in most cases or need seconds to minutes of per-object preprocessing. On a physical xArm7, I2CD produces planner-ready geometry for a $20$-object cluttered scene in $11$s versus $328$s for the strongest baseline, at comparable pick-and-place execution success ($85$ vs. $90$ of $100$ trials).

</details>


### [83] [MobiAgent: Dual-Loop Recursive Policy Self-Improvement for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03476)
*Chenzhi Liu,Yue Zhang,Jiehong Lin,Jianan Wang,Bo Wang,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MobiAgent：双环智能体框架，通过可组合原子技能与自主终身学习，实现长时程移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程移动操作面临执行误差累积与移动/操作能力干扰问题；现有VLA模型缺乏多阶段目标所需的层级推理，已有层级智能体存在子任务映射僵化、重规划不灵活、缺乏持续学习等缺陷。

Method: 提出双环框架。内环：用VLM进行滚动时域规划与视觉反思，将高层推理与底层控制解耦为可组合原子技能，由共享统一VLM骨干的专用流匹配专家执行，兼顾复用性并缓解能力干扰。外环：自动分割并验证部署轨迹，聚类发现原子技能，在无人工标注下持续微调技能库，实现终身学习。

Result: 在RoboCasa、BEHAVIOR-1K及真实任务上验证。BEHAVIOR-1K上超越π_0.5-TA 22.5个百分点，并能从执行失败中稳健恢复。自主数据回收使RoboCasa成功率由7.50%提升至27.50%，Astribot S1由32.5%提升至57.5%。

Conclusion: MobiAgent通过部署执行与策略自改进的双环协同，有效解决长时程移动操作中的层级推理与持续学习问题，具备稳健错误恢复与自主进化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon mobile manipulation presents significant challenges due to compounding execution errors and capacity interference between locomotion and arm control. While recent Vision-Language-Action models excel at short-horizon tasks, they lack the hierarchical reasoning required for multi-stage objectives. Furthermore, existing hierarchical agents suffer from rigid sub-task mapping, inflexible replanning, and a lack of continuous learning. To address these limitations, we introduce MobiAgent, a dual-loop agentic framework that bridges robust deployment execution and recursive policy self-improvement. During deployment, the Inner Loop decouples high-level reasoning from low-level control through highly composable atomic skills. It employs Vision-Language models for receding-horizon planning and visual reflection, dynamically composing skills to ensure robust error recovery. These skills are executed by specialized flow-matching experts that share a unified VLM backbone, maximizing reusability while mitigating capacity interference. Concurrently, the Outer Loop drives automated lifelong learning by autonomously segmenting and verifying deployment rollouts, clustering them to discover atomic skills, and continuously fine-tuning the skill library without human annotations. Evaluations on RoboCasa, BEHAVIOR-1K, and real-world tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of MobiAgent. It outperforms $π_{0.5}$-TA by 22.5 percentage points on BEHAVIOR-1K and enables robust recovery from execution failures. Through autonomous data recycling, success improves from 7.50% to 27.50% on RoboCasa and from 32.5% to 57.5% on Astribot S1.

</details>


### [84] [Detect and Suppress: A Mechanistic Defense against Adversarial Patches in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03498)
*Yukiya Horiba,Koshiro Aoki,Shunsuke Yasuki,Bum Jun Kim,Taiki Miyanishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析VLA模型的内部表征，找到一个与对抗补丁强相关的特征；仅在检测到攻击时抑制该特征，可在不微调的情况下提升鲁棒性，且发现干预时机至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗补丁可通过操纵视觉观测破坏VLA模型导致机器人控制失败，但目前对这些失败的内部机制以及如何通过针对性干预缓解攻击尚不清楚。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）对VLA表征进行机制性分析，识别出激活与对抗补丁存在性强相关的特征；在推理阶段，仅当线性探针（linear probe）检测到攻击时抑制该特征，无需微调VLA。

Result: 在LIBERO-10上针对VLA对抗补丁攻击进行评估。条件式干预（仅在检测到攻击时抑制）在间歇性攻击下提升成功率；而持续施加相同干预则显著降低策略性能。

Conclusion: 攻击相关的内部表征可作为VLA对抗防御的有效靶点；控制干预时机对于限制对正常策略行为的干扰至关重要。

Abstract: Adversarial patches can disrupt Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by manipulating visual observations, leading to failures in robot control. However, it remains poorly understood which internal mechanisms underlie these failures and how targeted interventions can mitigate them. In this work, we mechanistically analyze VLA representations using a sparse autoencoder (SAE) and identify a feature whose activation strongly correlates with the presence of an adversarial patch. Based on this analysis, we suppress the identified feature at inference time only when a linear probe detects an attack. This intervention improves robustness without the cost of fine-tuning the VLA. We evaluate our method against VLA adversarial patch attacks on LIBERO-10. Conditional intervention improves success rate under intermittent attacks, whereas continuously applying the same intervention substantially degrades policy performance. These results show that attack-related internal representations can provide useful targets for VLA adversarial defense and that controlling when to intervene is important for limiting disruption to nominal policy behavior.

</details>


### [85] [XGenAct: Geometry-Enhanced World Action Models through Cross-Task Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03516)
*Tingting Du,Ziyao Wang,Guoheng Sun,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XGenAct是一种世界动作模型，通过确定性编解码器将RGB观测、机器人动作、度量深度、表面法线和功能性角色分割统一表示为RGB视频，并用单一视频扩散Transformer和单一目标进行时序预测，在RLBench任务上取得52%成功率，显著优于最强基线（26%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型基于RGB和动作预测未来，未显式处理机器人操作所需的空间理解；先前工作仅通过专门头或分支添加有限的空间预测任务，导致空间监督范围和模型架构都碎片化。

Method: 利用确定性编解码器将RGB观测、机器人动作、度量深度、表面法线和功能性角色分割统一编码为RGB视频；训练时采样感知与动作任务，使用单一视频扩散Transformer和单一目标学习跨这些空间的时序预测，无需模态特定的学习头。

Result: 在留出的RLBench任务上，结构化感知训练相比仅RGB训练提升了平均闭环成功率；在五项任务外部对比中，XGenAct达到52%成功率，而评估的最强基线为26%；未来深度和分割预测精度优于先生成RGB再应用冻结感知专家的管线。

Conclusion: 将多模态空间监督统一为RGB视频形式的单一扩散模型框架，可在无模态特定头的情况下提升机器人操作的闭环成功率与空间预测精度，验证了统一世界动作模型的有效性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have advanced robot control by predicting how observations and actions evolve over time. Despite this progress, RGB and action based future prediction does not explicitly address the spatial understanding needed for robot manipulation. Existing efforts often add a limited set of spatial prediction tasks through specialized heads or branches, leaving both the range of spatial supervision and the model architecture fragmented. We introduce XGenAct, a world action model that represents RGB observations, robot actions, metric depth, surface normals, and functional role segmentation as RGB videos through deterministic codecs. By sampling perception and action tasks during training, XGenAct uses one video diffusion transformer and one objective to learn temporal prediction across these spaces without modality specific learned heads. On held out RLBench tasks, structured perception training improves average closed loop success over RGB only training, and XGenAct achieves 52% success in the five task external comparison, versus 26% for the strongest evaluated baselines. It also predicts future depth and segmentation more accurately than the evaluated pipelines that generate RGB first and then apply a frozen perception expert.

</details>


### [86] [DR-IPC: Disturbance-Resilient Integrated Planning and Control for LiDAR-Based Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03530)
*Peng Liu,Jingyan Wang,Qipeng Ye,Wen Li,Jinya Su,Zuo Wang,Shihua Li,Yunda Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DR-IPC框架，将轻量路径引导与非线性模型预测控制结合，统一规划与控制以提升LiDAR四旋翼在扰动环境下的导航鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR四旋翼在杂乱环境中的导航在外部扰动下仍具挑战，尤其当避障运动生成与抗扰控制被分离在不同层级处理时，系统性能受限。

Method: DR-IPC将轻量路径引导与NMPC结合，直接生成角速度与推力；采用互联的扩展卡尔曼滤波与非线性扰动观测器联合提供滤波状态估计与重构扰动用于NMPC预测；公式化统一了非线性四旋翼动力学、执行器约束、局部运动生成与惩罚式安全飞行走廊残差，无需独立轨迹优化阶段。

Result: Gazebo与MARSIM仿真及室内外实验验证了DR-IPC在风扰、悬挂负载、狭窄通道、球体撞击及动态障碍反应式避障下的性能；多目标导航中Gazebo完成任务数由1/10提升至9/10，实验高度RMSE由0.34 m降至0.01 m；系统机载运行于100 Hz。

Conclusion: DR-IPC通过一体化规划与控制有效增强四旋翼在扰动与杂乱环境中的导航鲁棒性，且可机载实时运行。

Abstract: LiDAR-based quadrotor navigation in cluttered environments remains challenging under external disturbances, particularly when obstacle-aware motion generation and disturbance-rejection control are handled in separate layers. This article presents disturbance-resilient integrated planning and control (DR-IPC), which combines lightweight path guidance with nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) to directly generate angular velocity and thrust. An interconnected extended Kalman filter and nonlinear disturbance observer jointly provide filtered state estimates and reconstructed disturbances for NMPC prediction. The resulting formulation unifies nonlinear quadrotor dynamics, actuator constraints, local motion generation, and penalised safe-flight-corridor residuals without requiring a separate trajectory-optimization stage. Gazebo and MARSIM simulations, together with indoor and outdoor experiments, validate DR-IPC under wind, suspended payloads, narrow passages, ball impacts and reactive avoidance of a dynamic obstacle. In multi-goal navigation with disturbances, DR-IPC increases the number of completed missions from 1/10 to 9/10 in Gazebo and reduces the altitude RMSE from 0.34 to 0.01 m in experiments. The complete system operates onboard at 100 Hz. Supplementary videos are available on the project page https://drpp316.github.io/DR-IPC-Page/, and the source code will be released.

</details>


### [87] [Autonomous Robotic Navigation for Endovascular Brain-Computer Interface Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03537)
*Harry Robertshaw,Weijie Qi,Nikola Fischer,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C. Booth,Sam E. John*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究首次在体外演示了用于血管内脑机接口（BCI）进入脑静脉系统的自主机器人导航，采用Soft Actor-Critic控制器与在线失败预测器，结果显示仿真成功率较高、20次物理实验中14次成功，但跨解剖泛化与仿真到现实标定仍是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内BCI可避免开颅手术，但设备需经解剖变异性大的脑静脉精准递送，现有方法难以兼顾自主性与安全性，缺乏对失败风险的在线预判手段，因此需要验证自主机器人导航在该路径上的可行性。

Method: 基于单一训练解剖结构做几何增强，在仿真环境中用Soft Actor-Critic训练控制器，完成从右颈内静脉到上矢状窦的两个序贯任务；在训练解剖与未见过的留出解剖模型上评估，共1,000次仿真回合（250次/任务-解剖条件）与20次透视引导体外机器人实验（5次/条件），并评估任务循环预测器进行在线失败识别。

Result: 仿真中任务A、B成功率在训练解剖为85.6%和98.4%，在留出解剖为42.0%和91.6%；20次物理实验中14次成功（总体70%），其中任务B在留出体模中成功率为80%。仿真失败预测器检出99.3–100.0%的失败，误报率0.8–6.7%；体外实验中预测风险在失败前升高，但部分成功实验中也出现高概率，显示迁移后标定退化。

Conclusion: 结果证明了自主脑静脉通路接入的可行性，并表明在线失败预测可支持人类监督；但解剖泛化能力与仿真到现实的标定是临床前转化前需要优先解决的问题。

Abstract: Endovascular brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) avoid craniotomy but require precise device delivery through anatomically variable cerebral veins. This work presents the first demonstration of in vitro autonomous robotic navigation for endovascular BCI access in the cerebral venous system. Soft Actor-Critic controllers were trained in silico for two sequential tasks spanning the right internal jugular vein to the superior sagittal sinus, using geometric augmentation of one training anatomy. Navigation was evaluated in a training anatomy and an anatomically unseen hold-out model over 250 in silico episodes and five fluoroscopy-guided in vitro robotic runs per task-anatomy condition, comprising 1,000 simulated episodes and 20 physical runs overall. Task recurrent predictors were also evaluated for online identification of impending navigation failure. In silico success rates for Tasks A and B were 85.6% and 98.4% in the training anatomy and 42.0% and 91.6% in the hold-out anatomy, respectively. Fourteen of 20 physical runs were successful (70% overall), including 80% success for Task B in the hold-out phantom. In silico the predictors detected 99.3-100.0% of failures with false-alarm rates of 0.8-6.7%. During in vitro evaluation, predicted risk increased before failed episodes, but elevated probabilities during some successful runs showed reduced calibration after transfer. These results demonstrate the feasibility of autonomous cerebral venous access and show how online failure prediction could support human oversight, while also identifying anatomical generalization and sim-to-real calibration as priorities before preclinical translation.

</details>


### [88] [RATE: Risk-Aware Tactile Encoding for Contact-rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03538)
*Yuyao Jiang,Haichao Liu,Jiarui Zheng,Zihan Ding,Weihao Yuan,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Risk-Aware Tactile Encoding (RATE)，学习编码任务条件下交互风险的触觉表征，通过历史条件预测与警报监督实现，并以轻量残差适配器与常规触觉特征互补，在仿真与真实任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉表征学习在机器人操作中进展显著，但相似触觉观测可能源于风险含义截然不同的交互条件（传感器噪声、任务必需的变化、新出现的非期望接触）。缺乏具身任务上下文的风险信息时，这些情况对下游策略存在歧义，导致对良性变化的过度修正或对真正风险接触的响应延迟。

Method: RATE（Risk-Aware Tactile Encoding）：以历史条件预测（history-conditioned prediction）捕获交互上下文，以警报监督（alert supervision）将上下文与任务条件风险关联；学习到的风险感知表征通过轻量残差适配器（lightweight residual adapter）补充常规触觉特征。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界中均取得任务成功率的显著提升；受控消融实验验证了警报引导学习与预测性时序建模的互补收益。

Conclusion: 将任务条件下的交互风险显式编码进触觉表征，可缓解相似观测带来的歧义，并作为常规触觉特征的补充提升机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is particularly valuable for contact-rich robotic manipulation. Recent work has made substantial progress in tactile representation learning for robotic manipulation. However, similar tactile observations can arise from interaction conditions with very different task-risk implications, such as sensor noise, task-necessary variations, and emerging undesirable contact. Without context-grounded risk information, these cases can be ambiguous to downstream policies, leading to unnecessary corrections to benign variations or delayed responses to genuinely risky contact. To address this limitation, we propose Risk-Aware Tactile Encoding (RATE), which learns tactile representations that encode task-conditioned interaction risk. Specifically, history-conditioned prediction captures interaction context, while alert supervision associates this context with task-conditioned risk. The learned risk-aware representation complements conventional tactile features through a lightweight residual adapter. Experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate substantial improvements in task success, with controlled ablations confirming the complementary benefits of alert-guided learning and predictive temporal modeling.

</details>


### [89] [A Unified Framework for Empowerment and Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03563)
*Wooyoung Chung,Tristan Shah,Volodymyr Makarenko,Stas Tiomkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文重新形式化empowerment目标，使其仅需一阶导数且可用标准MPC求解器优化，可单独使用或与任务代价结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的MPC性能依赖信息量丰富的代价函数，但设计代价函数通常需要大量领域知识。从系统动力学直接导出目标（如empowerment）是一种目标无关的替代方案，但现有empowerment控制器存在两个关键局限：1）empowerment用与执行控制策略不同的虚拟探测策略估计，模糊了其与最终行为的联系；2）计算通常需要动力学的二阶导数，限制了与标准MPC方法的兼容性。

Method: 作者提出用单一策略同时进行探测和动作，使empowerment目标直接关联到执行行为。由此得到的目标准则仅依赖一阶导数，可用标准MPC求解器优化，既可单独优化也可与任务特定代价组合。

Result: 在经典控制任务上评估，将empowerment与任务代价结合的成功率达到或超过单独使用任一目标的效果。

Conclusion: 该形式化为标准MPC与内在动机控制之间提供了实用的桥梁。

Abstract: Sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) is a powerful approach to trajectory optimization, but its performance depends on an informative cost function that often requires substantial domain knowledge to design. An alternative is to derive objectives directly from the system dynamics. Empowerment, defined as the channel capacity between an agent's inputs and its future state, provides such a goal-agnostic objective and has been shown to produce useful behaviors across a range of domains. However, existing empowerment-based controllers have two key limitations: empowerment is estimated using a virtual probing policy distinct from the executed control policy, obscuring its connection to the resulting behavior; and its computation typically requires second-order derivatives of the dynamics, limiting compatibility with standard MPC methods. We formulate empowerment using a single policy for both probing and acting, directly linking the empowerment objective to the executed behavior. The resulting objective requires only first-order derivatives and can be optimized with standard MPC solvers, either alone or in combination with a task-specific cost. We evaluate the proposed approach on classical control tasks and show that combining empowerment with task costs matches or exceeds the success rate achieved by either objective alone. Our formulation provides a practical bridge between standard MPC and intrinsically motivated control.

</details>


### [90] [AVL-JEPA: Preventing Causal Dynamics Information Collapse In Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03587)
*Yikang Qiao,Ling Zhang,Ziying Song,Duan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出现有JEPA存在"因果动态信息坍缩"问题，并提出AVL方法防止该坍缩，在多个机器人控制任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA通过预测未来潜在表征而非重构观测来构建世界模型，能聚焦高层语义动态，但可能保留高维视觉信息而丢弃动作的物理后果信息，即"因果动态信息坍缩"，这会损害规划所需的动态信息。

Method: 提出动作锚定视觉不变性潜在表征（AVL）：1）将执行的动作作为辅助动态锚，促使模型保留动态信息；2）设计视觉不变性通路，在不丢弃动态信息的前提下对齐扰动与干净潜在预测，迫使模型充分理解和利用因果动态信息。

Result: 在TwoRoom、PushT、OGBench Cube和Reacher四个机器人控制任务上，AVL显著提升视觉扰动下的成功率，同时保持干净环境性能；并通过物理后果对齐、干净-噪声动态一致性及目标转移子空间擦除的因果效应评估进一步验证。

Conclusion: 与规划因果相关的动态信息在AVL下得以避免坍缩并被有效保留。

Abstract: Joint embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) predict future latent representations without reconstructing observations, enabling world models to focus on high-level semantic dynamics. However, a JEPA can preserve high dimensional visual information while discarding information about the physical consequences of actions. We call this failure mode causal dynamics information collapse and propose action-grounded vision-invariance latent (AVL) to prevent this collapse. We first use the executed action as an auxiliary dynamics anchor that encourages the model to preserve dynamics information, and then use a vision-invariance pathway which aligns perturbed and clean latent predictions without discarding dynamics information, forcing the model to fully understand and utilize causal dynamics information. We validate AVL on four robotic control tasks (TwoRoom, PushT, OGBench Cube, and Reacher), showing that it substantially improves success rates under visual perturbations while preserving clean-environment performance. We further evaluate physical consequence alignment, clean-noisy dynamics consistency, and the causal effect of targeted transition subspace erasure. Collectively, these results indicate that dynamic information causally relevant to planning is preserved from collapse under AVL.

</details>


### [91] [World Action Learning via Interaction-Centric Spectral Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03607)
*Zhiming Liu,Yikun Miao,Ying Chen,Hongrui Yin,Fangqi Zhu,Xiaoyi Pang,Quanxin Shou,Zhengyang Yan,Haodong Wang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WING框架，通过分离观察者运动与手物交互、并在频域利用低频结构对齐，将第一人称视频中的交互知识迁移到机器人策略，在多个仿真与真实任务上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通用策略依赖大规模真实交互数据，但机器人示范采集成本高、难以规模化；第一人称（自我中心）视频蕴含丰富的人类交互经验与任务语义，然而直接迁移存在两大障碍：一是从帧重建推断的隐动作易被自我相机运动等无关变化主导，二是人类与机器人的行为时序动态存在差异。

Method: WING（World Action Learning via INteraction-Centric Spectral Latent Guidance）框架：先将观察者引起的相机运动与手-物交互解耦，把以交互为中心的成分蒸馏为隐动作；随后基于"跨具身任务语义集中于缓慢变化的时序结构"这一观察，在频谱域中识别自我中心隐动作与机器人行为之间的共享低频成分，并用其引导动作生成。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率99.20%，RoboTwin 2.0上93.80%，RoboCasa-GR1上57.7%；并在四个真实世界操作任务的多种泛化设定下表现强劲。

Conclusion: 以交互为中心的频谱引导为从人类自我中心视频向机器人控制迁移物理交互知识提供了一种有效且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Learning general-purpose robot policies requires large-scale real-world interaction data, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Egocentric videos offer abundant human interaction experience with task-relevant semantics for robotic manipulation, but direct transfer is challenging for two reasons: latent actions inferred from frame reconstruction can be dominated by nuisance variation such as ego-camera motion, and human and robot behaviors often exhibit different temporal dynamics. We propose WING (World Action Learning via INteraction-Centric Spectral Latent Guidance), a framework for transferring interaction knowledge from egocentric videos to robot policies. WING first separates observer-induced motion from hand-object interaction and distills the interaction-centric component into latent actions. It then exploits the observation that cross-embodiment task semantics are concentrated in slowly varying temporal structures, identifying shared low-frequency components between egocentric latent actions and robot behaviors in the spectral domain and using them to guide action generation. WING achieves average success rates of 99.20% on LIBERO, 93.80% on RoboTwin 2.0, and 57.7% on RoboCasa-GR1, and also performs strongly across four real-world manipulation tasks under diverse generalization settings. These results show that interaction-centric spectral guidance provides an effective and scalable way to transfer physical interaction knowledge from human egocentric video to robot control. Project page: https://mikuz12.github.io/wing/

</details>


### [92] [Bridging Frontier Reasoning and Robot Execution: From Autonomous Demonstration Generation to Dense Language Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03615)
*Bosung Kim,Alexander Trevithick,Ruiyi Wang,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出两种互补方法，将前沿模型的推理能力与低延迟本地执行相连接：一是用前沿模型自主生成示范以训练快速本地策略，二是引入多粒度密集语言监督，从而在长时程任务中实现可靠的指令跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型虽有强推理能力，能仅凭少量示范完成机器人操作，但其推理延迟高，无法满足实时机器人控制的需求，因此需要弥合前沿推理与低延迟本地执行之间的差距。
