Method: 方法包含两个互补桥梁：(1) 用前沿模型自主生成示范补充人类示范，并在上下文示例中注入纠错示范片段以提高生成可靠性，训练快速本地策略；部署时用一个执行框架将前沿模型指令与本地策略结合；(2) 在原始、原子、复合三种嵌套粒度上引入密集语言监督，每层附带多方面描述，以增强策略通过语言接口跟随指令的能力。

Result: 随着成功示例在上下文中累积，生成时间与成本下降；在RoboCasa 365和BEHAVIOR-1K的长时程任务中，组合监督在oracle与前沿模型指令下均取得最高性能；在结合语义规划与操作的填字任务中，两种桥梁协同工作的效果得到验证。

Conclusion: 自主示范生成与密集语言监督是互补的两个组件，能够有效连接前沿推理与低延迟本地执行，提升长时程机器人操作的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Recent advances in frontier models enable robot manipulation from only a few demonstrations, but high inference latency limits their use for real-time robot control. To bridge this gap, we study two complementary approaches that connect frontier reasoning with low-latency local execution. First, we use a frontier model to autonomously generate demonstrations that supplement human demonstrations for training a fast local policy. We augment its in-context examples with corrective demonstration segments that show how to recover from physical errors, improving generation reliability. Generation time and cost decrease as successful examples accumulate in context, suggesting a path toward more efficient data collection. At deployment, a harness combines frontier-generated instructions with a fast local policy, enabling efficient execution while preserving the frontier model's ability to guide and correct actions. With low-latency execution delegated to the local policy, the bottleneck shifts to its capacity to reliably follow the frontier model's diverse instructions. Our second bridge introduces dense language supervision across three nested granularities---primitive, atomic, and composite---with multi-aspect descriptions at each level. Across long-horizon tasks in RoboCasa 365 and BEHAVIOR-1K, where instructions change as execution progresses, the combined supervision achieves the highest performance under both oracle and frontier-model instructors, demonstrating more reliable instruction following through the policy's language interface. Finally, we evaluate both bridges together on a crossword task that combines semantic planning and manipulation within a fixed time budget. These results support autonomous demonstration generation and dense language supervision as complementary components for connecting frontier reasoning to low-latency local execution.

</details>


### [93] [LLA-MPC on Embedded Hardware: Rapid Adaptive Control with Thousands of Parallel Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03616)
*Henry Z. Liao,Maitham F. AL-Sunni,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实现了通用化的Look-Back and Look-Ahead自适应模型预测控制（LLA-MPC），一个无需学习的实时自适应系统辨识与控制框架，并在F1TENTH平台上完成实车验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原LLA-MPC框架仅在仿真中验证，且采用固定模型结构。本文希望将其推广到模块化实现，并在计算受限、状态估计含噪的真实平台上检验其可行性。

Method: 提出模块化的动力学与积分器实现；通过实时评估数千个候选模型，在嵌入式计算机上在线辨识轮胎参数；在F1TENTH平台上于受限算力与含噪状态估计条件下开展实验。

Result: 在低摩擦轮胎及变化路面的实验中，LLA-MPC能够完成高速跟踪任务，而固定标称模型则失败。

Conclusion: LLA-MPC作为一种无学习、实时自适应的辨识与控制框架，可在真实嵌入式平台上有效工作，并优于固定标称模型。

Abstract: We present a generalized implementation of Look-Back and Look-Ahead Adaptive Model Predictive Control (LLA-MPC), a learning-free framework for real-time, rapid adaptive system identification and control. The original formulation was demonstrated only in simulation, for autonomous racing with a fixed model structure. Our implementation has modular dynamics and integrators, and we validate it on the F1TENTH platform under constrained computation and noisy state estimation. The system identifies tire parameters online by evaluating thousands of candidate models in real time on an embedded computer. Experiments with low-friction tires across changing surfaces show that LLA-MPC completes high-speed tracking tasks where a fixed nominal model fails. Code, videos, and our related work are available at: https://lla-control.github.io.

</details>


### [94] [CORNAV: Construction-Aware Reasoning for Robot Navigation on Active Worksites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03622)
*Parastoo Ali Pour,Deepak Prakash Kumar,Tommy Zhou,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORNAV：基于蓝图与施工进度的导航框架，用2D CAD图纸和项目进度表提升施工机器人导航成功率至72.2%并消除硬性禁区违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工行业长期面临劳动力短缺、生产率低下（年损失超1.6万亿美元）和高伤害率问题，促使采用自主机器人。然而现有语言引导导航系统仅依赖语义场景理解，缺乏建筑图纸、动态工作进度和安全约束等施工语境，导致永久建筑特征定位不可靠、无法在活跃工地安全导航。

Method: CORNAV无需BIM，仅从2D CAD图纸和项目进度表出发：将建筑蓝图与分层开放词汇3D场景图对齐以支撑物体查询；将项目进度转换为时变导航约束；通过基于LLM的安全模块验证请求，在规划前对危险区域升级处理；最后由A*规划器强制执行强制禁区并优先规避高风险区域。

Result: 在室内办公室和真实施工现场中，蓝图接地将任务成功率从仅语义检索的13.0%提升至72.2%；进度感知消除了所有硬性禁区违规；安全模块能正确拒绝由错误标注项目进度引发的危险请求。

Conclusion: 引入蓝图接地、进度感知与LLM安全校验，可显著提升施工场景下语言引导导航的可靠性与安全性，且无需依赖BIM。

Abstract: The construction industry faces persistent labor shortages, low productivity that costs the global economy over $1.6 trillion annually, and one of the highest injury rates among major industries. These factors motivate the use of autonomous robots to improve efficiency and worker safety. Existing language-grounded navigation systems, however, rely on semantic scene understanding alone and lack access to construction-specific context such as architectural plans, evolving work schedules, and safety constraints. As a result, they localize permanent building features unreliably and cannot safely navigate active jobsites. We present CORNAV, a blueprint-grounded, schedule-aware navigation framework that operates from 2D CAD drawings and project schedules without requiring a Building Information Model. CORNAV aligns architectural blueprints against hierarchical open-vocabulary 3D scene graphs to ground object queries, converts project schedules into time-varying navigation constraints, and validates requests through an LLM-based safety module that escalates hazardous zones before planning. An A* planner then enforces mandatory exclusion zones while preferentially avoiding higher-risk areas. Across an indoor office and a real construction site, blueprint grounding raises task success from 13.0% to 72.2% over semantic retrieval alone, schedule awareness eliminates all hard-zone violations, and the safety module correctly rejects hazardous requests arising from mislabeled project schedules.

</details>


### [95] [EyeRobot 2.0: Active Gaze for Precise Manipulation without Wrist Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03710)
*Kush Hari,Justin Kerr,Nidhya Shivakumar,Samarth Mahapatra,Carmelo Sferrazza,Jiahui Lei,Jitendra Malik,C. Karen Liu,Ken Goldberg,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EyeRobot 2.0 提出以主动注视（AVF）为核心的框架，仅用单个双目相机实现精细双手操作，通过层级式注视伺服与目标选择策略在真实与仿真任务中显著优于被动双目方案，并在腕部相机被遮挡时超过 ego+wrist 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准操作策略通常依赖腕部相机来获得精细视角，但腕部相机视角易被夹持物体遮挡；同时移除腕部相机后仅用被动双目会使真实任务成功率从 52% 降至 27%。作者受人类视觉（主动注视、中央凹式采样）启发，希望仅凭单个双目相机实现精细双手操作并弥补这一性能差距。

Method: 构建 EyeRobot 2.0：通过旋转两个眼部视角将注视中心对准场景中的 3D 注视点，实现 Active Visual Fixation；对所得图像采用中央凹式处理，将更多视觉 token 分配给图像中心以聚焦任务相关特征。注视控制采用层级策略：先以目标物体为条件训练底层注视伺服策略（用稠密几何奖励的 RL），再训练根据任务进度输出注视目标的目标选择器（与 BC 夹爪策略协同训练）。此外将夹爪信息规范化到注视相关的 SE(3) 坐标系，以压缩待学习的动作分布。基于 7 个真实任务与 6 个仿真任务的遥操作数据，进行 1000+ 真实与 1800 仿真试验，与被动双目及 ego+wrist 策略对比。

Result: 仅用被动双目时，标准策略真实成功率从 52% 降至 27%；EyeRobot 2.0 仅用双目即缩小该差距，真实成功率比被动双目高 40%，仿真高 20%。在腕部视角清晰时，EyeRobot 2.0 与 ego+wrist 策略相当（69% vs. 64%）；当夹持物体遮挡腕部相机时，其成功率超过 ego+wrist 两倍以上（48% vs. 22%）。

Conclusion: 主动注视视觉为单双目精细双手操作提供了有效替代腕部相机的途径，能提升遮挡鲁棒性并保持竞争力，同时通过注视相关坐标系简化动作学习。

Abstract: Inspired by human vision, we introduce a framework using active gaze to enable fine-grained bimanual manipulation with only a single stereo camera. EyeRobot 2.0 physically attends to a 3D fixation point in the scene by swiveling two eye viewpoints to center their gaze on it. The resulting images are processed foveally by allocating more visual tokens to the image centers, focusing computation on task-relevant features. Such Active Visual Fixation (AVF) requires carefully coordinated gaze during task execution, which we accomplish hierarchically by first training a low-level gaze servoing policy conditioned on a goal object, then training a target selector which emits fixation goals based on task progress. Both modules are trained with RL on real-world data: the first is trained with a dense geometric reward and the second co-trains with the BC gripper policy which allows it to discover fixation sequences that can resemble a human's fixation sequence while performing the task. EyeRobot 2.0 further takes advantage of fixation by canonicalizing gripper information into a fixation-relative SE(3) frame, which compacts the size of the action distribution to learn. We collect teleoperation data for 7 real-world and 6 simulated tasks, and conduct over 1000 physical and 1800 simulated robot trials comparing EyeRobot 2.0 against passive stereo and ego + wrist camera policies trained on the same data. Removing wrist cameras is costly for standard policies: with only passive stereo, real-world success drops from 52% to 27%. EyeRobot 2.0 closes this gap with only stereo, outperforming passive stereo by 40% in real and 20% in sim. It matches ego + wrist policies when their wrist views are clear (69% vs. 64%), and more than doubles their success when grasped objects occlude the wrist cameras (48% vs. 22%)

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [The Reeb Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03126)
*Erin Chambers,Shankha Shubhra Mukherjee,Katharine Turner*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文定义了Reeb变换（所有单位球方向上的高度函数诱导的Reeb图族），为o-minimal可定义集建立了严格理论，并证明了其在若干情形下的单射性，同时指出三维以上维度的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状沿不同方向切片时的拓扑变化需要一种紧凑表示；Reeb图仅依赖单一方向，因此需要研究涵盖所有方向的Reeb图族（Reeb变换）作为描述子，并明确其表达能力的边界。

Method: 将Reeb变换定义为单位球面上所有方向的高度函数所诱导的Reeb图族；对o-minimal可定义集进行严格处理，重点分析嵌入于R^d的一维分层空间以及R^3中的曲面。

Result: 在多种情形下证明了Reeb变换的单射性，包括R^3中的紧曲面，从而捕捉到唯一重构该曲面所需的基本拓扑特征；但在三维以上维度，Reeb变换不再单射。

Conclusion: Reeb变换对R^3中紧曲面等情形是完备的描述子，可唯一重构；然而其单射性在高于三维时失效，说明该描述子在高维情形存在根本局限。

Abstract: Reeb Transforms offer a compact representation of how shapes in $\mathbb{R}^n$ change when sliced along varying directions. We define the Reeb Transform as the family of Reeb graphs induced by height functions along all directions in the unit sphere. In this paper, we develop a rigorous treatment of Reeb Transforms for o-minimal definable sets, with particular emphasis on one-dimensional stratified spaces embedded in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and on surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$. We establish injectivity of the Reeb Transform in multiple settings, including compact surfaces in $\mathbb{R}^3$, capturing the essential topological features needed to uniquely reconstruct such surfaces.
  However, in dimensions above three, the Reeb Transform ceases to be injective, indicating the limitations of this descriptor in higher-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [97] [Point and Line Nearest-Neighbor Searching in 3-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03451)
*Pankaj K. Agarwal,Esther Ezra,Micha Sharir*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对三维空间中点、直线、线段和三角形等几何对象的最近邻搜索问题的数据结构和算法，性能显著优于此前已知结果，涵盖了线性空间与常数查询时间两个极端情形，并给出了查询时间与空间之间的权衡界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维空间中涉及直线、线段、三角形等对象的最近邻搜索问题此前已有解法，但性能不够理想。作者希望改进这些问题的查询时间与空间复杂度，尤其是在（近）线性存储和快速查询时间这两个极端情形下获得更优结果，并建立查询时间与数据结构规模之间的权衡关系。

Method: 利用三维和四维空间中曲面排列（arrangements of surfaces）的组合与算法结果，特别是作者此前建立的关于此类排列中子结构垂直分解（vertical decompositions）的成果，构造了相应的数据结构和查询算法。例如，对于在 n 个点中查询直线或线段的最近邻，给出线性规模、O*(n^{1/2}) 查询时间的数据结构；并给出 O*(n^4) 规模、O*(1) 查询时间的数据结构。对于反问题（在 n 条直线、线段或三角形中查询点的最近邻），给出线性规模、O*(n^{2/3}) 查询时间的数据结构。

Result: 针对三维空间中点、直线、线段、三角形的最近邻搜索问题，所得查询时间和空间复杂度均显著优于此前最好结果。具体包括：在 n 个点中查询直线/线段的最近邻，线性空间下查询时间为 O*(n^{1/2})，或 O*(n^4) 空间下查询时间为 O*(1)；在 n 条直线/线段/三角形中查询点的最近邻，线性空间下查询时间为 O*(n^{2/3})。同时获得了查询时间与空间规模之间的权衡界限。

Conclusion: 本文为三维空间中多类几何对象的最近邻搜索问题提供了性能显著提升的数据结构，覆盖线性存储与快速查询两个极端情形，并建立了查询时间与空间之间的权衡关系。这些结果依赖于三维、四维空间曲面排列的组合与算法进展，特别是子结构垂直分解的相关成果。

Abstract: This paper presents data structures for nearest-neighbor (NN) searching problems involving points, lines, segments, and triangles in 3-space, achieving significantly better performance than the previously best-known results for these problems. For example, we present a linear-size data structure for answering NN queries with lines or segments amid $n$ points in 3-space with $O^*(n^{1/2})$ query time (where the $O^*(\cdot)$ notation hides subpolynomial factors). We also present a data structure of $O^*(n^4)$ size that answers such queries in $O^*(1)$ time. For the converse problem, in which we seek the nearest neighbor of a query point amid $n$ lines, segments, or triangles in 3-space, we present a linear-size data structure with $O^*(n^{2/3})$ query time. These results constitute a significant improvement over previous solutions. We obtain improved solutions for the two extreme regimes of (near-)linear storage and of fast query time. These results also yield trade-off bounds between the query time and the size of the data structure. Our results rely on several combinatorial and algorithmic results on arrangements of surfaces in 3-space and 4-space, particularly on recent results on vertical decompositions of substructures in such arrangements established by the authors.

</details>

### [98] [Generation of UL-Axions by a Kerr Black Hole. Part III: The fate of its Axion Wind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02250)
*Guy Pelletier*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究超大质量Kerr黑洞磁层产生的轴子风与周围等离子体/氢气的相互作用，发现仅引力Tcherenkov-Vavilov型流不稳定性可有效运作，可能开启轴子天文学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前两篇文章已论述活动星系核/类星体核区的超大质量Kerr黑洞可于磁层中产生大量轴子。本文（第三部分）旨在探究这些轴子风与最近AGN周围重子物质及轴子暗物质相互作用是否产生可观测动力学效应。

Method: 考虑与宇宙学中作为暗物质主要成分的超轻轴子（形成玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚、支撑巨大薛定谔波）相同的轴子；分析轴子风与宿主星系及晕内等离子体和氢气可能的相互作用机制；识别出可有效运作的引力Tcherenkov-Vavilov型声波流不稳定性。

Result: 该不稳定性在宿主星系中心区域至秒差距尺度上足够快地有效运作，使氢气与等离子体声波的Lyman-α诊断及21 cm超精细发射探测成为可能；轴子风散射极弱，可远距离穿越其他星系及其晕甚至更远直至量子热化，从而可能通过在地球上将其转换为伽马射线实现直接探测，开启局域宇宙的轴子天文学。Messier 87的参数表明它是同时开展伽马射线转换和核球Lyman-α诊断研究的良好候选体。

Conclusion: 尽管相互作用机制颇具吸引力，实际仅引力Tcherenkov-Vavilov型流不稳定性可有效运作。该机制支持多信使诊断，并因轴子风散射极弱而可能实现直接探测，Messier 87是开展此类轴子天文学研究的理想候选目标。

Abstract: In two previous articles, we have explained how super-massive Kerr Black Holes in the nucleus of an active galaxy or quasar can generate a large amount of axion particles in their magnetosphere. In that third part, we explore the possibility of observable dynamics resulting from some interaction of an axion wind with the baryonic matter and the axionic Dark Matter in the surroundings of the closest AGNs. The axions that we have considered are the same as those Ultra Light Axions favored in cosmology as the main content of Dark Matter and which form Bose-Einstein Condensates, supporting a gigantic Schrödinger wave. In the paper we investigate what can be the nature of the interaction of the axion wind with the plasma and the hydrogen gas in the host galaxy and its halo. Despite the intriguing nature of a possible interaction, we show that only a kind of gravitational Tcherenkov-Vavilov effect can be efficiently operating with acoustic waves through a streaming instability. The instability is fast enough to operate efficiently in the central region of the host galaxy up to parsec scales and makes Lyman-alpha diagnostics of the acoustic waves of hydrogen gas and plasma possible, as well as the detection its hyperfine emission at 21 cm. However it turns out that scattering of the axion wind is very long and thus these winds could travel over very large distances and cross other galaxies. And then the wind travels without significant loss along its halo and even beyond up to quantum thermalization, which makes possible direct detection of these axions by conversion into gamma-rays on Earth, opening a possible axion astronomy in the local Universe. The parameters of Messier 87 indicate that it is a good candidate for developing these investigations both through the conversion of axions into gamma-rays and Lyman alpha diagnosis in the bulge.

</details>


### [99] [Testing internal dynamics in neutron stars with radio pulsar timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02291)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过比较"等值反向力矩"与"独立力矩"两类模型，利用脉冲星相位观测检验角速度涨落的平稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星自转变化可能源于内部角动量交换（平稳自旋噪声），也可能源于独立力矩导致总角动量扩散（非平稳自旋噪声），需通过观测判别其平稳性。

Method: 构建并比较两类模型：无线辐射壳层与内部超流体的耦合模型，分别对应等值反向力矩与独立力矩；利用三颗孤立脉冲星的可测计时噪声相位数据进行贝叶斯推断。

Result: 三颗脉冲星推断结果各异：PSR J1359-6038轻度偏好独立力矩，几率比为700:1；PSR B1937+21两模型无明显偏好；Vela在glitch前区间因超额散射与未解释的残差结构而难以定论。

Conclusion: 脉冲星自旋噪声的平稳性因源而异，独立力矩模型在部分源中获得一定支持，但样本有限且个别源存在系统性干扰，尚无法给出普遍结论。

Abstract: Rotational variations in pulsars reflect either internal angular momentum exchange via equal and opposite torques (stationary spin noise) or independent torques causing diffusive changes in total angular momentum (nonstationary spin noise). We compare two models of a radio-emitting crust and interior superfluid to test the stationarity of angular velocity fluctuations through observed pulsar phases. We present results for three isolated pulsars with measurable timing noise, demonstrating distinct inference outcomes. For PSR J1359-6038, the data mildly favor independent torques over equal-and-opposite torques, with an odds ratio of seven hundred to one. For PSR B1937+21, the analysis shows no clear preference between the models. For Vela in a pre-glitch interval, excess scatter and unexplained residual structure limit the interpretation.

</details>


### [100] [Neutrino flavor instabilities intermediate between slow and fast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02332)
*Nicolas Viaux,Lucas Johns*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究超新星与中子星并合中混合型中微子味不稳定性的生长率，通过二维相图揭示纯型不稳定之间的临界曲线、交叉与相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中实际发生的味不稳定性往往并非单一纯类型，而是慢、快、碰撞型（SFI/FFI/CFI）的混合，既有研究多局限于理想化极限，缺乏对混合型行为的系统刻画。

Method: 在二维参数空间构建"相图"，两轴分别代表不同纯类型的不稳定性，分析其生长率，考察不同混合比例下的行为；并定性分析生长率谱γ_k，特别是最快增长傅里叶模k_max的变化。

Result: 发现连接各纯型不稳定起始点的临界曲线；观察到生长率谱中的交叉（crossover）现象及k_max上的相变；明确区分空间平均（k=0）不稳定与味波（k≠0）不稳定的不同行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解自然界中真实发生的（非理想极限下的）味不稳定性迈出一步，揭示了混合型不稳定的相图结构与谱特征。

Abstract: The neutrino flavor composition in supernovae and neutron star mergers is shaped by flavor instabilities, which are commonly classified into three types: slow (SFI), fast (FFI), and collisional (CFI). The focus here is on mixed-type instabilities combining attributes of the standard varieties. We examine instability growth rates in two-dimensional parameter spaces (``phase diagrams'') whose axes correspond to instabilities of pure type. We find critical curves connecting the pure-type instability onset points. We also exhibit qualitative trends in the growth-rate spectra $γ_{\bm{k}}$, emphasizing the occurrence of crossovers and phase transitions in the fastest-growing Fourier mode $k_{\textrm{max}}$ and drawing attention to the distinction between spatial-mean ($\bm{k} = 0$) and flavor-wave ($\bm{k} \neq 0$) instabilities. Our paper is a step toward understanding flavor instabilities as they actually occur in nature rather than in idealized limits.

</details>


### [101] [Extending the Pulsar radio population through Quantum Vacuum Cherenkov Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02429)
*Tales A. O. Gomes,Sergei V. Bulanov,Jaziel G. Coelho,Martin Jirka,Alexander J. Macleod,J. A. Rueda*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出量子真空中的切伦科夫辐射（QED效应）是一种新的对产生通道，可在传统CR/RICS机制被强烈抑制时仍维持脉冲星活动，从而在P-Ṗ图中给出扩展的死亡线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分射电脉冲星位于P-Ṗ图中"死亡谷"内或其下方，这对基于曲率辐射（CR）和共振逆康普顿散射（RICS）的标准对级联模型构成挑战，需要新机制解释其射电辐射。

Method: 在真空间隙模型框架下，引入由真空极化导致的强场QED现象——量子真空切伦科夫辐射，分析其切伦科夫光子发射的加速长度尺度及随后的光子平均自由程，评估其快速转化为e⁺e⁻对的可行性。

Result: 切伦科夫光子的加速长度尺度和平均自由程足够短，可快速转化为e⁺e⁻对；该通道作为互补机制，即使在经典机制被强烈抑制的区间仍然有效，并在P-Ṗ图中给出新的死亡线，与CR、RICS死亡谷部分重叠且向外延伸。

Conclusion: 量子真空切伦科夫辐射拓宽了射电脉冲星的可行参数空间，为长周期中子星的相干射电辐射提供物理依据，并为利用SKQ等下一代射电设施检验非线性QED提供新窗口。

Abstract: The presence of some radio pulsars within and below the so-called ``death valley'' in the $P$--$\dot{P}$ diagram challenges the standard pair-cascade models based on curvature radiation (CR) and resonant inverse Compton scattering (RICS) in neutron star polar caps. We show that Cherenkov radiation from the quantum vacuum, a strong-field quantum electrodynamics (QED) phenomenon enabled by vacuum polarization, constitutes a distinct pair-creation channel within the vacuum gap model. Within this model, the acceleration length scale for Cherenkov photon emission and the subsequent photon mean free path are sufficiently short to ensure rapid conversion into $e^+e^-$ pairs. This process acts as a complementary pair-creation channel, remaining viable even in regimes where classical mechanisms become heavily suppressed. This leads to a new set of death lines in the $P$--$\dot{P}$ diagram that partially overlap with and extend beyond the CR and RICS death valleys, enlarging the viable parameter space for radio-emitting neutron stars. The result provides a physical basis for coherent radio emission from long-period neutron stars previously considered inactive, and offers a new window for testing non-linear QED with next-generation radio facilities such as the Square Kilometre Array.

</details>


### [102] [Suppression of spiral density waves in collisionless accretion flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02475)
*Stephen Majeski,Matthew W. Kunz,Jason Dexter*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文用动理学与流体模型研究无碰撞剪切片中螺旋密度波的传播、阻尼和激发，发现微观等离子体不稳定性引起的黏性阻尼至关重要，导致发射区变异性降低、图案速度减慢，或改善EHT观测与理论的吻合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 螺旋密度波普遍存在于较差转动流中，其在低光度黑洞吸积流全局模拟中激发螺旋密度图案并影响发射区形态与观测变异性。然而以往研究均假设吸积等离子体始终处于局部热力学平衡（LTE），而实际热稀薄吸积流应远离LTE，需按无碰撞条件建模。

Method: 结合动理学与流体模型，在无碰撞剪切片（shearing sheet）中描述螺旋密度波的传播、阻尼与激发，以更准确反映低光度吸积流的等离子体条件，而非采用LTE假设。

Result: 研究发现微观等离子体不稳定性对螺旋密度波行为起关键作用：其诱导的弱粒子散射产生可观的黏性阻尼。观测上的体现包括发射区变异性减小，可能改善EHT观测与模拟/理论的吻合度；同时发射区图案速度相比采用LTE的模拟有所降低。

Conclusion: 无碰撞、非LTE处理揭示了黏性阻尼对螺旋密度波的决定性影响，其结果对EHT观测解释及未来EHT动态影像的解读具有重要意义。

Abstract: Spiral density waves are ubiquitous phenomena in differentially rotating flows. They are non-modal, non-axisymmetric perturbations that, due to shear in the background flow, possess time-evolving wavenumbers, frequencies, and amplitudes. In global simulations of optically thin, low-luminosity black-hole accretion flows, the excitation of such waves leads to spiral patterns in the plasma density that influence the morphology of the emission region. As such, the observed variabilities of such accretion flows are expected to be affected by the behavior of spiral density waves. Thus far, investigations of spiral density waves in accretion flows have exclusively used the assumption that the accretion-flow plasmas are in local thermodynamic equilibrium (LTE) at all times. In reality, hot and diffuse accretion-flow plasmas are expected to deviate far from LTE, and should be modeled accordingly. Here, we employ a combination of kinetic and fluid models to describe spiral density wave propagation, damping, and excitation in a collisionless shearing sheet, which more accurately represents the plasma conditions of low-luminosity accretion flows than do models that assume LTE. We find that micro-physical plasma instabilities are especially important in determining how spiral density waves behave, because the weak particle scattering that they induce leads to appreciable viscous damping. Observational implications of these results include reduced emission-region variability, which may improve agreement between Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) observations and simulation/theory, as well as a reduction in emission-region pattern speeds compared to simulations employing LTE, which bears on the interpretation of eventual EHT movies.

</details>


### [103] [A Refined Analytic Oblate--Schwarzschild Model for Thermal X-Ray Pulse Profiles of Rotating Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02653)
*Xi Yang,Tong Zhao,Renxin Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文在扁球-Schwarzschild近似下推导了修正的解析光弯曲通量表达式，用于快速、精确地拟合旋转中子星热X射线脉冲轮廓，以约束质量与半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eXTP等增强型X射线定时与偏振任务对脉冲轮廓精度要求提高，需要既保留解析计算效率、又更准确的引力光弯曲公式。

Method: 在扁球-Schwarzschild（OS）近似下，将弯曲、传播延迟与透镜效应近似同球形表面结合，推出修正解析通量表达式；以300 Hz双热斑、各向同性黑体辐射为算例，选取三种表面偏心率（自由设定值、均匀旋转牛顿流体的Maclaurin关系、旋转中子星表面拟合），并与数值Schwarzschild及二阶Hartle-Thorne射线追踪结果对比。

Result: 修正OS轮廓相对Hartle-Thorne参考的RMS通量差为1.76%–1.98%，而球对称Schwarzschild加多普勒（S+D）模型为6.50%–21.39%（均以相位平均参考通量归一）；Schwarzschild与Hartle-Thorne射线追踪之差保持在1.1%以下。

Conclusion: 该对比将解析光子传输误差与表面形变及旋转外部时空效应分离；在所考察构型中，考虑扁率可显著提升与射线追踪的一致性，同时保持解析计算的速度并明确揭示轮廓变化的物理来源。

Abstract: Thermal X-ray pulse profiles constrain neutron-star masses and radii through the effects of gravitational light bending and rotation on surface emission. The precision anticipated from the enhanced X-ray Timing and Polarimetry (eXTP) mission motivates more accurate analytic light-bending formulae that retain the efficiency needed for repeated model evaluations. We derive a refined analytic flux expression within the oblate--Schwarzschild (OS) approximation that combines bending, propagation-delay, and lensing approximations with a spheroidal surface. We compare its profiles with numerical Schwarzschild and second-order Hartle--Thorne ray tracing on the same spheroidal surfaces. The comparisons use a selected two-spot configuration at 300 Hz with isotropic blackbody emission and three choices for surface eccentricity: a freely specified value, the Maclaurin relation for a uniformly rotating Newtonian fluid, and a fit to rotating-neutron-star surfaces. Across these cases, the refined OS profiles have RMS flux differences of $1.76-1.98\%$ relative to the Hartle--Thorne reference, compared with $6.50-21.39\%$ for the spherical Schwarzschild-plus-Doppler (S+D) model. All differences are normalized to the phase-averaged reference flux. The corresponding differences between Schwarzschild and Hartle--Thorne ray tracing remain below $1.1\%$. These comparisons separate errors in analytic photon transfer from the effects of surface deformation and the rotating exterior spacetime. For the configurations examined, accounting for oblateness substantially improves agreement with ray tracing. The model retains the speed of an analytic calculation while making the physical origin of profile changes explicit.

</details>


### [104] [The Structure in the Structure Function of Black Hole Light Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02729)
*Daniel C. M. Palumbo,Alejandro Cárdenas-Avendaño,Lena Murchikova*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过构建约12年、230 GHz的异质Sgr A$^\ast$光变数据集，在解析Matérn协方差、透镜化合成薄吸积流电影和72个GRMHD模拟三个框架下研究其结构函数，发现MAD模型结构函数更平滑、斜率更陡，且观测在5–15分钟尺度上更接近MAD趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可实现事件视界尺度成像的两个黑洞中，只有银心Sgr A$^\ast$表现出亚小时光变，因此其内吸积流光变曲线对时空与等离子体的时域研究尤为宝贵；而观测采样节奏不固定，常用结构函数刻画光变性质，故需系统研究其结构函数。

Method: 汇编约12年、230 GHz下由不同仪器、持续时间和采样节奏获得的异质Sgr A$^\ast$光变数据，并在三个框架下研究结构函数：1）基于Matérn协方差决定的傅里叶谱的平稳随机过程解析预测；2）用Matérn协方差近似时空、光学薄合成吸积流的透镜化电影进行结构函数研究；3）分析72个广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟的结构函数库。

Result: 磁阻吸积盘（MAD）模型通常比标准与正常演化（SANE）模型产生更平滑、斜率更陡的结构函数；在5–15分钟时间尺度上，Sgr A$^\ast$的观测结构函数斜率更接近MAD趋势；但两类模拟在所有亚小时时间尺度上通常都比数据变化更剧烈。

Conclusion: 结构函数可作为区分MAD与SANE吸积模型的有效统计工具；观测到的Sgr A$^\ast$亚小时变率斜率支持MAD类模型，但模拟整体变率偏强，提示模拟与观测之间仍需进一步校准。

Abstract: Of the two black holes, M87$^\ast$ and Sgr A$^\ast$, for which event-horizon-scale imaging is currently achievable, only Sgr A$^\ast$ at our Galactic Center exhibits sub-hour variability, making light curve observations of the inner accretion flow especially valuable for time-domain studies of the spacetime and the surrounding plasma. Observed light curves are often sampled with variable cadence, so a structure function is often used to quantify their variability properties. In this work, we assemble a heterogeneous Sgr A$^\ast$ light curve data set from discrete observations acquired over $\sim12$ years {at 230 GHz}, with different instruments, durations, and cadences, and study its structure function in three frameworks. First, we consider analytic predictions for the features of structure functions of stationary random processes with Fourier spectra dictated by the Matérn covariance. We then study the structure functions resulting from lensed movies of synthetic optically thin accretion flows approximated with Matérn covariance in both space and time, and identify which features in the structure function can be attributed to the black-hole geometry. Lastly, we examine the structure functions of a library of $72$ general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations. We find that, in general, magnetically arrested disk (MAD) models produce smoother structure functions with steeper slopes than standard and normal evolution (SANE) counterparts. On time scales of $5-15$ minutes, the observed structure function slope of Sgr A$^\ast$ more closely aligns with the MAD trend, while both simulation types are generally more variable than the data on all sub-hour timescales.

</details>


### [105] [Study of the Ultra-Long GRB 220627A: Exploring a Possible Blue Supergiant Collapsar Origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02889)
*Ayush Garg,Amit Shukla,Dorottya Szécsi,G. C. Anupama*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对超长伽马射线暴GRB 220627A进行时间分辨光谱研究，发现其光谱演化类似磁星候选体，但能量学约束支持蓝超巨星塌缩星起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长伽马射线暴（ul-GRB）持续时间长达10^3-10^4秒，要求中心引擎长时间活动，对标准塌缩星模型构成挑战。典型GRB由于持续时间短难以解析其瞬时光谱演化，而ul-GRB提供了在延展时间尺度上研究光谱演化的独特机会。

Method: 采用基于HOP算法的分割方法确定最佳时间分辨光谱区间，对GRB 220627A（E_iso~10^54 erg，T_90>1000 s，含两个亮发射段、间隔约600 s静默期）进行Fermi-GBM时间分辨光谱分析，并使用Comptonized幂律（CPL）模型拟合约10 keV-40 MeV的瞬时发射。

Result: CPL模型在所有时间尺度上均能很好地描述瞬时发射。光谱显示从第一发射段的硬、慢冷却同步辐射谱（α_I=-0.70）演变为第二发射段的软、快冷却同步辐射谱（α_II=-1.29），该演化与磁星候选体GRB 091024A相似。但GRB 220627A的极端各向同性等效能超过磁星自转减慢所能提供的最大能量，强烈支持蓝超巨星塌缩星起源。

Conclusion: ul-GRB可表现出类似磁星的光谱演化，却由蓝超巨星塌缩星驱动；其长时间活动与多发射段结构可由延展恒星包层中的持续吸积自然解释。仅凭光谱演化存在前身星简并性，需结合时间分辨光谱与能量学约束来区分前身星情景并理解ul-GRB的物理起源。

Abstract: Ultra-long gamma-ray bursts (ul-GRBs) challenge standard collapsar models because their extreme durations require central engines active for $\sim10^{3}-10^{4}$ s. While the prompt spectral evolution of typical GRBs is difficult to resolve due to their short durations, ul-GRBs provide a unique opportunity to study spectral evolution on extended timescales. We present a time-resolved spectroscopic study of GRB 220627A, one of the most luminous ul-GRBs known ($E_{\rm iso}\sim10^{54}$ erg, $T_{90}>1000$ s), consisting of two bright emission episodes separated by $\sim600$ s quiescent interval. Using a novel HOP algorithm-based segmentation method, we identify optimal time intervals for time-resolved spectroscopy. A Comptonized power-law (CPL) model well describes the prompt emission ($\sim 10$ keV-40 MeV) across all timescales. Fermi-GBM spectroscopy reveals a transition from a hard, slow-cooling synchrotron spectrum ($α_{\rm I}=-0.70$) during episode-I to a softer, fast-cooling synchrotron spectrum ($α_{\rm II}=-1.29$) during episode-II. This evolution closely resembles that of the magnetar-candidate GRB 091024A, highlighting a potential progenitor degeneracy based solely on spectral evolution. However, the extreme isotropic-equivalent energy exceeds the maximum energy available from magnetar spin-down, strongly favouring a blue supergiant (BSG) collapsar origin. The prolonged activity and multi-episode structure are naturally explained by sustained accretion through an extended stellar envelope. Our results demonstrate that ul-GRBs can display magnetar-like spectral evolution while being powered by BSG collapsars. GRB 220627A highlights the need for combining time-resolved spectroscopy with energetic constraints to distinguish between progenitor scenarios and understand the physical origin of ul-GRBs.

</details>


### [106] [Candidate X-ray Counterparts of Ultra-High-Energy Emission of the Galactic Microquasar V4641 Sgr Revealed by Einstein Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02962)
*Yi-Heng Chi,Shao-Qiang Xi,Ping Zhou,Hua Feng,Ruo-Yu Liu,Songzhan Chen,Naomi Tsuji,Caterina Tresoldi,Gabriele Ponti,Zhen Cao,Yong Chen,Shumei Jia,Chi-Chuan Jin,Chengkui Li,Yuan Liu,Matthew Lundy,Kaya Mori,Haiwu Pan,Lian Tao,Junfeng Wang,Weimin Yuan,Juan Zhang,Haisheng Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用爱因斯坦探针FXT在微类星体V4641 Sgr南北TeVγ射线区发现空间重合的弥漫X射线结构，距源约20–40 pc，延伸数角分，单结通量约1–3×10⁻¹³ erg s⁻¹ cm⁻²、光子指数Γ≈2–3。单区轻子模型给出磁场约1–2 μG；若TeV–PeV仅部分来自逆康普顿散射、其余为强子过程，则允许更强磁场。观测支持数十秒差距尺度的相对论电子存在，但超高能γ射线起源仍未确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微类星体被视为潜在PeVatron，近期观测到其延展结构发射超高能γ射线；然而对这类延展结构的多波段研究不足，尤其缺乏X射线观测证据，限制了对超高能宇宙线加速、输运与辐射机制的理解。

Method: 使用爱因斯坦探针（Einstein Probe）搭载的跟随X射线望远镜（FXT）对微类星体V4641 Sgr南北TeVγ射线发射区进行观测，搜寻空间重合的弥漫X射线结构，并对各X射线结进行通量、光子指数及空间尺度测量；进而采用单区轻子模型估计磁场强度，并讨论轻子与强子混合解释。

Result: 发现与TeVγ射线区空间重合的弥漫X射线结构，位于距V4641 Sgr约20–40 pc处，延伸数角分。单个结在0.5–8 keV的通量约1–3×10⁻¹³ erg s⁻¹ cm⁻²，光子指数Γ≈2–3。单区轻子解释下，弱X射线发射对应磁场约1–2 μG；若仅部分TeV–PeV发射由逆康普顿散射产生、其余来自强子相互作用，则允许更强磁场。

Conclusion: EP-FXT观测支持在数十秒差距尺度上存在相对论电子，为微类星体延展结构的多波段研究提供了关键X射线证据；但超高能γ射线发射的起源（轻子或强子主导）仍未确定。

Abstract: Microquasars have emerged as promising PeVatron candidates capable of accelerating particles to PeV energies, with recent observations revealing ultra-high-energy (UHE) $γ$-ray emission from extended structures associated with these systems. Multi-wavelength studies of such extended structures are crucial for understanding the acceleration, transport, and radiation processes of UHE cosmic rays (CRs), yet observational evidence remains limited. Here, we report the discovery of diffuse X-ray structures spatially coincident with the TeV $γ$-ray emission regions to the north and south of the microquasar V4641 Sgr, using the Follow-up X-ray Telescope (FXT) onboard the Einstein Probe. We identify X-ray structures located $\sim20$-$40$ pc from V4641 Sgr, extending over several arcminutes. These structures exhibit X-ray fluxes of $\sim1$-$3\times10^{-13}$ erg s$^{-1}$ cm$^{-2}$ and photon indices of $Γ\sim2$-3 in 0.5-8 keV per individual knot. Under the one-zone leptonic interpretation, the weak X-ray emission implies a magnetic field of order $1-2 μ$G. Stronger fields remain possible if only part of the TeV-PeV emission is produced by inverse Compton scattering, with the remaining emission arising from hadronic interactions. The EP-FXT observations support the presence of relativistic electrons on tens-of-parsec scales, while the origin of the UHE $γ$-ray emission remains open.

</details>


### [107] [XMM-Newton Resolves Parsec-scale X-ray Jets in the PeVatron Microquasar V4641 Sgr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02966)
*Naomi Tsuji,Caterina Tresoldi,Kaya Mori,Hiromasa Suzuki,Matthew Lundy,Dmitry Khangulyan,Samar Safi-Harb,Laura Olivera-Nieto,Felix Aharonian,Shotaro Abe,Zhen Cao,Sabrina Casanova,Maria Chernyakova,Yi-Heng Chi,Stephane Corbel,E. de la Fuente,Hua Feng,Tatsuki Fujiwara,Noa Grollimund,Dezhi Huang,Yoshiyuki Inoue,Ryohei Kanazashi,Shigeo S. Kimura,Marianne Lemoine-Goumard,Jonathan Mackey,Alessio Marino,Samaresh Mondal,Kai Morita,Maxime Parra,Gabriele Ponti,Brian Reville,Megumi Shidatsu,Shunya Takekawa,Michelle Tsirou,Xiaojie Wang,Heshou Zhang,Ping Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用XMM-Newton对微类星体V4641 Sgr静止期的伽马射线发射区进行X射线巡测，首次发现沿射流轴延伸数十秒差距的X射线结结构（n1、s1），其非热辐射特征表明为同步辐射起源，并支持轻子起源的PeV伽马射线机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V4641 Sgr近日被确认为银河系最强PeV宇宙线加速器之一（探测到能量达0.8 PeV、尺度约1度的伽马射线发射）。为探究其伽马射线发射区的多波段对应体与辐射机制，作者在静止期开展X射线观测。

Method: 使用XMM-Newton对V4641 Sgr的伽马射线发射区进行静止期X射线巡测，分析X射线形态与能谱（光子指数、能流），并结合射电射流轴方位进行空间对齐比对，开展X射线至伽马射线的多波段建模。

Result: 发现沿射电射流轴、位于V4641 Sgr南北两侧的延伸X射线发射，首次揭示延伸数十秒差距的X射线射流结构。北、南结状结构n1与s1距中心双星约20 pc，并与伽马射线发射空间重合。各结光子指数Γ_X=1.9±0.1_stat±0.5_syst，2–10 keV能流约(2–4)×10^-13 erg cm^-2 s^-1，推测为非热同步辐射。X射线发射较伽马射线区更致密，故轻子起源更受青睐但强子起源无法排除；建模给出磁场强度仅3–5 μG。

Conclusion: 新发现的X射线结可能是粒子加速位点，如V4641 Sgr驱动的射流终止或（再）准直区，或类似SS 433/W50外射流结的下游结构。该发现提示沿射流的延伸X射线发射可能是PeVatron微类星体中此前被忽视的关键特征。

Abstract: The microquasar V4641 Sagittarii (V4641 Sgr) has recently been established as one of the most powerful Galactic PeV cosmic-ray accelerators (PeVatrons) through the detection of degree-scale gamma-ray emission to 0.8 PeV. Using XMM-Newton, we performed an X-ray survey of its gamma-ray-emitting regions during the quiescent phase. We discovered extended X-ray emission to the north and south of V4641 Sgr, aligned with the radio-jet axis, revealing X-ray jet structures extending over several tens of parsecs for the first time. The northern and southern knot-like features, dubbed n1 and s1, are located approximately 20 pc from the central binary and spatially coincident with the gamma-ray emission. The X-ray emission from each knot, characterized by a photon index of $Γ_X = 1.9 \pm 0.1_{\rm stat} \pm 0.5_{\rm syst}$ and an energy flux of $\sim$(2--4)$\times10^{-13}$~erg~cm$^{-2}$~s$^{-1}$ in the 2--10 keV band, is likely non-thermal in origin, indicating synchrotron radiation from accelerated electrons. The morphological coincidence between the X-ray and gamma-ray emission favors a leptonic scenario for the gamma rays, although the X-ray emission is more compact than the gamma-ray-emitting region and the hadronic scenario cannot be excluded. Through X-ray to gamma-ray modeling, we obtain a weak magnetic field strength of 3--5~$μ$G. We propose that the newly detected X-ray knots represent particle-acceleration sites, such as jet termination or (re-)collimation regions powered by V4641 Sgr, or downstream structures analogous to the knots of the outer jets in SS~433/W50. This discovery suggests that extended X-ray emission along jets would be a key signature, yet previously overlooked, in PeVatron microquasars.

</details>


### [108] [Comparing the size evolution of GRB 221009A with afterglow models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02988)
*S. Giarratana,O. S. Salafia,B. Marcote,Z. Paragi,M. Giroletti,G. Ghirlanda,L. Nava,A. van der Horst,J. K. Leung,L. Rhodes,P. G. Edwards,M. Pérez-Torres,J. Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用全球VLBI网在5 GHz对GRB221009A爆发后231–750天进行观测，测得亚毫角秒尺度源尺寸演化，得到幂律斜率a=0.52，并据此约束余辉动力学参数；结果显示文献中基于标准余辉模型的最佳拟合参数均无法在2σ内兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB余辉的尺寸测量可独立探测其动力学、结构与辐射机制，但此前对GRB221009A的模型拟合主要依赖光变曲线和能谱，缺乏直接的空间尺度约束，因此需要VLBI尺寸测量来检验标准余辉模型。

Method: 使用全球VLBI网在5 GHz对GRB221009A爆发后231–750天进行观测，测量亚毫角秒半高全宽尺寸；将新尺寸与文献中5、8 GHz数据以单一幂律∝t^a拟合；并用公开代码VegasAfterglow在均匀星际介质和星风密度剖面两种情形下建模前向激波余辉的尺寸演化。

Result: 尺寸演化幂律斜率为a=0.52^{+0.14}_{-0.12}；模型约束准直校正Sedov比：ISM情形log[(E_jet/n) erg^-1 cm^-3]=54.2^{+0.4}_{-0.5}，星风情形log[(E_jet/A_*)]=51.7^{+0.4}_{-0.2}。文献中可用VegasAfterglow复现的标准余辉模型拟合结果，其最佳参数无一能在2σ内与上述约束兼容，且其预测的尺寸演化与观测不符。

Conclusion: GRB余辉的尺寸测量可作为独立探针，用于检验其动力学、结构与辐射机制；现有基于光变和能谱的标准余辉模型难以同时满足VLBI尺寸观测约束。

Abstract: We present very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) observations of GRB221009A obtained with the global-VLBI network at 5 GHz between 231 and 750 days after the burst. The quality of our observations allowed us to measure the sub-milliarcsecond full-width at half maximum of the source surface brightness distribution with a one-sigma equivalent error of 13% and 30% at 231 and 391 days post-burst, respectively. By fitting the temporal evolution of these new size measurements alongside those previously published in the literature at 5 and 8 GHz with a single power law $\propto t^a$, we find a slope of $a = 0.52^{+0.14}_{-0.12}$. Using the public code VegasAfterglow, we model the 5 GHz size evolution considering the afterglow emission of the forward shock produced by a jet decelerating in either a homogeneous interstellar medium (ISM) or into a surrounding medium with a wind-like density profile. The model constrains the collimation-corrected Sedov ratio, that is, the ratio of the jet kinetic energy $E_\mathrm{jet}$ to the density of the surrounding medium ($n$ in the ISM scenario, or $A_\star$ in the wind scenario), in the range $\log[(E_\mathrm{jet}/n)\,\mathrm{erg}^{-1}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}] = 54.2^{+0.4}_{-0.5}$ for the ISM scenario and $\log[(E_\mathrm{jet}/A_\star)\,\mathrm{erg}^{-1}] = 51.7^{+0.4}_{-0.2}$ for the wind scenario. Among the works in the literature that fit the light curves and spectra of GRB221009A based on the standard afterglow model, and which can be reproduced with VegasAfterglow, none has best-fitting parameters that are compatible with these constraints within $2σ$. Moreover, we show that their predicted size evolution is not compatible with the observed one. Our results highlight the importance of size measurements of GRB afterglows as an independent probe of their dynamics, structure, and emission mechanisms.

</details>


### [109] [The 2015-2017 Large EVPA Rotation in OJ 287: Dominant Propagating Component in a Helical Magnetic Field with Time-Dependent Viewing Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03044)
*Wanchaloem Khwammai,Minchul Kam,Motoki Kino,Sascha Trippe,Hiroshi Nagai,Seiji Kameno,Ilje Cho,Ioannis Myserlis,Jeffrey A. Hodgson,Do-Young Byun,Sang-Sung Lee,Taehyun Jung,Anne Lahteenmaki,Merja Tornikoski*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用KVN、ALMA和Metsähovi在2012—2023年对耀变体OJ 287开展高频射电监测并结合光学偏振数据，发现2015—2017年出现一次幅度>~300°的大幅平滑EVPA旋转，伴随37—343 GHz系列射电耀发；ALMA 91.5 GHz光变拟合给出的变光性多普勒因子约为8—11且无系统性增长，表明增强的相对论束流效应并非耀发包络上升的主因，该事件源于新抛射扰动在螺旋磁场结构内传播时的暂时主导发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀变体射电与光学偏振中偶发出现的大幅EVPA（电矢量位置角）旋转，其物理起源（如喷流方向变化、相对论束流增强、激波传播等）仍存争议。作者希望借助十年尺度、多频段的高节奏监测，检验EVPA旋转是否由渐进增强的相对论束流效应驱动，并厘清其与同期射电耀发的关联及发生条件。

Method: 采用KVN（22--129 GHz）、ALMA（91.5--343.5 GHz）和Metsähovi（37 GHz）对OJ 287进行2012—2023年高节奏多频监测，并结合已发表的光学偏振测量。通过追踪毫米波段与光学波段的EVPA演化，识别旋转事件及其时间区间；对ALMA 91.5 GHz光变曲线建模，估算EVPA旋转期间的变光性多普勒因子δ_var，并检验其是否随耀发序列单调变化。

Result: 十年数据中仅2015—2017年出现一次异常大且平滑的EVPA旋转：毫米波段EVPA在约1.5年内旋转超过~300°，光学波段呈现幅度相当但演化更快的旋转，其余时期EVPA相对稳定。该旋转与37至343 GHz的一系列强射电耀发同时发生，耀发峰值幅度向2017年3月（旋转结束时）递增。ALMA 91.5 GHz光变建模给出旋转期间δ_var约8—11，且在耀发序列中无系统性增长，说明逐步增强的相对论束流并非耀发包络上升的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 大幅EVPA旋转被严格限制在2015—2017年且δ_var近乎恒定，表明该事件是一次新抛射的扰动在螺旋磁场穿绕的结构化内喷流区域中传播时暂时主导了偏振辐射，且喷流方向变化幅度适中。因此，大EVPA旋转随喷流取向演化而间歇性发生，只有当发射主导地位与观测几何条件有利地吻合时才会显现。

Abstract: We present high-cadence, multi-frequency monitoring of the blazar OJ 287 using KVN (22--129 GHz), ALMA (91.5--343.5 GHz), and Metsähovi (37 GHz), covering 2012--2023, together with published optical polarimetry. Within this decade-long dataset, an exceptionally large and smooth EVPA rotation is observed only during 2015--2017. The millimeter-band EVPA rotates by more than $\sim$300 deg over $\sim$1.5 yr, with a comparably large and more rapidly evolving rotation observed at optical wavelengths, while remaining comparatively stable at other epochs. The rotation coincides with a sequence of strong radio flares from 37 to 343 GHz whose peak amplitudes increase toward 2017 March, when the rotation ends. Modeling the ALMA 91.5 GHz light curve yields variability Doppler factors of $δ_{\rm var} \sim 8$--$11$ during the EVPA-rotation interval, with no systematic increase across the flare sequence. The absence of a monotonic change in $δ_{\rm var}$ indicates that progressively enhanced relativistic beaming is unlikely to be the primary driver of the rising flare envelope. The confinement of the large EVPA rotation to this interval, together with the nearly constant $δ_{\rm var}$, indicates that the 2015--2017 event occurred when a single newly ejected disturbance temporarily dominated the polarized emission while propagating through structured inner-jet regions threaded by a helical magnetic field under modest jet-direction changes. Large EVPA rotations therefore arise intermittently as the jet orientation evolves, and are realized only when emission dominance and viewing geometry align favorably.

</details>


### [110] [Twins in the Shadow: Phase Transitions in Proto-Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03148)
*Jannik Milthaler,Jan-Erik Christian,Pia Jakobus,Stephan Rosswog*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究强子-夸克物质一阶相变对原中子星演化的影响，发现引力孪生星仅在不额外存在重子孪生星时才可能，从而排除大多数孪生星对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孪生星（相同引力质量、不同半径的中子星）被视为一阶相变的"确凿信号"，但需检验原中子星演化是否允许这类构型存在。

Method: 构建高度可参数化的状态方程：强子物质采用相对论平均场方法，夸克物质采用常数声速方法；将原中子星冷却近似为恒定重子质量下熵递减的序列。

Result: 部分参数化给出引力孪生星；但原中子星演化表明，只有当混合星不额外具有重子孪生星时引力孪生星才可能，这排除了大多数孪生星对，尤其当相变发生在高密度时；在质量-半径图上，未被占据的混合支处于强子支的"阴影"中。

Conclusion: 原中子星演化对孪生星构型施加了强约束，显著缩小了可作为相变信号的孪生星参数空间。

Abstract: We study the impact of a first-order phase transition from hadronic to deconfined quark matter on the evolution of proto-neutron stars. To maximize the applicability of our results, we construct an equation of state that is highly parameterizable, where we use a relativistic mean field approach to model hadronic matter and a constant speed of sound approach to model quark matter. We approximate the cooling behavior of proto-neutron stars as a sequence of decreasing entropy at constant baryonic mass. Some parameterizations lead to so-called "twin-stars", where two neutron stars have the same (gravitational) mass but different radii. Detecting them is regarded as a smoking gun signal for phase transition. We find, however, that proto-neutron star evolution suggests that gravitational twin stars are only possible if the hybrid star does not have, in addition, a baryonic twin. This rules out most twin star pairs, especially if the phase transition occurs at large densities. In a mass-radius diagram the unpopulated hybrid branch appears to be "in the shadow" of the hadronic branch.

</details>


### [111] [Guide field effects on particle acceleration in three-dimensional relativistic magnetic reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03427)
*Despina Karavola,Lorenzo Sironi,Maria Petropoulou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过3D相对论对等离子体磁重联模拟，研究导向场强度对快速粒子加速通道的影响：该通道在3D中持续存在但参与粒子比例随导向场增强而下降，导致能谱更陡、最大洛伦兹因子更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期3D粒子模拟表明，在弱导向场下，粒子可通过逃逸重联等离子体并从大尺度上游电场获得能量，从而实现接近最大速率的加速。本文旨在探究当导向场强度与重联场相当乃至更强时，这一快速加速通道是否仍然存在。

Method: 开展相对论对等离子体磁重联的3D粒子模拟，并辅以对应的2D模拟；在重联出流方向采用开放边界条件以建立准稳态。

Result: 随着导向场增强，重联率降低，高能粒子方向更趋于与局域磁场对齐。3D中的快速加速通道（在2D中缺失）在所研究的整个导向场范围内均持续存在，但导向场越强，参与该通道的粒子比例越小。因此强导向场产生更陡的粒子能谱和更低的最大洛伦兹因子。

Conclusion: 导向场强度是调控相对论磁重联中粒子加速效率与能谱特征的关键参数：快速加速通道虽不因导向场增强而消失，但其作用范围逐渐收窄，从而整体上削弱加速效果。

Abstract: Relativistic magnetic reconnection is a key mechanism for particle acceleration in astrophysical plasmas. Recent 3D particle-in-cell simulations have shown that, for weak guide fields, particles can be accelerated at nearly the maximal rate by escaping the reconnected plasma and gaining energy from the large-scale upstream electric field. Here, we investigate whether this fast acceleration channel persists as the guide field becomes comparable to the reconnecting field. We perform 3D simulations of relativistic pair-plasma reconnection, complemented by corresponding 2D runs, using open boundaries along the reconnection outflows to establish a quasi-steady state. We find that increasing the guide field strength reduces the reconnection rate and causes energetic particles to become more closely aligned with the local magnetic field. In 3D, the fast acceleration channel---absent in 2D---persists across the full range of guide fields investigated, but involves a progressively smaller fraction of particles as the guide field increases. Consequently, stronger guide fields produce steeper particle energy spectra and lower maximum Lorentz factors.

</details>


### [112] [Forward Bayesian Inference for the Binary Black Hole Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03459)
*Poulami Dutta Roy,Tanmaya Mishra,Achal Kumar,Sergey Klimenko*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: PopCWB：基于cWB无模型搜索管线、采用前向贝叶斯方法的双黑洞族群推断框架，用于O3数据并约束黑洞最大质量与本地并合率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准族群分析采用逆向方法，依赖单个事件的后期样本；而针对cWB这类无模型搜索管线缺乏事件级参数估计，需要一种适配的前向族群推断方法。

Method: PopCWB采用前向方法，通过改变族群模型来寻找最能描述观测事件的最优模型。它不使用事件级后期样本，而是使用cWB重建的大量模拟事件及其通过机器学习回归估计的总质量，构建边缘概率分布，并以此构造似然函数。

Result: 将该框架应用于LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA O3期间cWB探测到的BBH事件，约束了一个包含脉动对不稳定性超新星和动力学并合效应的天体物理动机族群模型。预测来自恒星演化的最大黑洞质量为45.5^{+5.3}_{-6.5} M⊙，推断本地BBH并合率为23.1^{+11.3}_{-8.4} Gpc^{-3} yr^{-1}。

Conclusion: PopCWB为无模型搜索管线提供了一种可行的前向族群推断途径，其结果与现有文献族群模型进行了比较，验证了该方法在BBH族群分析中的适用性。

Abstract: We present PopCWB, a Bayesian framework for the inference of the binary black hole (BBH) population based on the unmodeled search pipeline coherent WaveBurst (cWB). The standard population analysis takes an inverse approach by inferring the underlying population model parameters for a given set of detected events using the individual posterior samples. In PopCWB, we take a forward approach to population inference by varying population models to identify the most optimal one which describes the observed events. Rather than using event-level posterior samples, PopCWB uses a large set of simulated events reconstructed by cWB and their total mass, estimated with a machine-learning regression, to construct marginal probability distributions. These marginal probabilities are then used in the construction of the likelihood. We apply PopCWB to BBH events detected by cWB during the third observing run (O3) of the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA detectors. We constrain an astrophysically motivated BBH population model that incorporates the effects of pulsational pair-instability supernovae and dynamical mergers. The analysis predicts a maximum black hole mass of $\mathbf{45.5^{+5.3}_{-6.5} M_{\odot}}$ from stellar evolution and an inferred local BBH merger rate of $\mathbf{23.1^{+11.3}_{-8.4} \, Gpc^{-3} yr^{-1}}$. We compare these results with the existing population models from the literature.

</details>
