### [113] [Flow Matching for Fast Posterior Sampling in Bayesian Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02377)
*Jan Blechschmidt,Oliver G. Ernst,Moritz Poguntke,Björn Sprungk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对用于PDE反问题的条件流匹配进行严谨评估，推导可计算的近似后验误差界，并提出渐近精确的混合采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯反问题中从后验采样是核心任务，但标准MCMC方法具有序列性、样本相关、且每次观测都需重跑的缺点。条件流匹配作为摊销式替代方案，训练一次即可对任意观测低成本生成独立近似后验样本，但其在函数值参数PDE反问题中的准确性缺乏严谨评估。

Method: 利用流的可处理密度，以总变差距离和KL散度推导底层近似后验的可计算准确性估计；并提出通过Metropolization实现的渐近精确混合采样器。

Result: 在多个数值算例中验证了准确性估计，并展示了摊销效果，包括电阻抗断层成像与无似然的Lotka-Volterra模型。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配为PDE反问题提供了高效摊销采样途径，配合误差估计与混合采样器可实现准确性与渐近精确性的兼顾。

Abstract: Sampling from the posterior is the central task of computational Bayesian inverse problems. The standard workhorse in Bayesian inference - Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) - is sequential, yields correlated samples, and must be rerun for each observation. Conditional flow matching offers an amortized alternative: a transport map, trained once on joint samples of parameter and data, that yields independent approximate posterior samples for any observation at negligible online cost, without new likelihood evaluations. We give a careful, MCMC-literate assessment of flow matching for PDE-based inverse problems with function-valued parameters. Exploiting the flow's tractable density, we derive computable accuracy estimates of the underlying approximate posterior in total-variation distance and Kullback-Leibler divergence and, moreover, propose a hybrid sampler that is asymptotically exact by Metropolization. We validate the accuracy estimates and demonstrate the amortization in several numerical examples, including electrical impedance tomography and a likelihood-free Lotka-Volterra model.

</details>


### [114] [Strong error analysis of a stochastic exponential integrator for SPDEs driven by fractional Brownian motion with H<1/2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02412)
*Antoine Tambue,Aurelien Junior Noupelah,Louis Aime Fono*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 研究由加性分数布朗运动（H∈(0,1/2)）驱动的半线性随机偏微分方程，建立适定性与时空正则性，并分析随机指数积分器的强收敛性，得到依赖于H的时间收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数布朗运动可刻画反持续（anti-persistent）时序相关性，广泛用于反常扩散、水文学、金融与地球物理过程。H∈(0,1/2)情形下噪声时间正则性低，给SPDE的适定性与数值离散分析带来困难，现有工作对该类方程数值方法的强收敛性研究不足。

Method: 针对加性分数布朗运动驱动的半线性SPDE，允许线性算子非自伴；结合解析半群估计、分数布朗运动的典范Hilbert空间结构与Malliavin calculus，建立mild解的适定性与时空正则性；对时间离散采用随机指数积分器，分析其强收敛性。

Result: 证明了解的适定性与时空正则性，导出了依赖于H的强时间收敛率，在最大空间正则性下达H+1/2；对具有非均质Darcy流的随机对流-扩散-反应问题进行的数值实验验证了理论时间收敛率；并用Monte Carlo方法估计了不同H∈(0,1/2)下解的均值。

Conclusion: 该工作为低Hurst参数加性分数噪声驱动的半线性SPDE提供了适定性、正则性与数值强收敛性的统一分析框架，随机指数积分器在最大空间正则性下可达到H+1/2的时间收敛率，数值实验与理论一致。

Abstract: Fractional Brownian motion provides a useful framework for modeling random phenomena with anti-persistent temporal correlations that arise in applications such as anomalous diffusion, hydrology, finance, and geophysical processes. In this paper, we study a class of semilinear stochastic partial differential equations driven by additive fractional Brownian motion with Hurst parameter $H\in(0,\frac12)$. We establish the well-posedness and space-time regularity of the mild solution and investigate the strong convergence of a stochastic exponential integrator for the temporal discretization. The analysis allows the linear operator to be non-self-adjoint and combines analytic semigroup estimates with the canonical Hilbert-space structure associated with fractional Brownian motion and Malliavin calculus to handle the low temporal regularity of the noise. We derive an $H$-dependent strong temporal convergence rate, which reaches $H+\frac12$ under maximal spatial regularity. Numerical experiments for a stochastic advection--diffusion--reaction problem with heterogeneous Darcy flow confirm the theoretical temporal convergence rates. We also estimate the mean of the solution with different Hurst parameters $H\in(0,\frac12)$ using the Monte Carlo method.

</details>


### [115] [Residual Minimisation for Transport-Based Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02499)
*Tristan Pryer,Nikolaos Rekatsinas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对二维/三维稳态输运方程最优控制问题（放疗光子输运），提出残差最小二乘泛函，其极小点等价于KKT解，可控制状态、伴随及控制的L2误差，并给出无需离散化的后验估计；进一步用神经网络参数化并证明收敛性与误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究由放射治疗计划中的光子输运问题驱动：需要在流入边界源作为控制、观测为角通量或其角积分（剂量代理）的情形下求解最优控制问题。其动机在于建立一个无需依赖离散化、对任意容许近似均成立的连续层面后验误差估计，并处理控制罚项与输运迹边界测度不同、且不要求|ω·n|有正下界的技术难点。

Method: 考虑二维或三维空间中的稳态线性输运方程最优控制问题，控制为流入边界源，观测算子可追踪角通量或其角积分。最优性系统为带角依赖的输运-伴随方程对，通过边界最优性条件耦合。构造残差最小二乘泛函，使其极小点与KKT系统解一致，并证明该泛函控制状态与伴随的L2误差及控制的通量加权L2误差；另用无加权最优性残差在目标范数下验证控制。随后用神经网络参数化状态、伴随与控制，采用适当求积（Monte Carlo形式将残差积分替换为加权样本均值）在神经网络空间上极小化。

Result: 证明该泛函给出连续层面后验估计，对任意容许近似成立，且无需|ω·n|正下界；估计中体现控制罚项与输运迹的不同边界测度。对神经网络离散化，给出在近似、求积与优化显式假设下的收敛性证明，并将总误差界定为近似误差、求积误差与优化误差的函数；Monte Carlo求积误差被纳入后验估计。二维与三维数值实验（含正则制造解）验证了残差-误差关系及该框架在场追踪控制问题中的灵活性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个基于残差的输运最优控制后验误差框架，兼具连续层面的严格估计与神经网络离散化的收敛保证，适用于场追踪控制问题，并为放疗剂量优化等应用提供了理论支撑与数值验证。

Abstract: We consider an optimal control problem governed by a stationary linear transport equation in two or three spatial dimensions, motivated by photon transport in radiotherapy treatment planning. The control is an inflow boundary source, and the observation operator may track either the angular flux or its angular integral, the latter serving as a simplified dose surrogate. The associated optimality system consists of a transport--adjoint pair with angular dependence, coupled through a boundary optimality condition.
  We formulate a residual least-squares functional whose minimisers coincide with solutions of the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker system and show that the functional controls the $L^2$ errors in the state and adjoint and the flux-weighted $L^2$ error in the control. This yields a continuum-level a posteriori estimate without recourse to discretisation. An additional unweighted optimality residual certifies the control in the norm of the objective. The estimate holds for every admissible approximation and accounts for the different boundary measures in the control penalty and transport traces, without a positive lower bound on $|ω\cdot n|$. We then parameterise the state, adjoint and control using neural networks and minimise over a neural network space using appropriate quadrature. The Monte Carlo formulation replaces each residual integral by a weighted sample mean, whose quadrature error enters the a posteriori estimate.
  We prove convergence under explicit assumptions on approximation, quadrature andoptimisation and derive bounds on the total error in terms of approximation, quadrature and optimisation error. Numerical examples in two and three dimensions illustrate the residual--error relation and the flexibility of the residual framework for field-tracking control problems, including regular manufactured solutions.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Closure of Dynamical Systems with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02564)
*Evan Habbershaw,John Harlim,Senwei Liang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于核岭回归(KRR)的闭合建模框架，用于识别动力系统中的缺失分量，涵盖ODE/PDE的差分方程闭合与动理学方程矩闭合两类问题，并给出误差界与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统建模中常存在未解析尺度或缺失项（闭合问题），需要有效方法识别并补偿这些缺失分量，而现有方法（如LSTM）在长期预测精度和鲁棒性上存在不足。

Method: 采用Kernel Ridge Regression构建闭合模型框架：第一类为ODE/PDE中的差分方程闭合，推导包含时间积分、未解析尺度近似及耦合插值误差的ODE误差界；第二类为一维动理学方程的矩闭合，将动理学与宏观通量之差建模为已解析宏观变量的函数，并对比基于PCA坐标的全局KRR模型与空间局部模型。

Result: 在Lorenz-63系统和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上实现准确的长期预测，显著优于基于LSTM的闭合模型；矩闭合中，全局模型对单峰初值表现良好但双峰初值精度下降，而采用合适建模输入的空间局部模型鲁棒性更强、预测精度更高。

Conclusion: KRR闭合建模框架能有效处理差分方程与代数（矩）两类闭合问题，误差界为方法提供理论支撑，空间局部建模在复杂初值条件下更具优势。

Abstract: We develop a closure modeling framework for identifying missing components of dynamical systems using Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR). The framework addresses two classes of closure problems: difference-equation closures arising in ODE and PDE settings, and algebraic closures arising from moment closure in kinetic equations. For the first class, we derive an error bound in an ODE setting that quantifies contributions from time integration, approximation of unresolved scales, and interpolation required to couple unresolved-scale effects to the resolved solver. Numerical experiments on the Lorenz-63 system and the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation demonstrate accurate long-horizon predictions and substantial improvements over an LSTM-based closure model. For the second class, we consider moment closure for a one-dimensional kinetic equation by modeling discrepancies between kinetic and macroscopic fluxes as a function of the resolved macroscopic variables. We compare global KRR models based on PCA coordinates with spatially local models. While the global model performs well for unimodal initial conditions, its accuracy deteriorates for bimodal initial conditions. Spatially local models with appropriate modeling inputs improve robustness and achieve higher predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [117] [Inferring constitutive forces from noisy Allen-Cahn movies with moment equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02672)
*Soobeen Jung,Hyunju Kim*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出观测感知、噪声校正的矩方程，并构建表面张力校准的本构Transformer（SCCT），从图像中推断Allen-Cahn本构力，效果优于单纯提升优化器或正则化灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从图像推断Allen-Cahn本构力时，非线性观测噪声会偏移力特征的均值，共享噪声使仪器与响应耦合，且借用其他求解器的时间平衡即使在干净数据上也会产生偏差。因此仅靠精确优化器不足，需在反演前处理这些观测与噪声效应。

Method: 建立观测感知、噪声校正的矩方程以消除上述偏差；提出表面张力校准的本构Transformer（SCCT），将矩与正Bernstein逆耦合，并能从图像和矩中自适应调整先验与精度；给出条件稳定性界，将矩残差与先验误差分离。

Result: 合成实验表明，校正估计方程比增加正则化灵活性更重要；学习自适应的收益较小且依赖训练比较。由平面影像推断的力能预测未见的平面及三维演化。环笼破裂案例显示，即使拓扑预测一致，扩散界面精度仍能区分不同本构力，而外部表面张力单独设定能量尺度。

Conclusion: 在Allen-Cahn本构力反演中，观测噪声校正与估计方程的正确性比优化器和正则化设计更关键；SCCT结合校正矩、Bernstein逆与自适应先验，可从平面图像推断具预测力的本构力，扩散界面精度是区分力的重要指标。

Abstract: Inferring an Allen-Cahn constitutive force from images requires more than an accurate optimizer: nonlinear observation noise alters the mean of force features, shared noise couples instruments to the response, and a temporal balance borrowed from another solver can remain biased even on clean data. We develop observation-aware, noise-adjusted moment equations that address these effects before inversion. The surface-tension-calibrated constitutive transformer (SCCT) couples the resulting moments to a positive Bernstein inverse and can adapt its prior and precision from images and moments. A conditional stability bound separates moment residuals from prior error. Synthetic tests show that correcting the estimating equation matters more than increasing regularizer flexibility; the benefit of learned adaptation is smaller and depends on the training comparison. Forces inferred from planar movies predict unseen planar and three-dimensional evolutions. A ring-cage breakup shows why diffuse-interface accuracy distinguishes forces even when their topological predictions agree, while external surface tension sets the energy scale separately.

</details>


### [118] [Property-Preserving Data Compression for Incompressible Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02776)
*Andrey Pluzhnik,Ramansh Sharma,Martin Burtscher,Varun Shankar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种保零散度（及周期域上保周期性）的不可压缩速度场有损压缩方法：用保性质矩阵值核拟合节点速度，再用标准有损压缩器压缩系数，解压后任意系数向量均满足约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可压缩速度场（散度为零）的有损压缩通常会破坏零散度与周期性等物理约束，因此需要一种在压缩过程中解析地保持这些约束的方法。

Method: 用保性质矩阵值核对节点速度进行拟合，压缩其系数（低秩表示或插值矩阵稀疏性）；给出全局Matérn插值、两级Nyström近似、Nyström–Wendland残差插值、多级Wendland插值与保性质（冗余）核框架等拟合方案；推导节点误差界，将压缩器容差与附加速度误差关联。

Result: 在四类压缩器上，针对cavity、Taylor–Green与species-transport流动进行实验：验证了系数误差假设下的误差界，并在匹配速度精度下比较存储量。低秩方法在足够精确解析速度场时给出紧凑表示；残差、多级与框架插值在低秩方法精度不足时扩展可达精度。同时测量了湍流速度统计的变化。

Conclusion: 该方法在解析保持不可压缩性与周期性的同时，支持精度—压缩的权衡。

Abstract: We develop a method for lossy compression of incompressible (i.e., divergence-free) velocity fields that preserves zero divergence and, on periodic domains, periodicity. We fit the nodal velocities with property-preserving matrix-valued kernels and compress their coefficients using standard lossy compressors. Every decompressed coefficient vector defines a field with the same constraints, independently of the compression tolerance. We present several fitting schemes designed around either low-rank representation of or sparsity in the interpolation matrix. More specifically, we show results for global Matérn interpolation, a two-level Nyström approximation, Nyström--Wendland residual interpolation, multilevel Wendland interpolation and a property-preserving (redundant) kernel frame. We derive nodal error bounds that relate the compressor tolerance to the additional velocity error. Experiments with four compressors on cavity, Taylor--Green, and species-transport flows verify the bounds under their coefficient-error assumptions and compare storage at matched velocity accuracy. The low-rank methods give compact representations when they resolve the velocity field sufficiently accurately; residual, multilevel, and frame interpolation extend the attainable accuracy when they do not. We also measure changes in turbulent velocity statistics. The resulting representations support an accuracy--compression tradeoff while preserving incompressibility and periodicity analytically.

</details>


### [119] [Efficient and Accurate Lift-and-Decoupling Generalized Transferable Neural Networks for Elliptic and Moving Interface Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02891)
*Tao Cheng,Junqing Jia,Lili Ju,Xiaoping Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出LD-GTransNet方法，高效高精度求解椭圆与移动界面问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面问题解具有分片光滑特性，传统神经网络方法难以统一逼近，常规lift方法存在冗余，且非线性训练计算成本高。

Method: 基于多层GTransNet框架，引入"lift-and-decoupling"策略：预置隐层神经元参数，将物理坐标与辅助变量分别由独立子网络处理，实现无需显式区域分解的统一全局逼近，并将求解归结为线性最小二乘问题。

Result: 在二维和三维基准界面问题上的大量数值实验表明，尤其在高对比系数和复杂界面几何情形下，性能持续优于现有神经网络方法与传统数值方法。

Conclusion: LD-GTransNet无需非线性训练，兼顾效率与精度，适用于高对比、复杂界面问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a highly efficient and accurate method, called ``lift-and-decoupling generalized transferable neural network" (LD-GTransNet), for numerical solution of elliptic and moving interface problems. Our method makes use of the multi-layer GTransNet approach and incorporates a novel {lift-and-decoupling} strategy, in which the hidden-layer neuron parameters are preset and the physical coordinates and the added auxiliary variable are processed by separate subnetworks. Such design enables a unified global approximation of piecewise smooth solutions without explicit domain decomposition, while preserving network expressivity and avoiding the redundancy inherent in conventional {lift} approaches. The resulting formulation reduces to a linear least-squares problem, thereby eliminating the need of nonlinear training and ensuring the high computational efficiency and accuracy. Extensive numerical experiments on benchmark interface problems in two and three dimensions demonstrate that, compared with existing neural network-based and traditional numerical methods, our proposed LD-GTransNet consistently delivers superior performance, particularly for problems with high-contrast coefficients and complex interface geometries.

</details>


### [120] [From Continuous to Fully Discrete Carleman Estimates: A Transfer Principle for Parabolic Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02983)
*Qi Lü,Yu Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象传递定理，将连续Carleman不等式与加权逼近、相容性性质结合，导出全离散后向Euler格式的离散Carleman估计，并在热方程的有限元与有限差分离散上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接针对离散抛物算子建立Carleman估计通常依赖定制加权恒等式，缺乏统一框架，且难以将PDE层面的Carleman分析与具体离散格式的数值验证分离。作者希望构建一套抽象机制，自动把连续Carleman不等式传递到离散格式。

Method: 对时间无关的正自伴算子类，为每个离散状态构造一个带位移的连续辅助问题，使其离散解恰为给定状态；借助在全离散载荷空间上一致成立的加权时空误差估计，在保持原格式残差项与观测项的前提下完成不等式传递，并分别用一致质量P1有限元（局部分级网格，二维/三维）和任意固定维Cartesian有限差分验证假设。

Result: 对热方程，传递后的Carleman估计在参数尺度 min{h^{-4/5},(δt)^{-2/5}} 内成立；对有界实值时空位势，所得估计给出松弛可观测性与近似零控，控制代价一致有界，终端误差在独立空间与时间加密下指数小，重构控制的弱聚点为连续零控。

Conclusion: 该抽象传递框架将PDE相关的Carleman分析与每种离散化所需的数值验证解耦，为全离散抛物问题的Carleman估计及其可观测性/可控性应用提供了统一且可复用的途径。

Abstract: Direct approaches to discrete parabolic Carleman estimates often rely on weighted identities tailored to the discrete operator. We prove an abstract transfer theorem that derives such estimates for fully discrete backward Euler schemes with conforming spatial reconstructions from a continuous Carleman inequality together with suitable weighted approximation and compatibility properties. For a class of time-independent positive self-adjoint operators, the key construction assigns to each discrete state a shifted continuous auxiliary problem whose discrete solution is exactly the prescribed state. Weighted space-time error estimates, uniform over the full discrete load space, then transfer the inequality while preserving the residual and observation terms of the original scheme. This separates the PDE-specific Carleman analysis from the numerical verification required for each discretization. We verify the hypotheses for the heat equation discretized by consistent-mass $P_1$ finite elements on locally graded meshes in two and three dimensions and by standard Cartesian finite differences in any fixed dimension. For the heat equation, the transferred estimate is valid for Carleman parameters up to the scale $\min\{h^{-4/5},(δt)^{-2/5}\}$. For bounded real-valued space-time potentials, the resulting estimates yield relaxed observability and approximate null controls with uniformly bounded cost and exponentially small terminal errors under independent spatial and temporal refinement; weak accumulation points of the reconstructed controls are continuous null controls.

</details>


### [121] [A Physics-Driven Multiscale Method for High-Frequency Helmholtz Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03144)
*Huangxin Chen,Jianhui Chen,Shubin Fu,Zhiyu Tan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析一种物理驱动的多尺度模型降阶方法，用于高频Helmholtz方程，通过局部波响应构造约化空间并给出稳定性与先验误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频Helmholtz方程数值求解面临强振荡与污染效应，需要兼具稳定性保证与低维度的模型降阶方法，将局部降维与全局逼近的稳定性定量联系起来。

Method: 在含速度消元的混合有限元框架下，于过采样区域求解带边界激励的局部Helmholtz问题生成快照，构建融入介质振荡与传播特性的约化空间；再用局部广义特征值问题选模态，形成元素间稀疏耦合的全局约化系统；通过将局部谱逼近关联到全局伴随问题逼近，由细网格inf-sup估计导出多尺度inf-sup条件。

Result: 多尺度inf-sup条件由细网格分辨率、过采样深度和首个被舍弃局部特征值控制；据此证明离散能量范数下的适定性与拟最优性，并给出显式刻画粗/细网格尺寸、过采样深度、谱截断与波数作用的先验误差界；二维与三维数值实验在均匀及强非均匀介质（含PML截断）中实现精度良好的波场逼近与全局维度、求解代价的显著降低。

Conclusion: 该分析在局部空间降维与全局逼近的稳定性、精度之间建立了定量联系，验证了方法在高频波传播问题上的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a physics-driven multiscale method for model reduction of the Helmholtz equation at high frequency. The method constructs a reduced approximation space from local wave responses within a mixed finite element formulation with velocity elimination. These responses are computed by solving Helmholtz problems with boundary excitations on oversampled regions, giving snapshots that incorporate the oscillatory behavior and propagation characteristics of the underlying medium. Local generalized eigenvalue problems then select modes from this physically informed space to form a reduced global system with sparse interelement coupling. To establish stability and convergence of this reduction, we relate the local spectral approximation to approximation of the global adjoint problem. Starting from an inf--sup estimate for the fine-grid formulation, this argument yields a multiscale inf--sup condition governed by fine-grid resolution, oversampling, and the first omitted local eigenvalues. The resulting stability establishes well-posedness and quasi-optimality of the multiscale approximation in a discrete energy norm. Together with local spectral estimates, it gives an a priori error bound that explicitly quantifies the roles of the coarse and fine mesh sizes, oversampling depth, spectral truncation, and wavenumber. This analysis provides a quantitative link between local space reduction and the stability and accuracy of the global approximation. Numerical experiments in two and three dimensions demonstrate accurate wavefield approximation with substantial reductions in global dimension and solution cost for homogeneous and strongly heterogeneous media, including perfectly matched layer truncations.

</details>


### [122] [Recursive Multilevel Decomposition of Pairwise Comparison Matrices: Algorithms and Inconsistency Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03184)
*Mert Çarboğa,Waldemar W. Koczkodaj,Yusuf Yaylı*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文在对数坐标下研究正互反成对比较矩阵，提出将残差矩阵按固定排序唯一分解为互相Frobenius正交的层，并给出两种复杂度分别为O(n³)和O(n²)的计算流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正互反成对比较（PC）矩阵的一致性分析中，已有的一致性—残差拆分通常只给出整体残差，缺乏对残差结构的分层刻画。作者希望在不依赖谱、稀疏或一般性假设的前提下，对任意n≥3的正互反PC矩阵给出唯一、可计算的残差分层结构，以提供几何平均优先级和Koczkodaj不一致性指标（Kii）之外的结构信息。

Method: 在逐元素对数坐标下处理正互反PC矩阵；基于已有的一致—残差拆分，对任意固定排序构造残差矩阵的自上而下分解，使其层间相互Frobenius正交；给出显式的活动矩阵递推算法（O(n³)）以及等价的累积参数提取流程（O(n²)）；以各层Frobenius范数平方定义排序条件下的残差能量谱；通过对数坐标逐元素指数化实现原始互反矩阵的精确Hadamard积重构。

Result: 分解对任意n≥3的正互反PC矩阵成立，具有唯一性且层间Frobenius正交；活动矩阵递推与累积参数提取两种算法复杂度分别为O(n³)与O(n²)；残差能量谱提供与几何平均优先级、Kii互补的结构信息；可通过Hadamard积精确重构原始互反矩阵；数值实验验证了两种流程一致、重构精确、正交性成立，且数值精度达到机器精度。

Conclusion: 该工作为正互反PC矩阵残差提供了一种无需额外假设的唯一正交分层框架，兼顾理论简洁性与计算效率，并为一致性/不一致性结构分析提供了新的能量谱视角。

Abstract: Positive reciprocal pairwise comparison matrices are studied in entrywise logarithmic coordinates. Starting from the established consistent-residual split, we show that, for any fixed ordering, the residual matrix admits a unique top-down decomposition into mutually Frobenius-orthogonal layers. The construction applies to every positive reciprocal PC matrix of size $n \geq 3$, without additional spectral, sparsity, or genericity assumptions. An explicit active-matrix recursion and an equivalent cumulative parameter-extraction procedure have computational complexities $O(n^3)$ and $O(n^2)$, respectively. The squared Frobenius norms of the layers define an ordering-conditioned residual-energy profile that provides structural information complementary to the geometric-mean priorities and the Koczkodaj inconsistency indicator (Kii). Entrywise exponentiation yields an exact Hadamard-product reconstruction of the original reciprocal matrix. Numerical experiments confirm agreement between the two computational procedures, exact reconstruction, mutual orthogonality, and numerical accuracy to machine precision.

</details>


### [123] [Reversible Stratonovich perturbations for accelerating convergence to equilibrium of Langevin dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03191)
*Guillaume Louis,Grigorios A. Pavliotis,Gilles Vilmart*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究Langevin型动力学采样，通过在过阻尼Langevin动力学中加入保持可逆性的Stratonovich扰动，证明了对高斯目标分布的最优缩放，并给出构造该扰动的算法，数值实验验证了方法的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升从概率分布中采样的效率，探索保持可逆性的Stratonovich扰动对Langevin型动力学的影响。

Method: 在过阻尼Langevin动力学中引入保持可逆性的Stratonovich扰动；对高斯目标分布证明最优缩放；推导构造该扰动的算法；通过数值实验与不可逆采样器和过阻尼Langevin动力学进行对比。

Result: 证明了Stratonovich扰动在高斯目标分布下的最优缩放，并成功构造出相应扰动的算法；数值实验显示该方法在非二次情形下仍具高效性。

Conclusion: 保持可逆性的Stratonovich扰动能够有效提升采样效率，且所提方法在超越二次型的目标分布上依然高效。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of sampling from a probability distribution using Langevin-type dynamics. We consider overdamped Langevin dynamics perturbed by adding a Stratonovich perturbation that preserves reversibility. We prove an optimal scaling for the Stratonovich perturbation applied to Gaussian target distributions and derive an algorithm to construct such perturbation. Our theoretical results are supplemented by numerical experiments in which we compare our proposed method with nonreversible samplers and overdamped Langevin dynamics. Our numerical experiments demonstrate the efficiency of the approach beyond the quadratic case.

</details>


### [124] [Simultaneous Global Convergence Failure of Newton's and Halley's Methods in Degree Four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03246)
*Alessio Basti,Tommaso Cremaschi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文构造了一个显式四次多项式，使牛顿法与Halley法在非空开初值集上均不收敛到根，填补了四次情形下牛顿-Halley同时失效的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿法与Halley法是经典求根迭代法，二者同时失效的问题在低次（尤其四次）情形尚缺乏显式例子，作者希望填补这一"四次缺口"并给出可复现构造。

Method: 在一个设计为使牛顿法呈现指定二循环的单参数四次族中，通过可复现的计算搜索找出显式四次多项式；随后用双区间收缩论证，分析证明牛顿法具有超吸引二循环、Halley法具有一个不同的吸引性多余二循环。

Result: 得到显式四次多项式，使牛顿法与Halley法在非空开初值集上均不收敛到根；计算搜索还给出另一个四次多项式，其数值证据表明牛顿法、Halley法与Schröder法同时失效。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了四次情形下牛顿-Halley同时失效的显式且可复现例子，填补了次数缺口，并为进一步研究Schröder法乃至多法同时失效提供了线索。

Abstract: We construct an explicit quartic polynomial for which Newton's and Halley's root-finding methods both fail to converge to a root on nonempty open sets of initial values. The example can be found through a reproducible computational search within a one-parameter family of quartics designed to exhibit a prescribed Newton two-cycle. We then prove analytically that Newton's method has a superattracting two-cycle and that Halley's method has a distinct attracting extraneous two-cycle, using a two-interval contraction argument. This fills a degree-four gap in the simultaneous Newton--Halley failure problem. The computational approach also identifies another quartic with numerical evidence of simultaneous failure of Newton's, Halley's, and Schröder's methods.

</details>


### [125] [On the Choice of Continuous and Discontinuous Lagrange Multipliers in Fictitious-Domain Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03267)
*Najwa Alshehri,Shumoua F. Alrzqi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文比较了FD-DLM方法在连续与不连续乘子空间下的两种低阶离散格式，分析弱耦合约束满足方式与原始场偏差的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FD-DLM方法用于处理界面不匹配背景网格的椭圆界面问题，但连续与不连续乘子空间离散格式的耦合约束满足方式及其对误差的影响尚需系统比较。

Method: 采用两种稳定的低阶FD-DLM离散格式，分别使用连续和不连续乘子空间，区分"逐元素满足弱耦合约束"与"两个离散原始场之间偏差大小"两种情况，进行数值实验对比。

Result: 连续格式通常产生更小的局部误差和最大不匹配值；不连续格式的不匹配值随网格加密下降更规则，且原始误差更小。

Conclusion: 连续与不连续乘子空间各有优势：连续格式在局部误差和最大不匹配方面更优，不连续格式在收敛规律和原始误差方面更优，选择取决于具体问题需求。

Abstract: This article examines the Fictitious-Domain approach using Distributed Lagrange Multipliers (FD-DLM), a method that handles elliptic interface problems with separate meshes without requiring the interface to match the background mesh. It compares two stable, low-order FD-DLM discretizations when using both continuous and discontinuous multiplier spaces. The study distinguishes between satisfying the weak coupling constraint per element and the size of the discrepancy between the two discrete primal fields. Numerical experiments indicate that the continuous formulation usually yields smaller local errors and maximum mismatch values, while the discontinuous formulation shows a more regular decrease in mismatch as the mesh is refined and has smaller primal errors.

</details>


### [126] [Fully mixed finite element methods for the coupling of viscoelasticity and reaction-diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03317)
*Alonso J. Bustos,Jeonghun J. Lee,Ricardo Ruiz-Baier*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种全混合有限元方法，用于粘弹性组织中钙信号的双向耦合力学-化学模型，证明了全局存在唯一性，建立了半离散格式的适定性与最优先验误差估计，并对三类弱对称有限元给出显式收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对粘弹性组织中钙信号的力学-化学双向耦合问题（钙产生主动应力、局部膨胀反馈至钙动力学），需要一种能同时精确处理力学与反应-扩散耦合的数值框架，并保证其数学适定性与收敛性。

Method: 采用全混合有限元方法：力学部分基于Kelvin–Voigt本构，以弹性/粘性应力、速度和旋转张量为未知量，总应力对称性以弱形式施加；反应-扩散系统（钙与失活三磷酸肌醇）亦写成混合形式，引入扩散通量作为附加未知量。将耦合系统约化为积分-微分问题以证明全局存在唯一性，再恢复混合变量。半离散格式针对一般协调有限元空间构造，在单一inf-sup条件下证明适定，并构造与散度可交换、保持弱对称配对的插值算子以获得最优先验误差估计；进一步对三类弱对称有限元给出显式收敛率，并对其中一类给出位移的超收敛局部后处理。

Result: 证明了耦合系统的全局存在唯一性；半离散格式在单一inf-sup条件下适定；获得最优先验误差估计及三类弱对称有限元的显式收敛率，其中一类具有位移超收敛局部后处理；二维与三维数值实验验证了预测的收敛率，并通过粘弹性基准与力学驱动的钙波列展示方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该全混合有限元框架为粘弹性组织中钙信号的双向耦合提供了严格的理论保证与高效数值方法，适用于一般协调有限元空间，并通过数值实验验证了理论与实际可行性。

Abstract: We propose and analyse a fully mixed finite element method for a two-way coupled mechanochemical model of calcium signalling in viscoelastic tissue. The mechanical response follows a Kelvin--Voigt law, with the elastic and viscous stresses, velocity, and spin tensor as unknowns, and the symmetry of the total stress imposed weakly. The reaction-diffusion system for calcium and inactivated inositol triphosphate is also formulated in mixed form, introducing the diffusive flux as an additional unknown. The coupling is bidirectional: calcium generates an active stress, while local dilation feeds back into the calcium dynamics. We establish global existence and uniqueness by reducing the coupled system to an integro-differential problem and subsequently recovering the mixed variables. The semidiscrete scheme is formulated for general conforming finite element spaces and shown to be well posed under a single inf-sup condition. An interpolation operator that commutes with the divergence and preserves the weak symmetry pairing yields optimal a priori error estimates. We specialise the analysis to three families of weakly symmetric finite elements, deriving explicit convergence rates and, for one family, a superconvergent local post-processing of the displacement. Numerical tests in two and three dimensions confirm the predicted convergence rates and illustrate the method through viscoelastic benchmarks and mechanically driven calcium wavetrains.

</details>


### [127] [Energetic Variational Deep Learning for Solving High-Dimensional Stationary Fokker-Planck Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03325)
*Fanze Kong,Zhouyuan Qin,Shuangquan Xie*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种结合蒙特卡洛弱监督、能量变分物理约束与拟牛顿细化的深度学习框架，用于求解高维稳态Fokker-Planck方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维稳态Fokker-Planck方程因维数灾难与归一化/尺度约束困难而难以求解，需要兼具物理一致性与数值稳健性的方法。

Method: 以蒙特卡洛统计量锚定稳态密度的尺度，用自由能变分损失施加平衡态物理约束，并通过拟牛顿（quasi-Newton）方法进行细化；该表述可推广至含逐点正交旋转分量的稳态系统，并借助一族测试函数处理更一般的非梯度漂移系统。数值实验覆盖高维稳态Fokker-Planck问题，包括粗糙势与含噪数据场景。

Result: 数值结果在多种高维稳态Fokker-Planck问题上均获得准确且稳健的密度近似，在粗糙势和噪声数据下表现良好。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效缓解高维稳态Fokker-Planck求解中的尺度与物理约束难题，具备对非梯度漂移与含噪环境的推广能力。

Abstract: This paper develops a deep learning framework for solving high-dimensional stationary Fokker-Planck equations by combining Monte Carlo weak supervision, energy-variational physics constraints, and quasi-Newton refinement. Monte Carlo statistics anchor the scale of the stationary density, while a free-energy variational loss enforces equilibrium physics; the formulation also applies to stationary systems with pointwise orthogonal rotational components and, using a family of test functions, to systems with more general non-gradient drift. Numerical results demonstrate accurate and robust density approximations across a range of high-dimensional stationary Fokker-Planck problems, including rough-potential and noisy-data settings.

</details>


### [128] [Learning the Willmore flow with a lightweight neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03534)
*Elie Bretin,Roland Denis,Tokuhiro Eto,Simon Masnou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文将Bretin等人针对均值曲率运动的相场神经算子方法推广至Willmore流，提出基于Allen-Cahn半群Bence-Merriman-Osher型展开的轻量单时间步架构，并用于非定向点云的曲线与曲面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有相场神经算子工作仅处理均值曲率运动（二阶），而Willmore流（四阶、依赖曲率）在几何处理与重建中更具挑战，缺乏高效的神经算子建模；同时非定向界面情形下卷积核的取向性会引入各向异性动力学，需要合适的核设计与高效初始化策略。

Method: 将相场神经算子方法扩展到Willmore流：架构为轻量、单时间步形式，动机来自Allen-Cahn半群的Bence-Merriman-Osher型展开；训练数据由混合最小化运动（minimizing movement）格式生成的轨迹提供。针对非定向界面，对比非约束卷积核与径向核的效果；并利用二维训练核的径向Fourier剖面构造三维核，为无定向3D模型提供有效初始化。

Result: 在非定向界面实验中，非约束卷积核产生各向异性动力学，而径向核改善演化过程；由二维核经径向Fourier剖面构造的三维核为无定向3D模型提供了有效初始化。方法应用于非定向点云的曲线与曲面重建，并实现无需额外3D训练的迁移模型重建。

Conclusion: 相场神经算子可有效推广至Willmore流，径向核设计及2D到3D的核迁移是关键要素；该轻量单步架构在非定向点云重建中表现良好，且支持跨维度模型迁移而无需额外3D训练。

Abstract: We extend to the Willmore flow the phase-field neural operator approach proposed by Bretin, Denis, Masnou, and Terii (2022) for mean curvature motion in two and three dimensions. The resulting lightweight, single-time-step architecture is motivated by a Bence-Merriman-Osher-type expansion of the Allen-Cahn semigroup, and is trained using trajectories generated by a hybrid minimizing movement scheme. In our experiments with non-oriented interfaces, unconstrained convolution kernels produce anisotropic dynamics, whereas radial kernels enhance the evolution process. We also construct three-dimensional kernels from trained two-dimensional kernels via their radial Fourier profiles, which provides an effective initialization for the non-oriented 3D model. Finally, we show applications to curve and surface reconstruction from unoriented point clouds, including reconstruction using the transferred model without the need for additional 3D training.

</details>


### [129] [Error Estimates in Physics-Informed Neural Networks to Heat-Wave Fluid-Structure Interactive System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03575)
*Pelin G. Geredeli,Mohammad Mahabubur Rahman,Deepanshu Verma*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次为耦合流体-结构相互作用PDE系统的PINN近似建立了误差估计，证明泛化误差受训练误差、求积点数与网络规模控制，并给出数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实应用中的Stokes-弹性系统等耦合PDE系统误差分析比单一PDE更为困难，而PINN在此类耦合系统上的理论保证尚属空白，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究热-波模型（抛物型与双曲型方程通过在固定边界界面上的传输条件耦合）的PINN近似。将精确解与PINN解之间的误差用相关的PINN残差进行界定。

Result: 证明了泛化误差可由训练误差、求积点数量以及网络规模控制；并针对给定测试问题开展了数值实验。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首次针对耦合流体-结构相互作用PDE系统的PINN近似给出误差估计，为耦合PDE系统的PINN理论分析提供了基础。

Abstract: We study a physics-informed neural network (PINN) approximation of a coupled PDE system that models the interaction between fluid and elastic dynamics. The canonical heat-wave model considered here couples a parabolic and a hyperbolic equation through transmission conditions on a fixed boundary interface, and serves as a prototype for the Stokes-elastic systems that arises in real world applications. This coupling makes the error analysis more delicate than that for a single PDE. We bound the error between the exact and PINN solutions by the associated PINN residuals, and we moreover prove that the generalization error is controlled by the training error, the number of quadrature points, and the size of the network. {To the best of our knowledge}, these are the first error estimates for a PINN approximation of a coupled fluid-structure interaction PDE system. We also show that numerical experiments with respect to a given test problem.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 调试8 GeV脉冲慢引出质子束与μ子束线；使用专用Range Counter探测器，通过探测μ子在铜吸收体中停止后发射的延迟电子来鉴别停止的负μ子；由测得的衰变时间谱重建停止μ子数、动量谱和横向束流剖面；并用基于Geant4的模拟进行对比。

Result: 观测到停止μ子成分，证明慢负μ子已通过TS传输至实验区；重建动量谱显示传输束流集中于低动量区；横向剖面非均匀，与弯曲螺线管磁场中的电荷漂移定性一致；Geant4模拟能重现对降能片厚度和探测器位置的总体依赖关系，但测得产额系统性偏高约30%。

Conclusion: 该结果为COMET中低动量负μ子传输提供了首个实验基准，并为未来束流表征与μ子产生研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The COMET Phase-$α$ experiment, the initial beam-operation stage of the COMET muon-to-electron conversion programme at J-PARC, took place in 2023 and marked the first beam operation at the COMET facility. It served to commission the delivery of a bunched, slow-extracted 8 GeV proton beam and the muon beamline. Its objective was to demonstrate experimentally that low-momentum negative muons could be transported to the experimental area through the Transport Solenoid (TS). We report a measurement of the transported muon beam using the Range Counter, a dedicated detector that identifies stopped negative muons by detecting delayed electrons emitted after the muons stop in a copper absorber. From the measured decay-time spectra, we reconstruct the number of stopped muons, the corresponding momentum spectrum, and the transverse beam profile. The observation of the stopped-muon component demonstrates that slow negative muons were transported through the TS to the experimental area. The reconstructed momentum spectrum indicates that the transported beam is concentrated in the low-momentum region. The non-uniform transverse profile is qualitatively consistent with charge drift in the curved solenoidal magnetic field. A Geant4-based simulation reproduces the overall dependence on degrader thickness and detector position; the measured yields are systematically higher by roughly 30%. These results provide the first experimental benchmark of low-momentum negative-muon transport in COMET and establish the basis for future beam characterisation and production studies.

</details>


### [131] [Entanglement-sensitive observables in $e^+e^- \to τ^+τ^-$ at STCF: a detector-level feasibility study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03024)
*Chentao Bao,Xi Tao,Hai Chen,Lailin Xu,Xiaorong Zhou,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了在STCF上通过e+e-→τ+τ-产生过程探测量子纠缠的探测器层面可行性，利用ρρ和πρ衰变道重建τ自旋密度矩阵，并评估纠缠度C[ρ]和Bell型量m12[C]的测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠为相对论量子系统中的非经典自旋关联提供了直接探测手段，τ衰变产物可作为自旋分析器。本文旨在检验在STCF（√s=7 GeV）上开展此类测量的探测器层面可行性。

Method: 利用完整STCF探测器与重建框架模拟信号及主要本底过程，针对ρρ和πρ衰变道设计专门的重建与事例筛选流程；由重建的衰变运动学推断τ+τ-自旋密度矩阵，并在选定的fiducial区域内计算纠缠度C[ρ]及Bell敏感量m12[C]。

Result: ρρ和πρ道的总信号效率约为4.5%和4.8%，纯度约为87.6%和93.2%。在|cosθ|<0.2、积分亮度1 ab^-1条件下，两道的C[ρ]相对精度约2.2%；m12[C]相对精度在ρρ道约1.4%、πρ道约1.5%（含统计与探测器分辨率不确定度）。ρρ道的重建m12[C]相对参考阈值的分离更大。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在STCF上通过强子τ衰变探测量子纠缠与非经典自旋关联在探测器层面是可行的，其中ρρ拓扑对Bell型自旋关联尤为敏感。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement in $τ^+τ^-$ production provides a direct probe of non-classical spin correlations in a relativistic quantum system, with the $τ$ decay products serving as spin analyzers. We investigate the detector-level feasibility of such measurements in $e^+e^-\toτ^+τ^-$ at the Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) at $\sqrt{s}=7$ GeV, using the $ρρ$ and $πρ$ decay channels. Signal and dominant background processes are simulated with the full STCF detector and reconstruction framework. Dedicated reconstruction and event-selection procedures yield overall signal efficiencies of approximately $4.5\%$ and $4.8\%$, with corresponding purities of about $87.6\%$ and $93.2\%$ for the $ρρ$ and $πρ$ channels, respectively. From the reconstructed decay kinematics, the $τ^+τ^-$ spin-density matrix is inferred and the concurrence $C[ρ]$ together with the Bell-sensitive quantity $m_{12}[C]$ are evaluated in a selected fiducial region. For the benchmark requirement $|\cosθ|<0.2$ and an integrated luminosity of $1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the projected relative precision on $C[ρ]$ is about $2.2\%$ in both channels, while that on $m_{12}[C]$ is approximately $1.4\%$ for $ρρ$ and $1.5\%$ for $πρ$, including statistical and detector-resolution uncertainties. The reconstructed $m_{12}[C]$ shows a larger separation from its reference threshold in the $ρρ$ channel, making this topology particularly sensitive to Bell-type spin correlations. These results demonstrate the detector-level feasibility of probing quantum entanglement and non-classical spin correlations in hadronic $τ$ decays at STCF.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Super-Kamiokande在SK-Gd阶段利用钆中子俘获探测反应堆电子反中微子，以约6σ显著度确认信号，并测得太阳中微子振荡参数，与太阳中微子数据统计兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2020年Super-Kamiokande进入SK-Gd阶段，通过在探测器水中溶解钆来探测中子俘获，从而提升对低能电子反中微子的灵敏度，为反应堆反中微子测量及振荡参数检验提供新能力。

Method: 使用SK-Gd数据对反应堆电子反中微子进行分析，总曝光量为411.52个有效天。在扣除偶然、地球中微子和散裂本底后，将观测到的瞬发能谱与月度事件率同振荡后的反应堆反中微子预期进行比较，并开展太阳与反应堆联合拟合。

Result: 观测能谱与月事件率同振荡预期一致；无反应堆假设被以约6σ的反应堆反中微子信号显著度排除。振荡参数测得为 sin²θ₁₂ = 0.500±0.155，Δm²₂₁ = (9.08₋₀.₅₄⁺⁰.⁵⁵)×10⁻⁵ eV²。与Super-Kamiokande太阳中微子测量在1.9σ水平统计兼容。联合拟合给出 sin²θ₁₂ = 0.332₋₀.₀₂₆⁺⁰.⁰²⁸，Δm²₂₁ = (8.94₋₀.₄₇⁺⁰.⁴⁶)×10⁻⁵ eV²。

Conclusion: SK-Gd阶段成功实现反应堆电子反中微子探测并显著排除无反应堆假设；反应堆与太阳中微子数据集统计兼容，联合拟合给出了精度更高的太阳中微子振荡参数结果。

Abstract: In 2020, Super-Kamiokande entered the SK-Gd phase, improving its sensitivity to low-energy electron antineutrinos through the detection of neutrons captured on gadolinium dissolved in the detector water. An analysis of reactor electron antineutrino data has been carried out using data from SK-Gd, with a total exposure of 411.52 live days. The observed prompt energy spectra and monthly event rates are consistent with the oscillated reactor antineutrino prediction after accounting for accidental, geoneutrino, and spallation backgrounds. The no-reactor hypothesis is disfavored with a reactor-antineutrino signal significance of approximately $6σ$. The oscillation parameters are measured to be $\sin^2θ_{12} = 0.500\pm0.155$ and $Δm^2_{21} = (9.08_{-0.54}^{+0.55}) \times 10^{-5} \mathrm{eV}^2$. A comparison with Super-Kamiokande solar neutrino measurements shows a statistical compatibility between the two datasets at the $1.9σ$ level. A combined solar and reactor fit in Super-Kamiokande yields best-fit values of $\sin^2θ_{12} = 0.332_{-0.026}^{+0.028}$ and $Δm^2_{21} = (8.94_{-0.47}^{+0.46}) \times 10^{-5} \mathrm{eV}^2$.

</details>


### [133] [Track fitting as a language translation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03339)
*Deepak Samuel,Christy Elsa Koshy*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该探索性研究将粒子径迹重建类比为语言翻译问题，采用仅解码器Transformer架构与自定义分词器，将探测器信息"翻译"为物理径迹参数。在合成数据上，径迹参数精度较简单回归方法提升一个数量级，与RANSAC相当，多径迹事件识别准确率达98%；在真实宇宙μ子数据上，天顶角分布与预期吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子物理分析依赖逐步流程：收集单个探测器击中、重组为径迹、再拟合模型函数，流程繁琐且在多径迹事件表达上不够自然。受大语言模型在语言语法与语义理解上的出色能力启发，作者希望探索能否借助Transformer将粒子径迹重建重构为一种"语言翻译"任务，即把探测器语言翻译为物理语言，从而简化流程并自然处理多径迹事件。

Method: 将粒子径迹重建表述为语言翻译问题：探测器击中序列视为源语言，物理径迹参数视为目标语言。设计了一个仅解码器（decoder-only）的简洁架构，并配套自定义分词器（tokenizer）。在合成数据集上评估径迹参数精度及多径迹事件表达能力，并与简单回归方法及RANSAC方法对比；随后在真实宇宙μ子数据上测试模型表现。

Result: 在合成数据集上，模型径迹参数精度比简单回归技术高约一个数量级，与RANSAC方法相当；多径迹事件识别准确率达98%。在真实宇宙μ子事件分析中，模型给出的天顶角分布与预期高度吻合。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑型语言模型在此不寻常的任务设定下具有竞争力，并天然具备表达多径迹事件的能力。作者认为需在更复杂几何条件下进一步研究，以充分理解该技术的潜力。

Abstract: Particle physics analysis depends on information retrieved from particle tracks in a detector. Traditionally, this involves collecting individual detector hits, recombining them to form tracks, and fitting them to a model function. Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in comprehending the grammar and semantics of languages. Our exploratory study leverages transformers to conceptualise particle tracking as a language translation problem, wherein the language of detectors is translated into the language of physics. A simple decoder-only architecture with a custom tokenizer was designed for this task, and the behaviour of the model in this atypical setting provided interesting insights. We evaluated the advantages of this formulation in terms of track-parameter accuracy, and the ease of representing multi-track events, and finally tested the model on real cosmic-muon data. On synthetic datasets, the model outperformed a simple regression technique by an order of magnitude in track-parameter accuracy while being on par with the random sample consensus (RANSAC) method. The model also identified multi-track events with an accuracy of 98\%. The analysis of real cosmic-muon events showed an excellent agreement of the zenith angle distribution with expectations. Our results suggest that a compact language model shows competitive performance and a natural ability to represent multi-track events. Further studies with complex geometries are required to understand the full potential of this technique.

</details>


### [134] [Search for pair-produced vector-like top quarks decaying into Lorentz-boosted top quarks and scalar bosons in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03489)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据中搜寻成对产生的矢量类顶夸克T（衰变为标量玻色子φ与SM顶夸克），未观测到显著偏离，并给出截面与分支比乘积的95%置信度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量类夸克（VLQ）是超出标准模型新物理的重要预言粒子，可解决规范层级问题等；矢量类顶夸克T的成对产生及其衰变链 T→φ+t、φ→γγ 提供了特征性的双光子加全强子顶夸克对信号，值得系统搜寻。

Method: 利用CMS于2016–2018年在CERN LHC采集的√s=13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，积分亮度138 fb⁻¹；针对事件中来自一个标量玻色子衰变的双光子，伴随SM顶夸克对的全强子衰变；针对boosted顶夸克特征进行优化，将顶夸克衰变产物重建为大半径喷注（large-radius jets）；对一系列T与φ质量点分别开展搜寻。

Result: 未观察到与预期本底的显著偏离；最大局域超出为2.7个标准偏差，考虑look-elsewhere效应后降为1.5个标准偏差；据此在95%置信度下给出T Tbar产生截面与分支比乘积的上限。

Conclusion: 在所考察的T与φ质量范围内，数据与标准模型本底一致，未发现矢量类顶夸克T的显著性证据；结果以95% CL的截面×分支比上限形式给出，对相关新物理参数空间施加了约束。

Abstract: A search for pair-produced vector-like top quarks, T, decaying into a new scalar boson $φ$ and a standard model (SM) top quark is presented. The search targets events containing a photon pair originating from the decay of one of the scalar bosons, accompanied by all-hadronic decays of the SM top quark pair. The analysis is optimized for boosted top quark signatures in which the decay products of the top quarks are reconstructed as large-radius jets. The search is based on proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC during 2016$-$2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The search is performed for an array of T and $φ$ masses. No significant deviation from the predicted background is observed. The largest local excess has a significance of 2.7 standard deviations, reduced to 1.5 standard deviations after accounting for the look-elsewhere effect. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the product of $\mathrm{T\bar{T}}$ production cross section and the branching fraction.

</details>

### [135] [Noncommutative Marcus-Ree inequality and Erdős channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02294)
*Repana Devendra,Chaman Kumar Sahu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Marcus-Ree关于双随机矩阵Frobenius范数的不等式推广到量子信道，证明其在混合酉信道成立但在一般unital信道失效，并刻画了Erdős信道（取等号者）的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: unital量子信道是双随机矩阵的非交换类比。1959年Marcus和Ree证明了双随机矩阵的Frobenius范数平方不超过其最大对角和。作者希望建立该不等式的量子版本，并研究取等号的信道（Erdős信道）的结构与分类。

Method: 对$\mathbb{M}_d$上的混合酉信道证明量子版Marcus-Ree不等式，并给出反例说明一般unital信道不成立；证明迹保持条件期望是Erdős信道且在同Choi秩中局部孤立；引入量子RCDS信道（所有支撑置换对角和相等的非交换类比），证明其结构定理；对Choi秩2的Erdős信道给出酉等价下的显式形式，并刻画混合Weyl-酉信道中的Erdős信道。

Result: 混合酉信道满足量子Marcus-Ree不等式，一般unital信道不满足。迹保持条件期望是Erdős信道且局部孤立。得到量子RCDS信道的结构定理及其Erdős刻画。Choi秩2的Erdős信道有显式形式，且存在不可数多个酉等价类（与经典情形不同）。刻画了任意Choi秩混合Weyl-酉信道中的Erdős信道。对qubit，恰有四个酉等价类的Erdős信道，由恒等信道和Pauli信道的均匀平均表示。

Conclusion: 将经典Marcus-Ree不等式、RCDS双随机矩阵结构及Erdős刻画统一推广到量子信道框架；限制到对角信道时结果可recover经典结论。量子情形中Erdős信道酉等价类不可数，与经典有限情形形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: Unital quantum channels are the noncommutative analogues of doubly stochastic matrices. In 1959, Marcus and Ree showed that the squared Frobenius norm of a doubly stochastic matrix is at most its maximal diagonal sum. We prove a quantum version of this inequality for mixed unitary channels on $\mathbb{M}_d$ and give an example showing that it fails for general unital channels. As in the classical case, we call the channels attaining equality as Erdős channels.
  We show that the trace-preserving conditional expectation onto any unital $C^*$-subalgebra of $\mathbb{M}_d$ is an Erdős channel. It is also locally isolated among channels of the same Choi rank. Next, we introduce quantum RCDS channels. These are the noncommutative analogue of doubly stochastic matrices whose supported permutations all have the same diagonal sum. We prove a structure theorem for them and use it to characterize the quantum RCDS Erdős channels. We give an explicit form, up to unitary equivalence, for the Erdős channels of Choi rank $2$, and further show that there are uncountably many unitary equivalence classes of Erdős channels unlike the classical case. We also characterize the Erdős channels among mixed Weyl-unitary channels of any Choi rank.
  For qubits, we prove that there are exactly four unitary equivalence classes of Erdős channels and they are represented by the identity channel and uniform averages of Pauli channels. Finally, restricting to the diagonal channels, our results recover the Marcus--Ree inequality, the structure of RCDS doubly stochastic matrices, and the characterization of Erdős.

</details>


### [136] [Stratification of the space of density matrices via invariant theory and Uhlmann's connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02773)
*Howy Jordan,Markus J. Pflaum,Gerd Rudolph*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明密度矩阵空间的秩分解构成(b)-正则Whitney分层，并将Uhlmann联络几何化为分层向量丛上的分层联络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中已有论断认为密度矩阵状态空间按等秩分解可得到Whitney分层，但一直缺乏严格证明；同时Uhlmann联络的几何处理也需要分层框架。

Method: 运用不变量理论的方法，对矩阵代数的秩分解进行严格分析，证明其为Whitney意义下的(b)-正则分层；并引入分层向量丛与分层联络的概念来刻画Uhlmann联络。

Result: 给出秩分解构成(b)-正则Whitney分层的严格证明；证明Uhlmann联络可几何地视为分层向量丛上的分层联络，且在纯态上退化为Berry联络；附录给出分层纤维丛与分层联络的自足阐述。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了密度矩阵等秩分解为Whitney分层这一论断缺乏严格证明的空白，并建立了Uhlmann联络的分层几何框架，所提出的分层联络概念具有独立价值。

Abstract: The space of $n\times n$ density matrices is the full state space of an $n$-level quantum system. While it has been asserted in the literature on quantum information theory that decomposing this state space into equirank pieces yields a Whitney stratification, rigorous proofs have remained absent. Using methods from invariant theory, this work provides a proof that the rank decomposition of the matrix algebra constitutes a (b)-regular stratification in the sense of Whitney. Furthermore, we demonstrate how the Uhlmann connection - which describes the adiabatic holonomy of mixed states and reduces to the Berry connection on pure states - can be geometrically treated as a stratified connection on a stratified vector bundle. Finally, our appendix provides a self-contained exposition of stratified fiber bundles and the new concept of stratified connections, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [137] [The monatomic limit of polyatomic gases as concentration of the internal-energy measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03060)
*Tommaso Ruggeri*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究Rational Extended Thermodynamics中多原子气体向单原子极限的奇异行为：证明在$a=(D-5)/2$时内部能量归一化平衡分布随$D\to3$集中于$I=0$，为Dirac处方及其一阶修正提供严格依据，并讨论ET14中负动态压力的容许性与相容初值需求，以及相对论情形下的伽马分布、能量恒等式与Synge能量一阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多原子气体在Racional Extended Thermodynamics中向单原子极限过渡时往往出现奇异性，经典做法依赖Dirac处方但缺乏严格论证；同时负动态压力的物理容许性及相容初值条件尚需澄清，相对论推广中内部能量分布与Synge能量的修正也有待刻画。

Method: 对内部态密度$\varphi(I)=I^a$取$a=(D-5)/2$，证明归一化平衡分布在$D\to3$时集中于$I=0$，给出Dirac处方及一阶修正的严格证明；在经典ET14中分析内部模态能量偏移$-3\Pi/2$、正性约束$-p<\Pi<(D-3)p/3$及$\Pi=0$处熵的边界角；在相对论情形下于固定动量导出条件伽马分布，计算本构积分并推导精确能量恒等式与Synge能量的一阶修正。

Result: 证明归一化平衡分布在$D\to3$时集中于$I=0$，严格验证Dirac处方及其一阶修正；正性给出$-p<\Pi<(D-3)p/3$，精确熵在$\Pi=0$处出现边界角；负动态压力可容许，但固定负值在给定总能量下不趋近单原子态；相对论中内部能量在固定动量下为条件伽马分布，本构积分可直接求得，并得到精确能量恒等式与Synge能量的一阶修正。此外，解释了相容初值的必要性。

Conclusion: 单原子极限的奇异性可通过分布集中严格处理，Dirac处方具有合理性；负动态压力需配合相容初值才能正确趋于单原子态；相对论内部能量分布与本构结构可由伽马分布统一描述，并给出Synge能量的精确修正框架。

Abstract: In Rational Extended Thermodynamics, the monatomic limit of polyatomic gases is often singular. For the internal-state density $φ(I)=I^a$, with $a=(D-5)/2$, we prove that the normalized equilibrium distribution of the molecular internal energy $I$ concentrates at $I=0$ as $D\to3$, providing a rigorous justification of the Dirac prescription and its first-order correction. In classical ET14, the internal-mode energy differs from equilibrium by $-3Π/2$; positivity yields $-p<Π<(D-3)p/3$, while the exact entropy develops a boundary corner at $Π=0$. Negative dynamic pressures remain admissible, but fixed negative values do not approach the monatomic state with the prescribed total energy. This explains the need for compatible initial data. In the relativistic case, the internal energy is conditionally gamma distributed at fixed momentum; the constitutive integrals follow directly, and we derive an exact energy identity and the first correction to the Synge energy.

</details>


### [138] [On the Cauchy Problem for the Sawada-Kotera Equation: Soliton resolution conjecture, Painlevé transcendents and asymptotic stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03097)
*Zheng-Kang Huang,Shou-Fu Tian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究含连续谱与有限离散谱的Sawada-Kotera方程在不同时空区域的长时渐近行为，证明孤子分解猜想、给出F-XVIII Painlevé超越函数渐近、建立N-孤子解的渐近稳定性，并首次将长时渐近理论推广到孤子与辐射共存的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Sawada-Kotera方程的长时渐近结果局限于纯连续谱情形，无法刻画孤子与辐射共存的物理场景。本文旨在突破这一限制，建立含离散谱（即存在孤子）的一般可容许数据下的完整长时渐近理论，同时证明孤子分解猜想与N-孤子渐近稳定性。

Method: 核心工具包括：一是新的亚纯Riemann-Hilbert框架，通过完整的六点极点轨道纳入离散谱，并保持余因子解析性与谱原点处的奇异结构；二是轨道式、区域相关的极点约化机制与ṧ∂-非线性最速下降法相结合，按区域选取贡献的孤子并将其从连续辐射中分离；三是借助正则修正SK问题与Miura变换处理谱原点处合并的驻点。

Result: 在不同时空区域得到匹配的长时渐近展开：证明了孤子分解猜想；渐近主项由F-XVIII Painlevé超越函数刻画；建立了N-孤子解的渐近稳定性；并在包含离散谱的可容许数据下实现孤子与辐射共存区制的长时渐近分析。

Conclusion: 该工作将Sawada-Kotera方程的长时渐近理论从纯连续谱推广到孤子与辐射共存的一般情形，所发展的亚纯Riemann-Hilbert框架与区域相关极点约化机制为含离散谱可积系统的渐近分析提供了可推广的新范式。

Abstract: We derive long-time asymptotics in different space-time regions for the Sawada-Kotera (SK) equation with admissible data involving continuous spectrum and finite discrete spectrum. Our result proves the soliton resolution conjecture, yields the asymptotics governed by the F-XVIII Painlevé transcendent, and establishes the asymptotic stability of \(N\)-soliton solutions. More importantly, this extends the long-time asymptotic theory to the coexistence of solitons and radiation, whereas previous results were restricted to the purely continuous spectrum. One key ingredient in our argument is a new meromorphic Riemann-Hilbert framework that incorporates the discrete spectrum through complete six-point pole orbits while preserving cofactor analyticity and the singular structure at the spectral origin. A second ingredient is the combination of an orbitwise, region-dependent pole reduction mechanism and the \(\bar{\partial}\)-nonlinear steepest descent method, which allows us to select the solitons contributing in each region and separate them from the continuous radiation. Our framework also resolves the coalescing stationary points at the singular spectral origin through the regular modified SK problem and the Miura map.

</details>


### [139] [Conformal Bootstrap for surfaces with boundary in Liouville CFT. Part II: spectral resolution and bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03539)
*Colin Guillarmou,Rémi Rhodes,Baojun Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是带边界紧致曲面上Liouville共形场论共形bootstrap证明的第二部分，研究半环带半群的谱理论并导出bootstrap公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Part 1建立的Segal公理与粘合性质基础上，需要进一步理解半环带半群的生成元、边界态空间的谱分解，并最终得到一般关联函数的bootstrap表达。

Method: 将半环带半群的生成元等同于边界Hamiltonian，利用散射方法给出其谱分解；由此得到边界态空间关于不可约Virasoro表示的直接积分分解，由边界谱测度控制。结合边界形变的Ward恒等式，将一般关联函数表示为沿切割曲线的谱参数积分，被积函数为bulk与boundary结构常数及相应共形块的乘积。

Result: 建立了半环带半群的谱分解与边界态空间的直接积分分解；给出了关联函数以谱参数积分形式表达的bootstrap公式。

Conclusion: 这些结果支撑共形块解析性与对称性，并有助于在共形块空间上构造映射类群表示。

Abstract: This paper is the second part of the proof of the conformal bootstrap for Liouville conformal field theory on compact surfaces with boundary. It is devoted to the spectral theory of the half-annulus semigroup and to the resulting bootstrap formula. Building on the Segal axioms and gluing properties established in Part~1~\cite{GRW1}, we identify the generator of the half-annulus semigroup with the boundary Hamiltonian and establish its spectral decomposition by scattering methods. This yields a direct-integral decomposition of the boundary state space into irreducible Virasoro representations, governed by the boundary spectral measure. Combining this decomposition with Ward identities for boundary deformations, we express general correlation functions as integrals over spectral parameters attached to cutting curves, with integrands given by products of bulk and boundary structure constants and the corresponding conformal blocks. These results also underpin the analyticity and symmetry properties of conformal blocks~\cite{GRSSbloc}, and contribute to the construction of mapping class group representations on spaces of conformal blocks~\cite{PapierBlocs}.

</details>


### [140] [Virasoro Conformal Blocks and modular functor from Liouville CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03540)
*Guillaume Baverez,Colin Guillarmou,Antti Kupiainen,Rémi Rhodes,Yuxiao Xie*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在中心荷 c>25 时，于含 m 个标记点、亏格 g 的黎曼曲面 Teichmüller 空间上整体构造了 Virasoro 共形块，证明其为全纯线丛的整体全纯截面并满足 Ward 恒等式；对给定标记曲面和裤子分解，共形块空间同构于 L²(R_+^{3g-3+m})，且承载映射类群的射影酉表示，并给出 Moore-Seiberg 移动下的酉同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Virasoro 共形块多在形式幂级数或局部范畴框架下研究，缺乏在 Teichmüller 空间上的整体解析构造。作者希望建立整体全纯的共形块并刻画其共形不变性与模空间上的表示论性质。

Method: 在 c>25 情形下，于亏格 g、m 个标记点的黎曼曲面 Teichmüller 空间上整体构造 Virasoro 共形块；利用全纯线丛的全纯截面实现共形块，并验证 Ward 恒等式；以标记裤子分解为框架，借助涉及 DOZZ 结构常数的测度建立 Hilbert 空间同构，并研究映射类群作用与 Moore-Seiberg 移动。

Result: 共形块是某全纯线丛的整体全纯截面且满足 Ward 恒等式，从而具备共形不变性；三点球面情形共形块空间为一维；对给定标记黎曼曲面及标记裤子分解，共形块空间一般为无限维 Hilbert 空间，且关于含 DOZZ 结构常数的测度同构于 L²(R_+^{3g-3+m})；该空间承载映射类群的射影酉表示，并对每个 Moore-Seiberg 移动存在酉同构。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 c>25 时 Virasoro 共形块的整体全纯构造，揭示了其 Hilbert 空间结构与 DOZZ 测度的联系，并建立了映射类群的射影酉表示及 Moore-Seiberg 移动的酉同构，为共形场论的模空间几何表述提供了整体框架。

Abstract: In this article, we give a global construction of the Virasoro conformal blocks on Teichmüller space of Riemann surfaces of genus $g$ with $m$ marked points, when the central charge is $c>25$. We prove that they are global holomorphic sections of a holomorphic line bundle and they satisfy the Ward identity, which encodes their conformal invariance. For the sphere with $3$ points, the space of conformal blocks is one-dimensional. For a given marked Riemann surface and a marked pair of pants decomposition, it is in general an infinite dimensional Hilbert space, isomorphic to $L^2(\R_+^{3g-3+m})$ with respect to some measure involving the DOZZ structure constants. We show that it carries a projective unitary representation of the mapping class group. More generally, there are unitary isomorphisms for each Moore-Seiberg move, changing a marked pair of pants decomposition into another one.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Parabolic induction for Takiff algebras and character formulas in Chari's category $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02787)
*Fulin Chen,Xin Huang,Ruling Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究Takiff代数 $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$ 的抛物子代数分类及其抛物诱导模的不可约性判据，并应用于仿射李代数范畴 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$ 中Harish-Chandra模的特征公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典半单李代数 $\mathfrak{g}$ 的抛物诱导模理论（尤其是Jantzen不可约性判据）推广到Takiff代数 $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$，并借此刻画仿射李代数Chari范畴 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$ 中Harish-Chandra模的特征，填补相应理论的空白。

Method: 定义 $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$ 的抛物子代数，用 $\mathfrak{g}$ 单根系上的 $(N+2)$-分拆对其进行分类；研究相应的抛物诱导 $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$-模，建立其不可约性的充要判据（推广Jantzen判据）。

Result: 给出 $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$ 抛物子代数的完整分类；建立抛物诱导模不可约性的充要判据；得到Chari范畴 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$ 中几乎所有不可约Harish-Chandra模的特征公式；当 $\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$ 时得到该范畴中所有不可约Harish-Chandra模的显式特征公式。

Conclusion: Takiff代数的抛物诱导理论可作为研究仿射李代数Harish-Chandra模的有力工具，将Jantzen判据推广至该框架并导出新的特征公式。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{g}$ be a finite-dimensional complex semisimple Lie algebra, and let $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{g}\otimes (\mathbb{C}[t]/t^{N+1}\mathbb{C}[t])$ denote the $N$-th Takiff algebra of $\mathfrak{g}$. We introduce a notion of parabolic subalgebras of $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$ and classify them in terms of $(N+2)$-partitions of a simple root system of $\mathfrak{g}$. We then study the corresponding parabolically induced $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$-modules and establish a necessary and sufficient criterion for their irreducibility, extending Jantzen's criterion for parabolically induced $\mathfrak{g}$-modules. As an application, we derive character formulas for almost all irreducible Harish-Chandra modules in Chari's category $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$ over the (centerless) affine Lie algebra associated with $\mathfrak{g}$. In particular, when $\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$, we obtain explicit character formulas for all irreducible Harish-Chandra modules in this category.

</details>


### [142] [Maximal graded subalgebras of simple vectorial Lie superalgebras with polynomial coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03340)
*Dimitry Leites,Irina Shchepochkina*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文对具有多项式系数的单（或接近单的Cartan延拓）向量李超代数，在与Weisfeiler滤链相关的分次下，描述了极大的单（及接近单的）分次子代数；结合Shchepochkina关于矩阵李超代数极大子代数的工作，解决了Dynkin问题的超化与无穷维版本（除3类例外环境外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sophus Lie最早尝试分类单向量李代数（由多项式系数向量场构成）的极大子代数，但如附录所示，该问题本质上是"wild"的：描述全部子代数包含矩阵对在同时共轭下的分类，因而子代数过多，无法逐一刻画。研究者转而区分若干在应用中重要的极大子代数类。本文旨在对单（或接近单的Cartan延拓）向量李超代数完成这一分类的超化版本，并与已有矩阵李超代数结果结合，解决Dynkin问题的超与无穷维推广。

Method: 考虑具有多项式系数的单（或接近单的Cartan延拓）向量李超代数，赋予与Weisfeiler滤链相关的分次结构，在此框架下描述极大的单（及接近单的）分次子代数。方法上是对Sophus Lie原始工作的超化处理，并与I. Shchepochkina关于矩阵李超代数极大子代数的描述（arXiv:hep-th/9702122）相结合。

Result: 给出了单（或接近单）向量李超代数在Weisfeiler分次下极大单（及接近单）分次子代数的完整描述；结合Shchepochkina的矩阵李超代数结果，实现了对单有限维李代数极大子代数（Dynkin问题）在超与无穷维情形下的求解，仅余3类例外环境子代数留待他处处理。

Conclusion: 本文与Shchepochkina的工作共同解决了Dynkin问题的超化与无穷维版本——即单有限维李代数极大子代数的描述——除3类例外环境外。这表明通过对分次结构与Cartan延拓的恰当处理，原本"wild"的极大子代数分类问题在分次意义下是可解且可控的。

Abstract: S. Lie was the first to try to classify maximal subalgebras of simple vectorial (i.e., consisting of vector fields) Lie algebras with polynomial coefficients. In Appendix it is shown that, as stated, the problem is wild: describing all subalgebras contains the classification of pairs of matrices up to simultaneous conjugation, so there are too many such subalgebras. Several researchers distinguished various classes of maximal subalgebras important in applications.
  Here, in simple (or in Cartan prolongations close to simple) vectorial Lie superalgebras with polynomial coefficients considered with gradings associated with Weisfeiler filtrations, we describe maximal simple (and close to simple) graded subalgebras --- a superization of what Sophus Lie tackled.
  Together with I. Shchepochkina's description of the maximal subalgebras of matrix Lie superalgebras, see arXiv:hep-th/9702122, this paper solves the super and infinite-dimensional version of Dynkin's problem --- the description of the maximal subalgebras of simple finite-dimensional Lie algebras --- except for the 3 exceptional ambients considered elsewhere.

</details>


### [143] [Maximal subalgebras in the three exceptional simple finite-dimensional Lie superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03354)
*Dimitry Leites,Oleksandr Lozhechnyk*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文解决了Dynkin极大子代数分类问题在三个特殊单李超代数（$\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$）上的遗留情形，完整分类了它们的分次极大子代数，并给出两个副产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dynkin分类了单有限维李代数的极大子代数。Shchepochkina解决了矩阵李超代数的超版本问题，Leites与Shchepochkina在姊妹篇中解决了具有多项式系数并按Weisfeiler分次化的单向量李超代数情形，但遗留三个例外：$\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$。它们以多种方式成为向量型，无法被一般论证覆盖。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对分次子代数，作者对三个李超代数分别列举方法适用的所有$\mathbb{Z}$-分次（$\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$有两种、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$有五种、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$有八种），并对每种分次描述全部极大分次子代数，其中半单者显式给出。利用一个简单判据将多数结果从"极大分次"升级为"极大"。所有计算在$\mathbb{Q}$上进行。

Result: 完整给出了三种李超代数在各$\mathbb{Z}$-分次下的全部极大分次子代数，半单部分显式列出；多数结果通过判据升级为极大子代数。副产品一：向量李超代数的分次子代数经典分为五类，当环境深度$>1$时不完整，须增加第六类，且恰在此处非空。副产品二：内蕴计算了$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$中所含$\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$副本的参数$a$。

Conclusion: 本文补齐了Dynkin问题超版本在三个例外李超代数上的分次情形，修正了向量李超代数分次子代数的五类划分（需增第六类），并内蕴确定了相关参数$a$，所有结果均在$\mathbb{Q}$上计算。

Abstract: Dynkin classified the maximal subalgebras of the simple finite-dimensional Lie algebras. The super version of his problem is solved, for the matrix Lie superalgebras, by Shchepochkina, and, for the simple vectorial ones with polynomial coefficients taken with their Weisfeiler gradings, in the companion paper by Leites and Shchepochkina --- with three exceptions left open: the three simple finite-dimensional Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$, $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$, which are vectorial in more than one way and are not covered by the general argument.
  Here we settle these three cases for \textbf{graded} subalgebras. For each of the three Lie superalgebras, we list all the $\mathbb{Z}$-gradings to which the method of that paper applies --- two for $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$, five for $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$, eight for $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ --- and describe, for every one of them, all the maximal graded subalgebras, the semi-simple ones explicitly. A simple criterion upgrades most of the answers from ``maximal graded\rq\rq\ to ``maximal\rq\rq. Every answer is computed over $\mathbb{Q}$.
  Two by-products: the classical list of \textbf{five} cases into which the graded subalgebras of a vectorial Lie superalgebra split is incomplete for ambients of depth $>1$ --- a sixth case has to be added, and it is non-empty precisely here; and the parameter $a$ of the copies of $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$ sitting inside $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$, resp. $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$, is computed intrinsically.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [The geometry of monarch matrix approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02226)
*Shintaro Yoshizawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将秩-r矩阵流形上最小二乘距离的Morse理论推广到Monarch矩阵（置换矩阵与两个块对角因子之积），刻画了规范维数、Bruhat型胞腔的余维与闭包关系，并给出等块情形下的Monarch-Schur定理、一般下界、Morse指标公式、焦点集以及四阶正则化下的全局收敛与最小范数解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helmke与Shayman的工作表明，对一般目标，到秩-r矩阵流形的最小二乘距离是非退化Morse函数，其指标由Bruhat胞腔余维决定。作者希望为Monarch矩阵这一结构化分解流形建立类似理论，以理解其几何、优化景观与权重衰减的作用。

Method: 通过研究Monarch矩阵（固定置换矩阵与两个块对角因子之积）的参数化，引入转移矩阵不变量来刻画规范维数、Bruhat型胞腔余维及闭包关系；在等块情形证明Monarch-Schur定理，并对一般分块导出下界；进一步证明一般二阶非退化性、推导Morse指标公式、显式给出测度为零的焦点集，并分析无正则化梯度流的非强制性及四阶正则化效果。

Result: 规范维数与Bruhat型胞腔余维/闭包关系由同一转移矩阵不变量决定但一般不相等；等块情形下Monarch-Schur定理刻画了最小化规范自由度并最大化流形维数的置换，一般分块有下界；建立了二阶非退化性、Morse指标公式与显式焦点集；无正则化梯度流因无界平坦方向缺乏强制性，四阶正则化恢复全局收敛，在存在精确解时选出规范最小范数解，否则给出良定义的最佳逼近像。

Conclusion: 该工作将Morse-Bruhat理论推广至Monarch矩阵流形，揭示其参数化几何与优化性质，并为结构化因子分解层中的权重衰减提供几何解释。

Abstract: Helmke and Shayman showed that, for generic targets, least-squares distance to the manifold of rank-r matrices is a nondegenerate Morse function whose indices are governed by Bruhat-cell codimensions. We develop the analogous theory for Monarch matrices, products of a fixed permutation matrix with two block-diagonal factors. The parametrization's gauge dimension, as well as the codimensions and closure relations of the associated Bruhat-type cells, is determined by a common transfer-matrix invariant, although these quantities are generally distinct. In the equal-block case, we prove the Monarch-Schur theorem, characterizing the permutations that minimize gauge freedom and maximize manifold dimension, and derive a general lower bound for arbitrary partitions. We further establish generic second-order nondegeneracy, a Morse-index formula, and an explicit focal set of measure zero. Finally, we show that the unregularized gradient flow lacks coercivity because of unbounded flat directions. A quartic regularizer restores global convergence, selects a canonical minimum-norm exact solution when one exists, and otherwise yields a well-defined best-approximation image. The regularizer preserves the Monarch architecture and provides a geometric interpretation of weight decay in structured factorized layers.

</details>


### [145] [Accelerated Safe Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02433)
*Xuting Gao,Sonia Martínez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出加速安全梯度流（A-SGF），将二阶加速流经二阶控制障碍函数二次规划滤波，用于强凸约束优化，在保证安全性的同时保持Nesterov加速收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强凸约束优化中，既要实现加速收敛，又需保证约束（安全）始终满足。传统加速方法在约束下难以兼顾可行性与加速性能，因此需要一种能过滤加速流、维持安全集前向不变性的机制。

Method: 提出A-SGF：对名义二阶加速流，通过基于二阶控制障碍函数（CBF）的二次规划进行滤波。建立所得向量场的严格可行性与局部Lipschitz连续性条件，证明提升安全集的前向不变性，并验证其唯一平衡点为约束极小值点。采用变分Lyapunov分析分离安全修正项的影响，推导障碍增益的显式条件。

Result: 证明了提升安全集前向不变，唯一平衡点为约束最优点；给出障碍增益条件，在此条件下A-SGF保持与无约束Nesterov加速法相同的收敛率。非线性二维算例验证了任意时刻可行性与更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: A-SGF能在强凸约束优化中同时实现安全性和加速收敛，为其理论性质提供了严格保证，并通过数值算例验证有效性。

Abstract: This letter introduces an accelerated safe gradient flow (A-SGF) for
  strongly convex constrained optimization problems. The A-SGF filters
  a nominal second-order accelerated flow through a second-order
  control barrier function quadratic program. We establish conditions
  for strict feasibility and local Lipschitz continuity of the
  resulting vector field, prove forward invariance of a lifted safe
  set, and show that its unique equilibrium is the constrained
  minimizer. A variational Lyapunov analysis isolates the effect of
  the safety correction and gives explicit conditions on the barrier
  gains under which A-SGF retains the same convergence rate as the
  unconstrained Nesterov accelerated method. A nonlinear 2D example
  demonstrate anytime feasibility and faster convergence of the
  algorithm.

</details>


### [146] [Fully Coupled Nonlinear Mean-Field FBSΔEs: Solvability and an LQ Buffer-Adjustment Illustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02467)
*Yingjie Zheng,Qingxin Meng,Maoning Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为完全耦合非线性平均场正倒向随机差分方程（依赖无条件一阶矩）的有限时域可解性建立了充分条件，利用支配-单调性方法给出唯一平方可积适应解、先验界与系数稳定性，并通过非线性算例与平均场LQ缓冲调整模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全耦合非线性平均场FBSDE的有限时域可解性理论尚不完善，尤其当方程依赖无条件一阶矩、前向递推含条件投影时，现有支配-单调性方法难以直接适用，且通常需要原系数满足小性限制。

Method: 在前向递推中使用下一时刻倒向状态及其与创新项乘积的条件投影；在中心坐标与均值坐标下构造确定性矩阵组合，并证明关于同伦参数一致连续的延拓估计；结合全局Lipschitz连续性与一个主动的支配-强制方向进行论证；对相反单调性方向施加符号变换处理；主动参数可归一化，无需对原系数附加小性约束；并用非线性算例、标量平均场LQ缓冲调整模型及精确有限情景树计算进行检验。

Result: 在全局Lipschitz连续与单一主动支配-强制方向下，得到唯一平方可积适应解、先验界与系数稳定性；主动参数可归一化而不需原系数的小性条件；非线性算例（含饱和状态与均值交互及退化支配方向）验证了假设；平均场LQ缓冲调整模型中，Hamiltonian解刻画了唯一开环最优控制，且精确有限情景树计算与直接二次优化结果一致。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了支配-单调性方法至依赖无条件一阶矩的完全耦合非线性平均场FBSDE，给出无需原系数小性条件的可解性判据，并通过理论与数值算例验证了结论的适用性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper establishes sufficient conditions for finite-horizon solvability of fully coupled nonlinear mean-field forward--backward stochastic difference equations with dependence on unconditional first moments. The forward recursion uses conditional projections of the next backward state and its product with the innovation. Building on existing domination--monotonicity methods, we formulate deterministic matrix combinations in centered and mean coordinates and prove a continuation estimate uniform in the homotopy parameter. Global Lipschitz continuity and one active domination--coercivity direction yield a unique square-integrable adapted solution, an a priori bound, and coefficient stability. The active parameter can be normalized without imposing an additional smallness restriction on the original coefficients. A sign transformation handles the opposite monotonicity orientation. A nonlinear example with saturating state and mean interactions verifies the assumptions, including degenerate domination directions. A scalar mean-field LQ buffer-adjustment model illustrates the theorem: its Hamiltonian solution characterizes the unique open-loop optimizer. An exact finite scenario-tree calculation checks this characterization against direct quadratic optimization.

</details>


### [147] [Mean-variance portfolio selection: connections between mean-field systems and forward-backward stochastic systems under initial-terminal cost coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02485)
*Xun Li,Hongyu Shi,Zhen Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究均值场系统与由均值-方差投资组合选择问题导出的前向-后向随机系统之间的联系，通过终端财富的条件期望过程将均值场项表示为后向状态，建立带初始-终端耦合值的随机最大值原理，并求解广义随机线性二次控制问题及有效前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值-方差投资组合选择问题自然产生均值场系统与前向-后向随机系统，且其成本泛函中存在前向状态终端值与后向状态初始值的耦合，使得经典前向-后向随机最优控制方法无法直接应用，因此需要建立新的理论工具。

Method: 引入终端财富的条件期望过程，将均值场项表示为后向状态，从而将问题转化为带耦合初始-终端值的前向-后向随机控制问题；建立含运行成本与耦合初始-终端值的随机最大值原理并给出最优性充分条件；研究允许控制权重不定的广义随机线性二次控制问题，通过Riccati方程导出最优反馈表示。

Result: 建立了适用于带运行成本与耦合初始-终端值的随机最大值原理及最优性充分条件；对允许不定控制权重的广义随机线性二次问题给出基于Riccati方程的最优反馈表示；将经典均值-方差投资组合选择模型作为特例，显式给出有效投资策略与有效前沿，并通过数值模拟考察参数敏感性及其金融含义。

Conclusion: 均值场系统与前向-后向随机系统之间的转化框架有效克服了初始-终端耦合带来的困难，所提出的随机最大值原理与Riccati方程方法可成功求解均值-方差投资组合选择问题，并给出显式有效前沿与策略，验证了理论的适用性与金融解释力。

Abstract: This paper investigates the connection between mean-field systems and forward-backward stochastic systems arising from the mean-variance portfolio selection problem. By introducing the conditional expectation process of terminal wealth, the mean-field term is represented by a backward state, leading to a class of control problems for forward-backward stochastic systems with coupled initial-terminal values. The coupling between the terminal value of the forward state and the initial value of the backward state in the cost functional prevents classical methods for forward-backward stochastic optimal control from being directly applicable. To overcome this difficulty, we establish a stochastic maximum principle for control problems with running costs and coupled initial-terminal values, and provide sufficient conditions for optimality. Furthermore, we investigate a class of generalized stochastic linear-quadratic control problems with initial-terminal coupling, allowing the control weight in the running cost to be indefinite, and derive the optimal feedback representation via a Riccati equation. The classical mean-variance portfolio selection model is shown to be a special case, for which the corresponding efficient investment strategy and efficient frontier are obtained explicitly, and numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the results and examine parameter sensitivity and its financial implications.

</details>


### [148] [Recursive Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Control with Indefinite Weights for Jump-Diffusion Systems with Random Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02530)
*Xinyu Ma,Yang Liu,Qingxin Meng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究有限时域随机LQ控制，对象为带界随机系数、不定权与线性递归代价的有限活动跳扩散；通过测度变换与积分因子将递归准则化为非递归LQ泛函，在变换测度下建立控制空间交集中的递归赋值，并由随机Riccati方程给出反馈表示、比较与唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机LQ理论多针对Brownian扩散与定常/确定系数、正定权代价；本文关注更一般的有限活动跳扩散、随机系数与不定权，且代价由BSDE（线性递归）给出，需在递归与非递归框架之间建立等价与可解性。

Method: 采用Brownian-Poisson测度变换与积分因子，将递归LQ准则约化为非递归LQ泛函；在物理与变换平方可积控制空间交集上，对L^1二次数据证明递归赋值与完全变换值相等；在变换一致凸与控制无关跳映射可逆条件下，从非递归值核识别随机Riccati方程，并用条件能量估计将鞅系数转回物理测度；最后用完全平方给出反馈表示。

Result: 建立了递归赋值与变换值的等价性；在变换一致凸与跳映射可逆下识别出随机Riccati方程，并实现系数从变换测度到物理测度的转移；得到反馈表示、比较定理及反馈容许类中的唯一性；指出对每个初始对，递归值恰在变换最优控制具有有限物理控制能量时可达。

Conclusion: 该工作将随机LQ理论推广到带不定权与线性递归代价的有限活动跳扩散，给出可解的Riccati刻画与反馈最优性条件；算例展示了非零反馈与在负控制权下仍具一致凸性的情形。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon stochastic linear-quadratic (LQ) control for finite-activity jump diffusions with bounded random coefficients, indefinite weights and a linear recursive cost. The cost is defined by a backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) whose generator has bounded deterministic coefficients multiplying the Brownian and jump integrands. A Brownian-Poisson change of measure and an integrating factor reduce the criterion to a nonrecursive LQ functional. On the intersection of the physical and transformed square-integrable control spaces, we establish recursive evaluation for L^1 quadratic data under the transformed measure and equality with the full transformed value. Under transformed uniform convexity and invertibility of the uncontrolled jump map, we identify the stochastic Riccati equation from the nonrecursive value kernel. Conditional energy estimates justify transferring its martingale coefficients to the physical measure. Completion of squares gives feedback representation, comparison and uniqueness in the feedback-admissible class. For each initial pair, the recursive value is attained exactly when the transformed optimizer has finite physical control energy. Analytical examples illustrate nonzero feedback and uniform convexity with a negative control weight.

</details>


### [149] [Second-Order Mean-Field Schrödinger Bridges with Partial State Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02537)
*Asmaa Eldesoukey,Md Zulfiqur Haider,Italo Napolitano,Yongxin Chen,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对惯性多智能体系统的Schrödinger桥问题新表述，其中仅在端点指定位置分布而速度自由度未知，推导了最优性系统并设计了嵌套不动点算法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制系统决策往往只能获得关于状态的**部分信息**。因此作者希望在惯性（含速度）多智能体系统中，仅给定集体位置的部分规格（聚合种群快照），而不指定速度，探索如何将集合驱动到匹配这些部分规格的状态。

Method: 在**平均场**框架下，集体动力学用带非局部相互作用的非线性 Vlasov--Fokker--Planck 积分-偏微分方程描述。作者在有限时间区间端点只指定位置分布，速度不受约束，据此推导对应的**最优性系统**，识别最优受控演化为通过前向与后向时间传播同时受初始与终端规格影响。进一步分析部分端点规格对最优控制与一致演化带来的特殊条件。为求解该系统，提出受经典 **Fortet--Sinkhorn** 迭代启发的**嵌套不动点算法**，扩展以处理非局部相互作用和时间耦合带来的非线性。

Result: 推导出部分端点约束下的最优性系统，揭示最优控制需同时综合初始与终端规格信息并进行双向（前向-后向）传播；证明部分端点规格会产生区别于完整规格情形的最优控制和一致性演化条件；提出的嵌套不动点算法能够处理非局部相互作用与时间耦合非线性并计算最优性系统的解。

Conclusion: 该工作将Schrödinger桥问题推广到惯性相互作用智能体系统并仅施加位置层面的部分端点约束，建立最优性刻画与可计算的嵌套迭代算法，为部分信息下的集合控制提供了理论与数值基础。

Abstract: Motivated by scenarios in control systems where decision making is informed by only partial information about the states, we introduce a novel formulation of the Schrödinger bridge problem for inertial interacting-agent systems. Within our setting, the agent ensemble is steered to match partial specifications of the collective configuration. Our analysis pertains to a mean-field framework in which the collective dynamics are described by a nonlinear Vlasov--Fokker--Planck integro-PDE with nonlocal interactions. We assume that only distributions over positions are specified at the endpoints of a finite time horizon, representing aggregate population snapshots, while the corresponding velocity degrees of freedom remain unspecified. We derive the associated optimality system, identifying the optimal controlled evolution as jointly informed by the initial and terminal specifications through forward and backward propagation in time. We also show that the partial endpoint specifications bring about distinct conditions on the optimal control and the consistent evolution. Subsequently, to compute solutions to the optimality system, we develop a nested fixed-point algorithm inspired by the classical Fortet--Sinkhorn iteration, but extended to handle the nonlinearities arising from nonlocal interactions and temporal coupling.

</details>


### [150] [Control of Markov Jump Linear Systems with Uncertain Lumpable Cluster Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02573)
*Carlos A. F. Persiani,Ram Padmanabhan,Melkior Ornik,Marco H. Terra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对系统模态仅知所属簇（簇内模态与转移概率均不确定）的马尔可夫跳变线性系统，构建极小极大最优控制问题以实现鲁棒调节，将解化为Riccati递推，控制器仅依赖簇信息，并证明递推收敛与闭环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中马尔可夫跳变线性系统的当前活动模态往往无法被精确观测，只能判定其属于某个可观测的模态簇，这导致系统动力学参数与转移概率同时存在不确定性；传统依赖精确模态的控制器在此情形下失效，故需研究面向簇级观测的鲁棒控制方法。

Method: 在模态仅知所属簇的假设下，将控制问题建模为极小极大最优控制问题，以鲁棒调节为目标；借助正则化最小均方优化的结果，把该问题的求解约化为一个Riccati递推，使控制器仅依赖模态所属簇而非精确模态。

Result: 证明了所构造Riccati递推的收敛性，并建立了相应闭环系统的稳定性；通过数值算例验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提簇级观测下的极小极大鲁棒控制方法可行且有效，为模态不可精确观测的马尔可夫跳变系统提供了一种仅依赖簇信息的可计算控制方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the control of Markov jump linear systems when the active system mode is not exactly known. We rather assume that this true mode is only known to belong to an observed cluster of modes, making both the dynamics and the associated transition probabilities uncertain. We construct a min-max optimal control problem in this setting, with the objective of robust regulation. Using results in regularized least mean-square optimization, we describe how the solution of this problem can be reduced to a Riccati recursion, such that the controller depends only on knowledge of which cluster the mode lies in. We subsequently establish convergence of the recursion and stability of the resulting closed-loop system. A numerical example illustrates the effectiveness of the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [151] [Static Output Feedback Control for Multi-Agent Systems Using Nash Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02589)
*Paul Serna-Torre,Patricia Hidalgo-Gonzalez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种半定规划（SDP）模型，用于为网络化线性时不变多智能体系统（MAS）合成静态输出反馈控制，并证明可基于状态反馈的Nash均衡构造输出反馈控制。控制保证闭环稳定性与扰动下的耗散性。四个案例研究验证了其成本效益、时域性能与可扩展性，其中第四个案例为改进IEEE 24节点电网的频率调节，在261阶非线性电磁暂态模型上实现分布式逆变器控制。框架已在Python和MATLAB开源工具箱中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅在小规模数值模型中验证其控制框架，缺乏对网络化MAS大规模、实际应用场景的可扩展性与实用性的验证；同时多智能体系统中输出反馈控制（相较状态反馈更贴近实际部分状态可测情形）的合成仍具挑战。

Method: 构建半定规划（SDP）模型，用于合成网络化线性时不变MAS的静态输出反馈控制。理论上证明MAS的输出反馈控制可由先前针对状态反馈控制导出的Nash均衡构造，该均衡设定各智能体在系统动力学约束下最小化各自的个体代价函数。所得控制通过SDP求解，保证闭环稳定性及存在扰动时的耗散性。

Result: 前三个数值案例验证并与现有先进框架比较了所提控制的成本效益与时域性能；第四个案例针对改进IEEE 24节点电网设计频率调节方案，为视为智能体的逆变器合成分布式控制器，各逆变器仅使用相邻逆变器的部分状态信息。该控制方案在描述电网电磁暂态动力学的261阶高保真非线性模型中实现，相比以往小规模数值验证，展示了所提SDP的可扩展性与实际效用。

Conclusion: 所提SDP框架能有效合成网络化MAS的静态输出反馈控制，兼顾稳定性、耗散性与成本效益，并在大规模真实电网场景中验证了可扩展性与实用性。该框架已实现为Python和MATLAB开源工具箱，便于研究者与从业者直接应用。

Abstract: This work proposes a semidefinite programming (SDP) model that synthesizes static output control feedback for networked linear time-invariant multi-agent systems (MAS). We theoretically prove that the output feedback control of MAS can be constructed using the Nash equilibrium previously derived for state-feedback control. The Nash equilibrium considers that agents seek to minimize their individual cost functions subject to the system dynamics of the MAS. The resulting control from the pro- posed SDP model ensures the stability of the closed-loop system and dissipativity when a disturbance is present in the MAS.
