A set of four case studies is implemented. The first three numerical cases serve to validate and compare cost effectiveness and time-domain performance of the proposed control framework with other state-of-the-art frameworks. The fourth case designs a frequency regulation scheme for a modified IEEE 24-bus power grid. Using the proposed framework, distributed controllers are synthesized for the inverters which are considered agents in the grid. Each inverter uses partial state information of the neighboring inverters. The resulting control scheme is implemented in a high-fidelity non-linear model of 261 states that describes the electromagnetic transient dynamics of the power grid. Hence, compared to prior studies that usually validate their frameworks in small-scale numerical models, this fourth case demonstrates a virtue of our pro- posed SDP: its scalability and practical utility in real-world networked MAS. We implement the proposed framework in an open-access toolbox in Python and MATLAB, enabling practitioners and researchers to readily implement it in their applications.

</details>


### [152] [Data-Driven Static Output-Feedback Control for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02623)
*Paul Serna-Torre,Adam Sedlak,Patricia Hidalgo-Gonzalez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出数据驱动的输出反馈控制框架，用于线性时不变多智能体系统，通过离线数据求纳什均衡并求解静态输出反馈控制器，并在非线性电网电压频率调节中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动MAS控制方法多依赖强可控性假设、需要在线信息与状态估计，且验证多停留在小规模数值示例，难以反映真实系统特性，因此需要更弱假设、纯离线且面向高保真非线性系统的框架。

Method: 首先设计两种算法，利用状态与输入轨迹的离线数据样本确定MAS的纳什均衡（NE）；然后在给定NE解的基础上，构建半定规划以计算各智能体的静态输出反馈控制器；最后将该框架应用于含构网型与跟网型逆变器的非线性多节点电网，设计电压与频率调节控制方案。

Result: 在高保真非线性MAS模型中成功实现电网稳定与逆变器调节服务；相比比例积分控制等传统策略，超调量最多降低78%，调节时间缩短30%。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架在更弱可控性假设下无需在线信息与状态估计即可实现MAS输出反馈控制，并在真实非线性电网场景中验证有效，揭示了小规模数值示例难以捕捉的特性。

Abstract: This work proposes a data-driven framework that synthesizes output-feedback control for linear time-invariant multi-agent systems (MAS). We first develop two algorithms that use offline data samples of state and input trajectories to determine a Nash equilibrium (NE) for MAS. Next, we formulate a semidefinite program that, given the NE solution, computes the static output-feedback controllers for the agents in MAS. Compared to prior work, virtues of our framework include weaker controllability assumptions, the absence of online information and data-driven estimation of states. Next, we use the proposed framework to design control schemes for voltage and frequency regulation for nonlinear multi-nodal electrical networks with grid-forming and grid-following inverters. Thus, compared to prior work that implements their data-driven frameworks in small-scale numerical examples, we validate the performance of our data-driven framework in high-fidelity nonlinear models of MAS. This implementation in realistic MAS provides insights that small-scale numerical examples may fail to capture. Results show successful grid stabilization and provision of regulation services from the inverters in the grid using our proposed framework. It can attain up to 78% less overshoot and 30% faster settling time than traditional strategies such as proportional-integral control.

</details>


### [153] [A hybrid CNN-adjoint optimization framework for reconstruction of viscoelastic tissue properties in magnetic resonance elastography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02634)
*Anwesa Dey,Johann Rudi,Elena Cherkaev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究磁共振弹性成像（MRE）中复值剪切模量的反演重建，比较了伴随优化、CNN重建及二者结合的混合框架，证明混合方法可加速收敛并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从MRE测得的位移场反演复值剪切模量是一个严重不适定的反问题，尤其在噪声和有限边界激励下困难突出。纯PDE约束优化虽能精确细化系数重建，但性能强烈依赖初始值，因此需要一种能提供快速、信息丰富的初始重建的方法。

Method: 正向模型采用含复剪切模量的修正稳态Stokes系统的标量形式。作者建立了正问题的适定性、极小元的存在性及伴随公式的一阶最优性条件，并实现带Armijo线搜索的非线性共轭梯度法。同时构建二维CNN，将复值位移测量映射为空间变化的复剪切模量场作为初始重建，提出用CNN输出初始化伴随优化的混合方法。CNN在含单一扰动的系数场上训练，并在单一及未见过的组合构型上测试。

Result: 数值实验显示，CNN能泛化到更具挑战性的组合构型，随后的PDE约束优化进一步细化重建系数；混合方法相比单独使用伴随优化收敛更快、精度更高。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动初始化与基于物理的优化相结合，可为实现高效且准确的MRE重建提供有效途径。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance elastography (MRE) is a noninvasive imaging modality for quantifying the viscoelastic properties of soft tissues from shear wave propagation. Recovering the complex-valued shear modulus from measured displacement fields leads to a severely ill-posed inverse problem, particularly in the presence of noise and limited boundary excitations. We investigate adjoint-based optimization, convolutional neural network (CNN) reconstruction, and a hybrid framework combining both approaches. The forward model is based on a scalar form of the modified stationary Stokes system with a complex shear modulus. We establish well-posedness of the forward problem, existence of minimizers, and first-order optimality conditions for the adjoint-based formulation, and implement a nonlinear conjugate-gradient method with Armijo line search. While PDE-constrained optimization can accurately refine coefficient reconstructions, its performance depends strongly on initialization. We therefore construct a two-dimensional CNN that maps complex-valued displacement measurements to spatially varying complex shear modulus fields and provides rapid, informative initial reconstructions. The proposed hybrid method uses the CNN reconstruction to initialize the adjoint-based optimization, yielding faster convergence and improved accuracy. The CNN is trained on coefficient fields containing individual perturbations and tested on both individual and previously unseen combined configurations. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the CNN generalizes to these more challenging configurations, while subsequent PDE-constrained optimization further refines the reconstructed coefficient. These results demonstrate the potential of combining data-driven initialization with physics-based optimization for efficient and accurate MRE reconstruction.

</details>


### [154] [Advances in Continuous-Time Multiparametric Optimal Control: The Role of Time-Varying Active Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02680)
*Lida Lamakani,Efstratios N. Pistikopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出在计算前即可判定多参数最优控制中临界区域边界形状（超平面或曲面）的方法，通过相邻区域的活动约束序列给出五条规则，并在Newell-Lee蒸发器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数最优控制在连续时间下将初始状态空间划分为临界区域，区域边界或为闭式线性超平面，或为需计算的曲面。现有方法无法在计算前确定边界属于哪一类，导致不必要的计算开销。作者希望仅凭活动约束序列就能预先判定边界形状。

Method: 作者分析相邻两个区域各自的时间变化活动约束序列，推导边界为超平面的两个条件：(1) 新约束在边界上每一点同时变为活动；(2) 其他约束保持活动的时长在边界上每一点相同。否则边界为曲面。由这两个条件从活动约束序列中读出五条规则，每条规则判定边界为超平面或曲面。

Result: 五条规则被应用于Newell-Lee蒸发器，将七区域划分的八条边界成功分类：四条为闭式给出的超平面，四条为精确计算的曲面。

Conclusion: 仅通过相邻区域的活动约束序列即可在计算前预测临界区域边界的形状，避免了对超平面边界的多余计算，并通过Newell-Lee蒸发器实例验证了规则的有效性。

Abstract: Multiparametric optimal control in continuous time partitions the space of initial states into critical regions, each with its own control law involving a set of time-varying active constraints. The boundaries between two neighbouring regions are either hyperplanes, which are given in closed form by one linear equation, or curved boundaries, which must be computed. The sequence of time-varying active constraints plays a key role in defining the linear or nonlinear shape of a critical-region boundary. In this paper we show how to determine that shape prior to any computation. In particular, we show that the boundary is a hyperplane under two conditions. First, the new constraint becomes active at the same instant at every point of the boundary. Second, the durations for which the other constraints stay active are the same at every point of the boundary. Otherwise, the boundary is curved. Both conditions can be read from the sequences of active constraints of the two neighbouring regions, which gives five rules, each yielding a hyperplane or a curved boundary. We illustrate the rules on the Newell-Lee evaporator, where they classify the eight boundaries of a seven-region partition into four hyperplanes, given in closed form, and four curved boundaries, computed exactly.

</details>


### [155] [Finding Stationary Points under Higher-Order Smoothness: Trapping Algorithms and First-Order Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02791)
*Huanjian Zhou,Masashi Sugiyama,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在更高阶光滑性条件下，仅使用函数值或仅使用梯度寻找近似稳定点的查询复杂度，覆盖不同维度、预言机模型、随机化与约束情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在仅能访问函数值或梯度的情况下，寻找近似稳定点的查询复杂度下界与上界，特别是在高阶光滑性和不同维度设定下，以突破或刻画经典梯度方法的复杂度障碍。

Method: 针对常数维度与 $C^{p,\beta_p}$ 目标，提出确定性函数值梯度捕获算法，并使用高阶线积分实现仅梯度捕获；对二维 $C^{1,1}$ 目标采用随机化积分；对一般维度提出懒惰复用边界模型的“捕获或下降”框架；并证明随机化下界。

Result: 常数维度 $d$、$C^{p,\beta_p}$ 目标下，$\mathbb R^d$ 上查询复杂度为 $O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)})$，$[0,1]^d$ 上为 $O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)/\nu})$；二维 $C^{1,1}$ 目标随机化仅梯度查询复杂度为 $O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$，突破了确定性下界 $\Theta(\varepsilon^{-2})$。一般维度下，$p\ge2$ 时函数值复杂度为 $O(d^p+d^{p-1+\beta_p/(\nu-1)} \varepsilon^{-\nu/(\nu-1)})$，梯度复杂度为 $O(d^{p-1}+d^{p-2+\beta_p/(\nu-1)} \varepsilon^{-\nu/(\nu-1)})$，适用于 $\mathbb R^d$ 近似稳定点与 $[0,1]^d$ 近似 KKT 点，且在 $\beta_p=1$ 时精度指数与经典 AR$p$ 指数 $(p+1)/p$ 一致。高维下证明仅梯度随机化下界：对 $C^{1,1}\cap C^{p,1}$ 目标为 $\Omega(\varepsilon^{-(3/2+1/(2p))})$，与 $p=2$ 已知上界匹配、与 $p=3$ 上界在对数因子内匹配；无 $C^{1,1}$ 假设时对任意固定 $p\ge3$ 得到 $\Omega(\varepsilon^{-5/3})$ 下界。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了高阶光滑性下近似稳定点搜索的查询复杂度，给出了常数维与一般维度的确定性上界、随机化仅梯度上界及高维随机化下界，揭示了随机化相对确定性的优势以及维度对复杂度的影响。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of finding approximate stationary points under higher-order smoothness using only function values or only gradients. Our results cover different dimensions, oracle models, randomization, and constraints.
  For constant dimension $d$ and $C^{p,β_p}$ objectives, with $ν:=p+β_p$, we give deterministic function-value gradient trapping algorithms with query complexities $O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)})$ on $\mathbb R^d$ and $O(\varepsilon^{-(d-1)/ν})$ on $[0,1]^d$. High-order line integration enables gradient-only trapping. For two-dimensional $C^{1,1}$ objectives, randomized integration yields $O(\varepsilon^{-4/3})$ gradient queries on $\mathbb R^2$, breaking the deterministic gradient-only $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ barrier.
  For general dimension, we introduce a trapping-or-descent framework that lazily reuses boundary models. For $C^{p,β_p}$ objectives with $p\ge2$, it achieves deterministic query complexities $O(d^p+d^{p-1+β_p/(ν-1)} \varepsilon^{-ν/(ν-1)})$ using function values and $O(d^{p-1}+d^{p-2+β_p/(ν-1)} \varepsilon^{-ν/(ν-1)})$ using gradients. These bounds apply to approximate stationary points on $\mathbb R^d$ and approximate KKT points on $[0,1]^d$. Their dimension-independent accuracy exponent matches the classical AR$p$ exponent $(p+1)/p$ when $β_p=1$.
  For sufficiently high dimension, we prove randomized lower bounds for gradient-only methods. For $C^{1,1}\cap C^{p,1}$ objectives, our lower bound is $Ω(\varepsilon^{-(3/2+1/(2p))})$, matching known upper bounds for $p=2$ and, up to logarithmic factors, for $p=3$. Without the $C^{1,1}$ assumption, we obtain an $Ω(\varepsilon^{-5/3})$ lower bound for every fixed $p\ge3$.

</details>


### [156] [When Can an Infinite-Stream Evaluation Support Dynamic Programming? Compatibility, Recursive Compression, and Generalized Summation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02842)
*Yingzhe Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非标准无限期准则下的动态规划问题，核心是可评估性（一步控制替换后仍可求值）与持续性信息的递归传播。针对绝对可和加权准则，证明精确连续自治持续状态的最小欧氏维数等于时间Hankel秩，从而用有限Hankel秩刻画时间非平稳能否被有限内生状态增广替代；该下界对非线性连续编码器亦成立，且在单状态MDP中即已出现。对Candelpergher-Ramanujan求和，常见的持续测试更早失效；Abel求和则给出前缀封闭的对照基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非标准无限期准则（如加权准则、各类发散级数求和）下的动态规划，仅靠有限的流值不足以保证良定义：一步控制替换后必须仍可求值，持续信息需递归传播。现有理论缺乏对"何时时间非平稳可被有限内生状态替代"的精确刻画，也缺乏对不同求和准则在共同持续测试下行为的比较。

Method: 对期望阶段奖励流，分离出四个层面：定义域可容许性、有限维连续自治可实现性、标量充分性、上下文序一致性。对绝对可和加权准则，通过构造与下界论证，证明精确连续自治持续状态的最小欧氏维数等于时间Hankel秩（下界对非线性连续编码器成立）。再将共同持续测试应用于Candelpergher-Ramanujan求和与Abel求和进行对照分析。

Result: 对绝对可和加权准则：精确连续自治持续状态的最小欧氏维数=时间Hankel秩，有限Hankel秩恰好刻画时间非平稳可被有限内生状态增广替代的情形；下界对任意非线性连续编码器成立，且在单状态MDP中即已出现。对字面Candelpergher-Ramanujan求和，共同持续测试更早失效——仅改变当前动作就可能离开评估域；Abel求和则表现为前缀封闭，可作为对照基准。

Conclusion: 时间为非平稳的准则能否由有限内生状态增广精确表示，取决于时间Hankel秩是否有限；该结论不依赖编码器线性性。不同求和准则在共同持续测试下的稳健性存在本质差异：Candelpergher-Ramanujan求和连一步动作替换都难以保持可评估性，而Abel求和因前缀封闭性更稳健。

Abstract: Dynamic programming for nonstandard infinite-horizon criteria requires more than finite stream values: one-step control substitutions must remain evaluable, and continuation information must propagate recursively. For expected stage-reward streams, we separate domain admissibility, finite-dimensional continuous autonomous realizability, scalar sufficiency, and contextual order consistency. For absolutely summable weighted criteria, we prove that the minimum Euclidean dimension of an exact continuous autonomous continuation state equals the temporal Hankel rank. Thus finite Hankel rank exactly characterizes when temporal nonstationarity can be replaced by finite endogenous state augmentation; the lower bound applies even to nonlinear continuous encoders and already holds in a one-state MDP. For literal Candelpergher Ramanujan summation, the same common-continuation test fails earlier: changing only the current action can leave the evaluation domain. Abel summation provides a contrasting prefix-closed benchmark.

</details>


### [157] [Quantitative Mean-Field Limits for Recent Variants of Consensus-Based Optimization Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02901)
*Konstantin Haug*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本论文为δ-CBO、共识冻结（CF）与共识跳跃（CH）三种基于共识的优化（CBO）变体严格推导了混沌传播结果，证明当粒子数N→∞时，相互作用粒子系统的经验测度收敛到相应平均场方程的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBO是一类零阶、多粒子的全局优化方法，能够为高维、非凸及非光滑问题提供可证明的求解方案，其核心在于随机探索与确定性收缩之间的权衡。近年来出现了多种CBO变体，但缺乏对它们在粒子数趋于无穷时是否忠实逼近理想化平均场动力学的严格定量刻画，因此需要建立相应的混沌传播理论。

Method: 研究对象为三种CBO变体：δ-CBO（扩散函数为常数）、共识冻结CF（分片式CBO，将总时间域划分为共识点固定的区间）、共识跳跃CH（确定性迭代，每次跳到以上一分布共识点为中心的高斯分布）。采用耦合方法推导混沌传播估计，并分两种情形处理：一是在经验测度一致有界的高概率集上；二是不加限制的情形，借助矩界与对粒子间依赖性的细致处理，在不施加有利事件条件的情况下获得收敛性。

Result: 对三种CBO变体均建立了如下结论：随着粒子数N趋于无穷，相互作用粒子系统的经验测度收敛到对应平均场方程的解；并给出了两种情形（高概率有界集与无限制情形）下的定量混沌传播估计。

Conclusion: 该论文为全部三种CBO变体提供了定量的混沌传播估计，确立了粒子系统在大粒子极限下对理想化平均场动力学的忠实近似，从而为这些变体的理论可靠性提供了严格支撑。

Abstract: Consensus-based optimization (CBO) is a robust and flexible zero-order, multi-particle class of methods devised to provide provable solutions to high-dimensional global optimization problems, including truly nonconvex and/or nonsmooth cases. The algorithmic framework is grounded in a principled trade-off between stochastic exploration of the search space and deterministic contraction toward a consensus point. This thesis is concerned with three recently established variants of CBO. The $δ$-CBO method is similar to the classic CBO method, with the difference that the diffusion function is constant. The consensus freezing (CF) scheme can be understood as a piecewise CBO method. The overall time horizon is divided into intervals in which the consensus point is fixed. The consensus hopping (CH) scheme can be understood as a deterministic iterative scheme that jumps at each iteration to a Gaussian distribution centered at the consensus point of the previous distribution. The central contribution of this thesis is the rigorous derivation of propagation of chaos results for all three schemes. For each variant, we establish that the empirical measure of the interacting particle system converges, as the number of particles $N$ approaches infinity, to the solution of the corresponding mean-field equation. Two regimes are treated: first, on a high-probability set on which the empirical measures are uniformly bounded; second, in the unrestricted setting, where moment bounds and a careful treatment of the inter-particle dependence yield convergence without imposing conditioning on favorable events. The propagation of chaos estimates are derived by the coupling method. Together, these results provide quantitative propagation of chaos estimates for all three CBO variants and establish that the particle systems faithfully approximate the idealized mean-field dynamics in the large-particle limit.

</details>


### [158] [An Adaptive Symplectic Proximal Point Algorithm: Faster Convergence and Improved Numerical Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02963)
*Yi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应辛普�ecic加速近端点算法（ASPPA），证明其平方范数项收敛率为O(1/k²)，并在强单调包含问题下具有指数收敛率，数值实验验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端点算法（PPA）是求解单调包含问题的基础方法，许多凸优化算法均可视为其特例，因此加速PPA变体受到广泛关注。然而在某些特定情形下，PPA的收敛速度快于加速PPA，这促使作者研究一种自适应版本的辛普�ecic加速PPA。

Method: 提出自适应辛普�ecic近端点算法（ASPPA），即辛普�ecic加速PPA的自适应变体，并对其进行理论收敛性分析。

Result: 证明ASPPA关于平方范数项的收敛率为O(1/k²)，与部分加速PPA的收敛率相同；同时证明在求解强单调包含问题时ASPPA具有指数收敛率。数值实验表明ASPPA性能表现良好。

Conclusion: ASPPA兼具与加速PPA相当的收敛率，并在强单调情形下实现指数收敛，数值实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: The proximal point algorithm (PPA) stands as a fundamental approach for solving monotone inclusion problems. Notably, several key convex optimization algorithms have been proven to be specific instances of PPA. Given the importance of the PPA, there has been growing interest in developing its accelerated variants. However, for some specific cases, the PPA converges faster than the accelerated PPAs. In this paper, we mainly study an adaptive version of symplectic accelerated PPA, called adaptive symplectic proximal point algorithm (ASPPA). We first prove that the convergence rate of ASPPA with respect to the square norm term is $O(1/k^2)$, which is the same as the convergence rate of some accelerated PPAs. Also, we show that ASPPA exists exponential convergence rate when solving strongly monotone inclusion problem. Our numerical experiments indicate that the performance of ASPPA is very good.

</details>


### [159] [System Level Synthesis for Fast Chance-Constrained Post-Fault Ascent Replanning under Persistent Propulsion Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02989)
*Dai Shen,Chen Yang,Lin Cheng,Hongbo Zhang,Hongtao Zheng,Shengping Gong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种快速机会约束随机上升轨迹重规划器，显式处理持续推力不确定性，通过系统级综合、Cholesky变换与任务导向终端权重，实现闭环反馈设计与确定性重规划的交替求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非致命推进故障会使标称上升轨迹失效，但飞行器仍具备到达原轨道或降级目标的能力。确定性重规划虽可恢复标称可行性，但识别出的推进能力仍存在残余不确定性，可能导致路径约束违反与终端入轨散布。

Method: 不确定性模型将预测时域内共享的持续随机不确定性与逐区间独立扰动相结合。对固定标称轨迹，采用系统级综合参数化闭环响应；通过基于Cholesky的响应变换，将跨时间相关反馈设计问题转化为共享同一Riccati递推的逐列仿射递推。另在无相位修正春分点根数下，由最小能量入轨后轨道修正推导任务导向终端权重，将终端协方差整形与修正需求直接关联。随机重规划问题通过确定性轨迹重规划与反馈控制器设计的交替迭代求解，并以基于协方差的机会约束回退量耦合两次更新。

Result: 非线性蒙特卡洛仿真表明：等效修正需求降低、机会约束得到有效满足，并在多种不确定性水平与故障条件下实现快速在线重规划。

Conclusion: 该方法能在推进故障后显式处理持续不确定性，兼顾约束满足与终端性能，并具备快速在线重规划能力。

Abstract: A nonfatal propulsion fault can invalidate the nominal ascent trajectory while leaving sufficient vehicle capability to reach the original orbit or a degraded target. Deterministic replanning can restore nominal feasibility, but residual uncertainty in the identified propulsion capability may still lead to path-constraint violations and terminal injection dispersion. This paper develops a fast chance-constrained stochastic ascent trajectory replanner that explicitly accounts for persistent propulsion uncertainty. The uncertainty model combines persistent random uncertainties shared across the prediction horizon with interval-wise independent disturbances. For a fixed nominal trajectory, system level synthesis is used to parameterize the closed-loop responses. A Cholesky-based transformation of the system responses converts the resulting cross-time correlated feedback design problem into column-wise affine recursions that share a common Riccati recursion. A task-oriented terminal weight is further derived from the minimum-energy post-injection orbital correction in phase-free modified equinoctial elements, thereby relating terminal covariance shaping directly to correction demand. The stochastic replanning problem is solved by alternating deterministic trajectory replanning and feedback controller design, with covariance-based chance-constraint backoffs coupling the two updates. Nonlinear Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate reduced equivalent correction demand, effective chance-constraint satisfaction, and fast online replanning over a range of uncertainty levels and fault conditions.

</details>


### [160] [Forward-Backward McKean-Vlasov System with Jumps and a Monotone Source With à Control-Shifted Interface : Well-Posedness, Stability, and Yosida Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03041)
*Kayembe Tshiswaka Tcheick,Mabela Matendo Rostin,Kabengele Mpunga Yannick,Bosonga Bofeki Jean Pierre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有跳跃的单向耦合前向-后向McKean-Vlasov系统，其中前向方程独立于后向变量，BSDE依赖前向状态及其分布。源项由极大单调算子（凸势的次微分）沿前向状态累加，界面被控制量平移。建立模型层面适定性与强稳定性（估计对模型族一致）；研究Yosida逼近的强收敛、Minty型强-弱闭性、以及可积性条件下的—致收敛。在非对称损失厌恶原型中，界面附近层的占据估计给出显式Yosida收敛速率；当连续鞅部分非退化且界面绝对连续移动时，占据时间与层宽成线性关系，该速率控制后向分量与累积源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向-后向McKean-Vlasov系统在随机控制、平均场博弈与非线性期望中具有核心地位，而含跳跃及极大单调算子（如次微分、障碍/反射型源项）的单向耦合情形尚缺乏系统的适定性、稳定性与逼近理论。控制量平移不连续界面、且源项依赖状态分布，使经典的Minty理论、Yosida逼近与占据时间分析需要扩展。本文旨在为此类系统建立统一的模型层面适定性与稳定性框架，并给出Yosida逼近的收敛性及可量化速率。

Method: 考虑单向耦合的前向-后向McKean-Vlasov系统：前向方程独立于后向变量，BSDE依赖前向状态及其分布。源项由极大单调算子（凸势的次微分，在其最小范数选择的间断界面处被控制量平移，并含状态分布项）沿前向状态累积。先证明模型层面适定性与对模型族一致的强稳定性估计；再研究Yosida逼近，建立强收敛、状态/分布/控制（随动界面）变化下的Minty型强-弱闭性，以及在可积性条件下的均匀收敛。针对非对称损失厌恶原型，用界面附近层的占据估计导出显式Yosida收敛速率；在连续鞅部分非退化且界面绝对连续移动的假设下证明占据时间与层宽成线性关系，并用以控制后向分量与累积源。

Result: 获得模型层面适定性与强稳定性，且估计对模型族一致。Yosida逼近方面：证明强收敛、状态-分布-控制变动下的Minty型强-弱闭性，以及可积性条件下的均匀收敛。在非对称损失厌恶原型中，通过界面附近层的占据估计得到显式Yosida收敛速率；当连续鞅部分非退化且界面绝对连续移动时，占据时间关于层宽呈线性，同一速率控制后向分量与累积源。

Conclusion: 本文为含跳跃、单向耦合的前向-后向McKean-Vlasov系统（源项为沿前向状态累积的极大单调算子并含分布依赖）建立了适定性、强稳定性与Yosida逼近理论，给出Minty型闭性与均匀收敛，并在非对称损失厌恶原型中借助占据估计得到显式的Yosida收敛速率及其对后向分量与累积源的控制。

Abstract: We study a forward-backward McKean-Vlasov system with jumps and one-way coupling: the forward equation is independent of the backward variables, whereas the BSDE depends on the forward state and its law. A source generated by a maximal monotone operator is evaluated along the forward state and then accumulated in the backward equation. The operator is the subdifferential of a convex potential evaluated at the state translated by a function of the control, plus a term depending on the law of the state, so the control shifts the discontinuity interface of its minimal-norm selection. We establish modelwise well-posedness and strong stability, with estimates uniform over the model family. We then study the Yosida approximation: strong convergence, Minty-type strong-weak closedness when the state, its law, and the control (hence the moving interface) vary, and uniform convergence under an integrability condition. For an asymmetric loss-aversion prototype, a quantitative occupation estimate for a layer around the interface yields an explicit Yosida convergence rate. When the continuous martingale part is nondegenerate and the interface moves absolutely continuously, the occupation time is linear in the layer width. The same rate controls the backward components and the cumulative source.

</details>


### [161] [A Counterexample to the Exact Eight-Point Angular-Energy Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03207)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文给出Mouchet八点角能量猜想的一个精确反例，证明同一族方形反棱柱在有理间隔489/500处的角和严格更大，从而推翻该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mouchet于2005年提出、2006年发表的八点角能量猜想，同时给出了全局最大值的根式取值以及单位边长上下正方形隔h₀=√(1+2√2)/2的方形反棱柱构型。本文旨在检验该猜想的精确性。

Method: 将168个夹角余弦之和约化为单变量公式，对导数进行因式分解，并用有理不等式证明其符号；同时刻画该族构型中的唯一最大化间隔。所有反例所需的比较均为精确计算。

Result: 同一族方形反棱柱在有理间隔489/500处的角和严格大于猜想所给出的最大值和构型，给出精确反例。

Conclusion: 该结果否定了Mouchet所陈述的精确猜想。

Abstract: We give an exact counterexample to the eight-point angular-energy conjecture stated by Mouchet in 2005 and published in 2006. The conjecture specifies both a radical value for the global maximum and a square antiprism with unit-side horizontal squares separated by $h_0=\sqrt{1+2\sqrt{2}}/2$. We prove that the same family at the rational separation $489/500$ has a strictly larger angular sum. The proof reduces the sum of all $168$ angle cosines to a one-variable formula, factors its derivative, and certifies its sign using rational inequalities. We also characterize the unique maximizing separation within this family. All comparisons needed for the counterexample are exact. The result disproves the exact stated conjecture.

</details>


### [162] [Near-Optimal Convex Optimization with Lazy Second-Order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03222)
*Xinliang Zhang,Lesi Chen,Chengchang Liu,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究惰性二阶预言机下凸优化的复杂度，证明了迭代次数下界为 Ω(m + m^{1/7} ε^{-2/7})，并提出达到该下界（差对数因子）的新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在惰性二阶预言机（每次迭代查询梯度、每 m 次迭代查询一次 Hessian）的设定下，已有上界 O(m + m^{13/21} ε^{-2/7}) 与下界之间存在明显差距，需要刻画该设定下的最优复杂度。

Method: 提出一种新颖的块零链（block zero-chain）构造来证明下界；并设计一种新算法以获得匹配的上界。

Result: 证明了下界 Ω(m + m^{1/7} ε^{-2/7})，并提出新算法达到上界 O~(m + m^{1/7} ε^{-2/7})，改进了先前 O(m + m^{13/21} ε^{-2/7}) 的结果，且在对数因子内紧致。

Conclusion: 在惰性二阶预言机设定下，凸优化寻找 ε-解的迭代复杂度被刻画为 Θ~(m + m^{1/7} ε^{-2/7})，上下界在对数因子内匹配。

Abstract: This paper studies the complexity of convex optimization using lazy second-order oracles (Doikov, Chayti, and Jaggi, ICML 2023), where an algorithm queries gradients every iteration and Hessians once per $m$ iterations. Under this setting, we show a lower bound of $Ω(m+ m^{1/7} ε^{-2/7})$ on the number of total iterations to find an $ε$-solution using a novel block zero-chain construction. Then we propose a novel method that achieves a new upper bound of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(m+ m^{1/7} ε^{-2/7})$, which significantly improves the prior one (Chen, Liu, Luo, and Zhang, COLT 2026) of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(m+ m^{13/21} ε^{-2/7})$ and is tight up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [163] [A Newton--Kleinman Iteration for Infinite-Dimensional Linear-Quadratic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03242)
*Timo Reis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对具有无界控制/观测算子的无穷维系统,本文提出基于系统节点域上Lur'e恒等式线性化的Newton--Kleinman迭代,规避代数Riccati方程的不适用性,保持稳定性并单调降低代价算子,强收敛至最优值算子,并给出基于位移系统求解与输入空间求逆的低秩数值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷维系统的最优控制中,控制与观测算子可能无界,经典代数Riccati方程不再有意义,无法作为Newton--Kleinman迭代的基础。因此在无穷维、无界算子框架下需要新的迭代构造与收敛理论。

Method: 在系统节点域(system-node domain)上建立Lur'e恒等式并对其线性化,取代代数Riccati方程;采用基于预解式的规范化处理以覆盖无界控制;从任意镇定反馈出发构造迭代。

Result: 迭代良定义、保持稳定性,并产生单调递减的代价算子序列;在有限代价可检测性及有限维输入空间假设下,迭代强收敛到最优值算子,且与固定规范化参数无关;有限维情形下取零状态规范化即精确还原经典Newton--Kleinman迭代;数值上实现为基于位移系统求解与输入空间求逆的低秩格式。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于无界控制与观测的无穷维线性二次最优控制,并在线性化圆柱尾流(边界牵引控制、无界观测,每个自适应网格一次更新)及带边界牵引控制与边界位移观测的阻尼线弹性问题上得到验证。

Abstract: We develop a Newton--Kleinman iteration for linear-quadratic optimal control of infinite-dimensional systems with possibly unbounded control and observation operators. In this setting, the classical algebraic Riccati equation need not be meaningful and therefore cannot serve as the basis of the iteration. We instead linearize a Lur'e identity formulated directly on the system-node domain. Starting from any stabilizing feedback, the resulting iteration is well defined, preserves stability, and yields a monotonically decreasing sequence of cost operators. A resolvent-based normalization makes the construction applicable to unbounded control. Under finite-cost detectability and for a finite-dimensional input space, the iterates converge strongly to the optimal value operator, independently of the fixed normalization parameter. In finite dimensions, the natural zero-state normalization recovers exactly the classical Newton--Kleinman iteration.
  The formulation leads to a low-rank numerical realization based on shifted system solves and inversions on the input space. We illustrate the method on a linearized cylinder wake with boundary traction control and unbounded observation, using one Newton--Kleinman update per adaptively refined spatial mesh, and on a damped linear elasticity problem with boundary traction control and boundary displacement observation.

</details>


### [164] [Wasserstein Contraction of Stochastic Systems on Manifolds: A Differential Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03264)
*Dongjun Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将确定性收缩分析推广到连续时间随机系统，以Wasserstein距离度量收敛，通过均方微分构造随机变分动力学，提出正交微分耦合，并应用于紧流形上的随机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性收缩分析可将全局收敛问题化为局部问题，但随机系统缺乏对应理论。作者希望建立随机版本的收缩分析，用Wasserstein距离刻画概率律的收敛，并利用噪声耦合这一随机特有自由度，进而突破确定性反馈的全局镇定障碍。

Method: 采用均方微分构造随机变分动力学；沿初始条件曲线对该动力学的均匀L2稳定性进行积分，得到关联概率律的Wasserstein收缩；给出基于微分Lyapunov函数的判据，并在流形上导出内蕴Riemannian表述；引入正交微分耦合，将框架推广到同步耦合之外。

Result: 所得判据显式揭示状态空间曲率如何进入收缩机制，在正交标架情形下可恢复经典Ricci曲率结构；将该框架用于紧流形上的随机控制，通过引入随机控制输入克服确定性障碍——原本无法经光滑时不变确定性反馈全局镇定的系统，在W2意义下变为全局收缩，且其稳态分布可任意集中于指定目标平衡点。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续时间随机系统提供了与确定性收缩分析平行的理论框架，把噪声耦合提升为设计自由度，统一了微分Lyapunov判据、流形上的Riemannian/Ricci曲率刻画以及随机控制的全局镇定，表明随机性可用于实现确定性手段无法达到的全局收敛与分布集中。

Abstract: Contraction analysis of dynamical systems turns a global convergence problem into a local one. If the variational system is stable in a uniform sense, then all trajectories of the original system converge toward each other. This paper develops a stochastic analogue for continuous-time stochastic systems, where convergence is measured by Wasserstein distance between probability laws. The key step is to employ mean-square differentiation to construct stochastic variational dynamics. Its uniform $L_2$ stability can then be integrated along curves of initial conditions, yielding Wasserstein contraction of the associated probability laws. This yields criteria formulated in terms of differential Lyapunov functions, and on manifolds, it leads to intrinsic Riemannian formulations. A distinctive feature of the stochastic setting is that the coupling of the noises becomes a design freedom. To exploit this freedom, we introduce an orthogonal differential coupling that extends the framework beyond synchronous coupling. The resulting criterion explicitly exposes how the curvature of the state space enters the contraction mechanism and, in the orthonormal-frame case, recovers the classical Ricci-curvature structure. Finally, we apply the framework to stochastic control on a compact manifold. By introducing stochastic control input, we overcome a deterministic obstruction to global stabilization. That is, a system that cannot be globally stabilized through smooth time-invariant deterministic feedback is rendered globally contracting in $W_2$, and its steady state distribution can be made arbitrarily concentrated around a prescribed target equilibrium.

</details>


### [165] [Information Limits of Low-Rank Approximation Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03321)
*Kang Liu,Bohao Qu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了低秩近似认证所需的矩阵-向量乘积成本，涵盖相对矩阵误差与均方输出误差两类目标。核心结果是：当近似空间扩展时，一批验证响应可复用支撑整条嵌套路径而不随检查次数增加预算；跨W条路径时借助共享残差能量的集中界获得√log(W+1)依赖，并给出匹配下界证明其最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩近似通常需要额外的矩阵-向量乘积来验证其误差是否满足预设容差，这类认证成本此前缺乏精确刻画，尤其是当近似空间逐步扩展、需要反复验证时，如何避免验证成本随检查次数线性增长是实际关注的问题。

Method: 作者针对单一候选近似矩阵，在失效概率趋于零时确定维数一致的最坏情形极小极大查询常数；随后研究验证响应的复用，构造与验证独立的候选族，使一批验证支持整条嵌套路径；对W条路径利用共享残差能量的集中界分析依赖关系；并给出匹配下界以证明最优性；最后在同一离散谱族上比较两种一致有效证书，对各自规则族内优化验证预算。

Result: 单一候选矩阵情形得到了精确的维数一致极小极大查询常数。复用验证响应时，一批即可支持整条嵌套路径且查询预算不随检查次数增长；跨W条路径获得√log(W+1)依赖，并通过匹配下界在固定内部误差目标与足够小分离间隙下证明其最优。两种一致证书经预算优化后，验证与构造超出真实目标的成本量级分别为N^{1/3}与N^{2/3}。

Conclusion: 该工作精确刻画了低秩近似认证的查询成本，证明验证响应可高效复用于嵌套近似路径，并给出跨路径的√log(W+1)最优集中界；同时揭示了两种一致有效证书在验证与构造预算上的量级差异，代码已匿名公开。

Abstract: Low-rank approximation can require additional matrix--vector products to verify that its error meets a prescribed tolerance. We characterize this certification cost for both relative matrix error and mean-square output error. For a single approximation matrix candidate, we determine the exact dimension-uniform minimax query constant as the allowed failure probability vanishes. Our main result concerns reusing validation responses as the approximation space expands. For a candidate family constructed independently of validation, one batch supports an entire nested path without increasing the query budget with the number of checks. Across \(W\) paths, a concentration bound exploiting shared residual energy yields a \(\sqrt{\log(W+1)}\) dependence. A matching lower bound establishes its optimality for fixed interior error targets and sufficiently small separation gaps. Finally, we compare two uniformly valid certificates on the same dispersed-spectrum family. Optimizing the validation budget within each rule family yields costs of orders \(N^{1/3}\) and \(N^{2/3}\) for validation and construction beyond the true target. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Low-rank-approximation-1275/

</details>


### [166] [Optimal allocation in combat models with multiple battlefields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03336)
*Antonín Slavík,Vladimír Švígler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究固定规模兵力在多个独立战场对抗已知敌军分布的最优分配问题，通过损失与生存函数刻画战场结果，得到最优分配性质、NP难性与伪多项式算法，并给出多目标分配规则及经典战斗模型应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典战斗模型（如Lanchester、游击战、混合模型）通常依赖特定战斗方程，限制了对兵力分配最优结构的一般性分析。本文希望在不假设具体战斗方程系统的前提下，仅通过损失与生存函数刻画战场结果，从而建立更一般的兵力分配理论。

Method: 以损失函数和生存函数描述各独立战场的战斗结果；在凸敌损函数下分析最优分配的结构；证明在严格凸、严格增且满足端点归一化的损失函数族上问题的NP难性；对总兵力规模与饱和阈值为整数的情形给出精确伪多项式算法；并针对最大化成功交战、最大化己方幸存者、最大化敌我损失比三类目标推导分配规则。应用涵盖含经典Lanchester、游击战与混合模型的幂律函数族及隐式定义结果的模型。

Result: 对凸敌损函数，存在最优分配且至多一个战场部分饱和；对每类满足端点归一化的严格凸、严格增损失函数族，问题为NP难；总兵力与饱和阈值为整数时可用精确伪多项式算法求解；并得到面向成功交战、己方幸存与敌我损失比最大化的具体分配规则。

Conclusion: 该框架在无需特定战斗方程的前提下统一刻画多战场兵力分配问题，揭示了最优分配的稀疏饱和结构，明确了计算复杂性边界，并提供算法与多目标分配规则，可推广至经典与现代战斗模型。

Abstract: We study the allocation of a force of a fixed size across independent battlefields against a known distribution of opposing forces. Formulating battlefield outcomes through loss and survival functions allows us to establish allocation results without assuming a specific system of combat equations. For convex enemy loss functions, an optimal allocation exists with at most one partially saturated battlefield. We prove NP-hardness for every prescribed family of strictly convex, strictly increasing loss functions satisfying an endpoint normalization, and give an exact pseudopolynomial algorithm for the special case when the total force size and saturation thresholds are integers. We also derive allocation rules for maximizing successful engagements, maximizing own survivors, and maximizing the ratio of enemy losses to own losses. Applications include a power-law family containing the classical Lanchester, guerrilla, and mixed combat models, as well as models with implicitly defined outcomes.

</details>


### [167] [Compatible Bases and Actuator Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03353)
*Dawid E. Kędzierski,Piotr Krasoń*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究有序块矩阵的约束基选择问题，证明与尾部空间旗兼容的“代表基”可由内蕴块配额 $r_k=w_k-w_{k+1}$ 刻画，并揭示其与有限时域线性控制中执行器调度（每个执行器恰用一次）的对应关系；基于此提出带未来秩剪枝的深度优先搜索，将基线首次解搜索节点数 $3\cdot2^m+m-2$ 降至 $3m+1$，实验验证了公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序块矩阵中与嵌套尾部列空间旗相容的基选择具有结构性约束，而有限时域线性控制中执行器调度要求每个执行器恰使用一次，也自然地产生“代表系统”。作者希望刻画这两类问题共享的代数结构，并利用该结构设计更高效的调度搜索。

Method: 将块矩阵 $B=[B_1\mid\cdots\mid B_n]$ 的尾部矩阵秩记为 $w_k$，研究与其诱导的嵌套列空间旗相容的代表基；证明与整个旗相容等价于满足内蕴块配额 $r_k=w_k-w_{k+1}$。将可达性矩阵 $[A^{N-1}G\mid\cdots\mid G]$ 上的执行器调度视为代表系统，建立“保持完整可达性剖面”与“与尾空间旗相容”的等价性，并据此构造带未来秩剪枝准则的精确深度优先搜索。

Result: 在显式结构化族与固定搜索顺序下，基线搜索在找到首个解前访问 $3\cdot2^m+m-2$ 个节点，而未来秩剪枝将其减少到 $3m+1$ 个节点；精确算术实验在所有测试实例中复现了这两个公式。

Conclusion: 块配额刻画了代表基与尾部空间旗的完全相容性，并在有限时域线性控制的执行器调度中自然出现；利用该结构得到的未来秩剪枝可将搜索复杂度从指数级降至线性级，且理论公式经精确算术实验验证。

Abstract: We study a constrained basis-selection problem for an ordered block matrix $B=[ B_1\mid\cdots\mid B_n ].$ The successive tail matrices determine a nested family of column spaces, and we consider bases of representatives which are compatible with this induced flag. Our main linear-algebraic result shows that, among bases of representatives, compatibility with the entire flag is characterized by the intrinsic block quotas $r_k=w_k-w_{k+1}$, where $w_k$ is the rank of the $k$-th tail matrix.
  We then show that the same structure arises naturally in finite-horizon linear control. For the reachability matrix $[A^{N-1}G\mid \cdots\mid G]$, requiring every actuator to be used exactly once turns an actuator schedule into a representative system. Preservation of the complete reachability profile is then equivalent to compatibility with the tail-space flag.
  This correspondence leads to an exact depth-first search with a future-rank pruning criterion. For an explicit structured family and a fixed search ordering, the baseline search visits $3\cdot2^m+m-2$ nodes before the first solution, whereas future-rank pruning reduces this number to $3m+1$. Exact-arithmetic experiments reproduce these formulas for all tested instances.

</details>


### [168] [Convex geometry of diffusion laws and gradient flows for stochastic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03487)
*Yupeng Bai,Louis-Pierre Chaintron,Zhenjie Ren,Songbo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究路径空间上受控扩散律的弱随机控制梯度型动力学，借助一致凸运行代价诱导的Bregman几何，构造隐式近端格式与镜像流，并给出全局存在性、收敛速率、离散误差及有限粒子逼近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱随机控制的目标关于漂移一般非凸，直接优化困难；但一致凸运行代价可使路径律上的泛函严格凸，从而获得良好几何结构，为梯度型算法提供理论基础。

Method: 构造隐式近端格式并证明适定性与向连续时间镜像流的收敛；在镜像代价等于运行代价且律势线性时，在中心熵坐标下将流化为仿射，得到牛顿流的变分构造；对一般一致凸镜像代价与非线性凸律势，利用二次BSDE的BMO估计在全部学习时间上构造自然梯度流；分析相互作用有限粒子逼近；在Markov情形采用反射耦合。

Result: 证明近端格式适定且收敛到镜像流；仿射情形给出统一稳定性估计与指数收敛到最优解；一般情形建立定量收敛与一阶离散误差；有限粒子逼近以指数速率收敛到有限粒子最优，且与平均场最优的归一化差异为O(1/N)；Markov情形下反馈控制具有一致空间正则性与指数收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作通过Bregman/镜像几何与BSDE估计，为弱随机控制提供了变分、连续时间与离散化的统一分析框架，并覆盖有限粒子与Markov反馈控制情形。

Abstract: We study gradient-type dynamics for weak stochastic control through the laws of controlled diffusions on path space. Although the objective is generally nonconvex in the drift, a uniformly convex running cost induces a strictly convex functional of the corresponding path law and hence a natural Bregman geometry. We construct the associated implicit proximal scheme and prove its well-posedness and convergence to a continuous-time mirror flow. When the mirror cost coincides with the running cost and the law-dependent potential is linear, the flow becomes affine in centered entropy coordinates. This yields a variational construction of the resulting Newton flow, uniform stability estimates, and exponential convergence to the optimizer. For a general uniformly convex mirror cost and a nonlinear convex potential of the law, we construct the natural-gradient flow globally in learning time using BMO estimates for quadratic BSDEs, and establish quantitative convergence and a first-order discretization error. We further analyze interacting finite-particle approximations, proving exponential convergence to the finite-particle optimum and an O(1/N) normalized discrepancy from the mean-field optimum. In the Markovian setting, reflection coupling yields uniform spatial regularity and exponential convergence of the feedback controls on Rd, including for nonquadratic state-dependent Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [169] [Optimality of delayed reward attainment under uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03506)
*Chuwei Wang,Alexander Vladimirsky,Anastasia Bizyaeva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究不确定奖励下的具身决策问题，将其建模为含两个终端的确定性最优控制问题，揭示奖励学习与状态演化的交互，并给出规划视界缩短条件与主动信息获取策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者需在信息价值与决策延迟成本之间权衡，而动物觅食、机器人导航等具身决策中，空间移动同时影响信息质量与行动成本，此类交互机制尚需系统刻画。

Method: 将问题形式化为确定性最优控制问题：两个终端备选，一个奖励已知，另一个需通过带噪观测逐步学习；通过分析奖励学习与状态演化的耦合，推导延迟奖励获取的最优性条件、可预先缩短规划视界的充分条件，以及状态依赖观测质量下的主动传感策略。

Result: 证明延迟奖励获取在多种系统中常为最优，尤其当备选初始难以区分且等待成本较低时；对部分问题给出可先验缩短规划视界的充分条件，保证后续观测不影响值函数；当观测质量依赖状态时，控制器常应策略性地将状态演化至信息更丰富的位置再决策。

Conclusion: 延迟决策与主动信息获取是具身不确定性决策的普遍最优策略，与生物及工程系统中的行为一致；所提最优控制框架及视界缩短条件为分析与设计此类系统提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Decision making under uncertainty requires balancing the value of additional information against the cost of delaying the decision until that information becomes available. This challenge is especially prevalent for embodied decisions such as those faced by animals moving between food sources and robots selecting navigation targets, where movement through space shapes both the quality of information and the cost of acting. We consider a version of this problem, where the decision is on choosing among rewards of uncertain value, and those rewards can only be attained through changing the decision maker's state sufficiently. Our focus is on interactions between reward learning and state evolution, restated as a deterministic optimal control problem with two terminal alternatives, one with a known reward and the other learned gradually through noisy observations. We demonstrate that delayed reward attainment is often optimal in a variety of systems, especially so when the alternatives are initially difficult to distinguish and the cost of waiting is relatively low. For a subset of problems, we also derive sufficient conditions that allow shortening the planning horizon a priori, guaranteeing that further observations would not affect the value function. Finally, when observation quality depends on the state, we show that it is often optimal for a controller to evolve the state strategically toward more informative sensing locations before making a decision, in line with the behavior broadly observed in biological and engineered systems.

</details>


### [170] [Grid Theory and Polynomiality in Dynamic Lot-Sizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03559)
*El-Mehdi Mehiri,Nabil Absi,Elodie Suzanne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"网格理论"（Grid Theory），一种基于累积产量与生产上下界加性结构的框架，用以解释某些动态批量（lot-sizing）问题为何具有多项式可解性。核心结论：最优累积产量轨迹可限制在离散的"加性网格"上；当"加性维数"有界时，网格包络可多项式构造，并给出多项式时间动态规划算法，从而统一证明恒定产能、最小订货量、固定产能水平数、固定次数多项式产能、周期性产能和分段多项式产能等多类情形（包括产能取值数随规划期增长的情形）的多项式可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态批量问题一般为NP难，但若干受限情形却被证明存在多项式算法，已有文献多为逐个情形的独立证明，缺乏统一解释。作者希望回答"为什么某些动态批量问题具有多项式可解性"这一根本性问题，找出决定多项式可解性的结构性机理，而非仅关注不同资源取值个数的多少。

Method: 1) 针对带生产下界与上界的单产品动态批量模型，证明存在最优极值解：在每个再生区间内，除至多一个生产量外，其余生产量都取在边界值上；2) 由此引入"加性网格"（additive grids），并给出主网格定理（Main Grid Theorem），说明最优累积产量轨迹可限制在这些离散集合中；3) 引入"加性维数"（additive dimension）刻画边界和具有低维表示的产能边界剖面；4) 在加性维数有界时，构造多项式规模的网格包络，并设计基于网格的动态规划算法；5) 将框架推广至可分凹成本函数。

Result: 主网格定理成立，最优累积产量可限制于加性网格；加性维数有界可推出网格包络的多项式可构造性及多项式时间网格动态规划算法；框架统一给出多类问题的多项式可解性，包括恒定产能、最小订货量、固定产能水平数、固定次数多项式产能、周期性产能、分段多项式产能；特别是在不同产能取值个数随规划期增长时，多项式性仍可能成立。

Conclusion: 决定动态批量问题多项式可解性的充分机制是"加性结构"（additive structure），而非不同资源取值（如产能值）的个数。网格理论提供了统一的结构性视角与算法工具，可覆盖并推广已知的多项式可解族，同时揭示新的一类可多项式求解情形。

Abstract: Why are some dynamic lot-sizing problems polynomial? We address this question by introducing Grid Theory, a structural framework based on cumulative production and the additive structure of production bounds. For a general single-item dynamic lot-sizing model with lower and upper production bounds, there exists an optimal extreme solution in which, within each regeneration interval, all but at most one production quantity lie on a boundary value. This induces additive grids, and the Main Grid Theorem establishes that an optimal cumulative production trajectory can be restricted to these discrete sets. Although the resulting grids may be exponentially large, we introduce the notion of additive dimension to capture production-bound profiles whose boundary sums admit a low-dimensional representation. We show that bounded additive dimension yields a polynomially constructible grid envelope and a polynomial time grid-based dynamic programming algorithm. The framework extends to separable concave costs and establishes polynomial solvability of several families, including constant capacities, minimum order quantities, a fixed number of capacity levels, fixed-degree polynomial capacities, periodic capacities, and piecewise polynomial capacities. In particular, polynomiality may hold even when the number of distinct capacity values grows with the planning horizon. Grid Theory thus identifies additive structure, rather than the number of distinct resource values, as a sufficient mechanism for polynomial solvability.

</details>


### [171] [Differential inclusions and the chattering phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03561)
*Martin Ivanov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 这是一份关于含抖振（chattering）现象的微分包含解存在性问题的初步手稿草稿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注微分包含中出现的抖振现象，希望研究此类问题解的存在性，目前处于非常初步的写作阶段。

Method: 摘要中未给出具体方法。

Result: 摘要中未报告任何具体结果。

Conclusion: 该文本仅为说明性描述，尚不构成完整摘要，无法据此提取实质性结论。

Abstract: This is a very first draft of a manuscript about existence of solutions of differential inclusions involving chattering.

</details>


### [172] [Closing the Accuracy Gap in Stochastic First-Order Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03633)
*Haihan Zhang,Wendao Wu,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑非凸-强凸双层优化在随机一阶预言机下的最优精度依赖。证明全局无偏、有界方差梯度下存在 Ω(ε^{-6}) 下界，与已有上界的精度指数匹配，并在给定曲率控制与小区间条件下给出含 κ、χ、Δ、σ_g^2 的精细下界，同时用冻结线性倾斜估计器实现匹配的领先速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化在随机一阶预言机下的最优精度依赖尚未被严格刻画；已有上界达到 ε^{-6} 指数，但缺乏匹配的下界证明，尤其在全局光滑、非凸-强凸设定及算法无关意义下。

Method: 在全局 Lipschitz 梯度、下变量上梯度有界、下 Hessian 块 Lipschitz 等假设下，针对任意随机自适应算法构造硬实例：将序列硬目标嵌入精确下层响应，保持全局正则性与有限区间；通过多尺度分解限制每新方向携带的梯度信号，从而导出所需样本复杂度下界。并辅以冻结线性倾斜估计器，其偏差正比于下层 Hessian 变化，显式揭示结构依赖。

Result: 对 ρ≳μ、χ≲κ、足够高精度与足够大维度，建立 Ω(Lκ^2Δε^{-2}+L^3χ^2κ^8σ_g^2Δε^{-6}) 下界，适用于全欧氏空间、任何随机自适应算法，甚至每个样本为标量函数梯度时亦成立。在 ρ=Θ(L) 的常见尺度区，领先随机项条件数依赖为 κ^{10}，而可达依赖为 κ^{11}。估计器分析在指定曲率控制与小区间区给出匹配领先速率。

Conclusion: 双层随机优化的 ε^{-6} 精度指数在全局光滑非凸-强凸情形下是紧的；下界对算法与维度成立，且冻结线性倾斜估计器达到匹配主导速率，明确了条件数、Hessian 变化与初始区间/方差预算的结构关系。

Abstract: We characterize the optimal accuracy dependence for smooth nonconvex--strongly-convex bilevel optimization with stochastic first-order oracles. For globally unbiased fresh-gradient observations with bounded variance, we prove an $Ω(ε^{-6})$ lower bound matching the accuracy exponent of existing upper bounds. We consider globally Lipschitz gradients, a bounded upper gradient in the lower variable, and Lipschitz lower Hessian blocks, with target $\mathbb{E}|\nabla F(\widehat{x})|\leε$. Let $L$ be the common first-order scale, $μ$ the lower strong-convexity modulus, $ρ$ the lower Hessian-variation budget, $κ=L/μ$, and $χ=1+ρ/μ$. For $ρ\gtrsimμ$, $χ\lesssimκ$, sufficiently high accuracy, and sufficiently large dimension, we establish $Ω\left(Lκ^2Δε^{-2}+L^3χ^2κ^8σ_g^2Δε^{-6}\right)$, where $Δ$ is the initial gap budget and $σ_g^2$ is the lower-gradient variance budget. The bound holds over full Euclidean spaces against arbitrary randomized adaptive algorithms, even when every sample is the gradient of a scalar function. In the common-scale regime $ρ=Θ(L)$, the leading stochastic term has condition-number dependence $κ^{10}$, compared with the achievable $κ^{11}$ dependence. The proof embeds a sequential hard objective into an exact lower response while preserving global regularity and finite gap. A multiscale decomposition limits the gradient signal carrying each new direction, yielding the required sample complexity. We complement this lower bound with a frozen-linear-tilt estimator whose bias is proportional to lower Hessian variation. Its analysis makes this structural dependence explicit and yields matching leading rates in the specified curvature-controlled and small-gap regimes.

</details>


### [173] [The Minimax Cost of Smoothness in Fully First-Order Stochastic Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03638)
*Wendao Wu,Haihan Zhang,Chenheng Zhang,Yanyi Li,Chunyuan Zheng,Cong Fang,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非凸-强凸随机双层优化的样本复杂度，证明F²SA-p所达到的ε^{-4-2/p}指数是紧的（上下界匹配），并在高阶光滑预算下给出极小极大最优复杂度刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F²SA-p已实现$\widetilde O(pε^{-4-2/p})$的样本复杂度，但指数$4+2/p$的必要性此前未知，需要证明匹配下界以确立其最优性。

Method: 构造在固定非退化正则预算、固定样本预算下对任意随机算法均成立的极小极大下界；下界允许维度随精度增长。同时分析$L_j=MΛ^j(j!)^β$的极小极大固定预算复杂度，并设计加权独立批次的梯度差分法。

Result: 证明匹配上下界$Θ_p(ε^{-4-2/p})$，常数为$p$的函数。对$L_j=MΛ^j(j!)^β$给出$Q_{p,β}^*(ε)=Θ(ε^{-4}[\inf_{1≤r≤p} r^βε^{-1/r}]^2)$，$p∈N∪{∞}$。无限阶时$\beta=0$为$Θ(ε^{-4})$，$\beta=1$为$Θ(ε^{-4}\log^2(1/ε))$。

Conclusion: 定量光滑性带来精确的复杂度增益，而仅有定性解析性仍允许$Θ(ε^{-6})$复杂度；加权独立批次的梯度差分法在阶数上一致达到最优界。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of finding a point with expected hypergradient norm at most $ε>0$ in nonconvex--strongly-convex stochastic bilevel optimization using fresh, globally unbiased first-order samples with bounded variance. For lower-variable smoothness order $p\ge1$, F$^2$SA-$p$ achieves $\widetilde O(pε^{-4-2/p})$ (Chen et al., 2026), but necessity of the exponent $4+2/p$ was open. We prove matching upper and lower bounds $Θ_p(ε^{-4-2/p})$ under fixed nondegenerate regularity budgets, with constants allowed to depend on $p$. The lower bound holds for unrestricted randomized algorithms under a fixed sample budget, allows dimension to grow with accuracy, and preserves global unbiasedness and all prescribed lower-variable smoothness bounds, even with a scalar lower variable and exact upper gradients. We further characterize the minimax fixed-budget complexity under $L_j=MΛ^j(j!)^β$ for $j\ge3$: $Q_{p,β}^*(ε)=Θ\left(ε^{-4}\left[\inf_{1\le r\le p,,r\in\mathbb N} r^βε^{-1/r}\right]^2\right)$, for $p\in\mathbb N\cup{\infty}$, with constants independent of $p$. At infinite order, this gives $Θ(ε^{-4})$ for $β=0$ and $Θ(ε^{-4}\log^2(1/ε))$ for $β=1$. A gradient-difference method with weighted independent batches attains these bounds uniformly in order. Thus quantitative smoothness yields precise gains, whereas qualitative analyticity alone still permits $Θ(ε^{-6})$ complexity.

</details>


### [174] [From Mixing to Tearing: Graph Decomposition in Decentralized Optimization via Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03709)
*Kuangyu Ding,Gesualdo Scutari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出图撕裂消息传递框架GATE及其轻量化变体GATE-S，通过联合设计优化子问题与协作通信，实现图上强凸光滑函数和的最小化，并给出显式线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化方法（gossip或生成树路由）以通信为中心，仅将网络用于混合/聚合信息以支撑预设的本地优化更新，缺乏一个利用图结构来联合设计优化子问题与协作计算通信的通用框架。

Method: 从第一性原理出发构建框架，联合设计一致性约束的线性表示、对偶变量分块及协作求解各块子问题的连通智能体簇。GATE以每条边一个变量、树块为实例：每轮迭代智能体最小化两个端点cost-to-go消息之和并松弛结果，消息按树递归由局部最小化更新；进一步提出GATE-S，采用可处理的局部模型与轻量消息参数化以降低每轮计算与通信开销。

Result: 建立了线性收敛，收敛率显式地体现函数正则性、网络拓扑与所选划分之间的相互作用，揭示了图分解的影响；数值实验验证了理论结果并评估了算法效率。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了以图结构联合设计优化子问题与协作通信的通用框架，GATE/GATE-S在理论上具备显式线性收敛保证，并在实验中验证了有效性。

Abstract: We study the minimization of sums of smooth strongly convex functions over undirected graphs, with each function held by one agent and communication restricted to neighbors in the graph. Existing decentralized methods, whether based on gossip or on routing over spanning trees, typically
  use the network to mix or aggregate information to enable
  {\it prescribed} local optimization
  updates. What this communication-centered viewpoint lacks is a general
  framework that uses graph structure to {\it jointly} design the
  optimization subproblems and the cooperative computation and communication through which agents solve them
  cooperatively.
  We develop such a framework from first principles,
  jointly designing the linear representation of agreement constraints, the blocks of
  the resulting dual variables (jointly optimized), and connected cluster of agents that
  cooperatively solve each block subproblem over the assigned subgraph. GATE (Graph-Tearing message passing) is a first instance of this framework: one variable per edge and tree blocks. At each iteration, agents update their assigned edge variables by
  minimizing the sum of the two endpoint cost-to-go messages and
  relaxing the result. The messages are updated through local minimizations following the tree recursion. To reduce per-iteration computational and communication costs, we develop GATE-S, a surrogate variant using tractable local models and lightweight message parametrizations. We establish linear convergence with a rate explicit in the interplay among function regularity, network topology, and the chosen partition, revealing the effects of graph decomposition. Numerical experiments are conducted to validate the theoretical results and evaluate the efficiency of our algorithms.

</details>
